      notify: true            turn on reminders
      notifymes: "…"          the text (shared by all three; substitutions work)
      notifybtn: [ … ]        buttons for the reminder (nextMessage — a string with the block id)
      notifymin:  "1440"      a day before
      notifymin2: "180"       three hours before
      notifymin3: "60"        an hour before
    A negative value moves the reminder AFTER the appointment: "-180" — three
    hours after it. The calendar counts the time itself and does not send a reminder
    about a cancelled booking — this is what makes it better than a home-made scheduler.
    Put into the reminder text what it is read for: a preparation memo,
    the address, what to bring.

qb.cfg.tgchannel("subscription", { channel: "@mychannel",
                                   resultvar: "subscribed", userid: "[ClientID]" })
    Telegram groups and channels:
      "message"               Sending a message to a channel/group
      "starpayment"           Payment with stars
      "subscription"          Checking the subscription to a channel/group
      "ban"                   Ban in a channel/group
      "unban"                 Unban in a channel/group
      "restrict"              Setting restrictions for a user
      "promote"               Setting rights for a user
      "customtitle"           Setting an administrator title in a supergroup
      "exportinvitelink"      Generating an invitation link
      "createinvitelink"      Generating an invitation link with parameters
      "approvejoin"           Approve joining
      "declinejoin"           Decline joining
      "getadministrators"     Get the list of administrators
      "getmembercount"        Get the number of members
      "mesdelete"             Deleting a message from a channel/group
      "mesedit"               Editing a published message
      "pin"                   Pinning a message
      "unpin"                 Unpinning a message
      "forward"               Forwarding a message to a channel/group
      "copy"                  Copying a message to a channel/group
      "poll"                  A poll in a channel/group
      "chatinfo"              Channel/group data
      "revokeinvitelink"      Revoking an invitation link
      "subscriptionlink"      A link to a subscription for stars: subprice — the price
                              per month, the link goes into invitelinkvar
      "reaction"              The bot's reaction to a message: reactionemoji,
                              empty — remove the reaction
      "topiccreate"           Creating a forum topic: topicname, the topic number
                              goes into _topic_id and into topicvar
      "topicclose"            Closing a forum topic by threadid
      "topicreopen"           Reopening a forum topic by threadid
      "topicdelete"           Deleting a forum topic by threadid
      "giftlist"              The list of gifts: _gifts_count, _gift_id,
                              _gift_price, the whole list — in giftlistvar
      "gift"                  A gift to a subscriber: giftid, gifttext,
                              userid empty — to the current subscriber

    A REACTION INSTEAD OF A MESSAGE. When you need to show that a request was accepted,
    put a reaction rather than writing "accepted" — the discussion is not cluttered. Telegram
    allows the bot only the emoji from the set enabled in the chat.

    FORUM TOPICS KEEP THE CONVERSATION WITH ONE CLIENT TOGETHER. Create a topic for
    a request and substitute its number into threadid for sending, forwarding and a poll:
    then the conversation does not dissolve in the general stream. The group must be
    a forum, the bot needs the right to manage topics.

    A GIFT STAYS IN THE RECIPIENT'S PROFILE — this is more noticeable than a "thank you". The bot
    pays from its star balance. DO NOT WRITE THE GIFT ID BY HAND: the set
    and the prices change, take the id with the "giftlist" operation in the same scenario.

    COMMENTS UNDER A CHANNEL POST. A Telegram channel has no comments —
    they live in the LINKED DISCUSSION GROUP. Telegram itself forwards each
    post there, and the thread of replies under this forward is the comments.
    Both events come to the bot as messages from the group, so they are parsed
    by the same start block. They cannot and need not be requested: they come
    by themselves, but re-reading what was missed while the bot was off is impossible.
    In MAX it is the other way round: comments are only REQUESTED with the "comments" operation.
    A MAX channel post without an author does NOT COME to the bot — publish the posts under which
    the bot reads comments through the bot (the number — _group_message_id,
    save it in a list) and poll them on a schedule. "comments" also returns
    the bot's OWN comments: start them with a marker (for example "💬") and skip them
    when parsing, otherwise the bot takes its own answer for a new question. Remember
    the last parsed comment, so as not to answer twice.

    How to link a comment to a record:
      1. published a post — save _group_message_id in a field of the record;
      2. an automatic forward came (_tggroup_is_automatic_forward = 1) — find
         the record by this id from _tggroup_forward_from_message_id and save
         _tggroup_message_id as the THREAD NUMBER, and _tggroup_id as the group;
      3. a comment came — find the record by _tggroup_message_thread_id;
      4. a reply into the same thread — the message operation: channel — the id of the discussion
         group, replyid — the number of the comment (_tggroup_message_id)
         you are replying to: the reply goes under it, the author gets
         a notification. threadid is not suitable in a discussion group — it is
         for forum topics;
      5. an employee replied in the thread themselves — this is a group message with
         _tggroup_reply_to_message_from_id = the question's author. On behalf of
         a channel or an anonymous admin _tggroup_user_id comes as
         136817688 / 1087968824. The _tggroup_sender_chat_id of a comment is
         the channel of the post, not the author: "the channel replied" cannot be recognised by it.

    THREE RULES WITHOUT WHICH THIS DOES NOT WORK:
      • SILENCE BY DEFAULT. The bot answers nothing to the automatic forward of a post and to ordinary chatter
        in the discussion, otherwise it sends the menu to every
        remark. The author of an automatic forward is Telegram itself (id 777000), and the bot
        registers it as a subscriber: writing to it is useless;
      • COMMANDS ONLY FROM YOUR OWN PEOPLE. Check _tggroup_user_id against the list of
        admitted employees. An empty list means "we trust nobody",
        not "we trust everybody";
      • THE BOT DOES NOT READ ITSELF. Its own remark comes back as an ordinary
        message from the group. Write into the thread WITHOUT the prefix by which
        you recognise commands, otherwise the bot executes its own answer a second time.

    CHANNEL EVENTS ARE CAUGHT THE SAME WAY AS MESSAGES FROM A GROUP. The bot receives
    an answer to a poll, a join request, a member joining and leaving, a reaction,
    a new channel post and a boost. Each event has its own flag variable
    (_pollanswer, _joinrequest, _chatmember, _reaction, _channelpost, _boost,
    _reactioncount, and for a VK community — _vkcomment),
    and they are caught the same way as group events: a chain with a start block,
    in its conditionlist — a branch on the needed variable. An event has no text,
    so it cannot be told by text — only by the variable.
    For a person who is already in the bot, the event comes TO THE block they
    stand on, and only if this block waits for the event: the event variable
    (#{_joinrequest}, #{_pollanswer}…) is mentioned in the conditions of its
    transitions or in its scenario. Otherwise the event is silently lost.
    So an event bot is ONE block leading to itself, with the event parsing
    in its scenario: then the person always stands where they are awaited.
    If there are many screens — mention the event variables in the common settings step
    of EACH scenario (setVars "ev": "~#{_joinrequest}", "ev2": "~#{_chatmember}")
    and lead all blocks to the parsing block: then any screen accepts the event.

    WHAT IS USUALLY DONE ON THEM:
      • where the subscriber came from — for each ad its own "createinvitelink" link
        with creates_join_request = true and the source name; the request brings
        the link in _joinrequest_invite_link — find the source by it, approve with
        "approvejoin" and record it. After approval a member join also comes
        (_chatmember_joined) — do not count it a second time; leaving — _chatmember_left;
      • a request to a closed channel — ask a question in a private chat and approve with the operation
        "approvejoin" if the answer is correct. This is a captcha against bots and a way
        to collect a form before entry;
      • a poll answer — record the choice in a list and build a segment;
      • a member joining — a greeting in a private chat and a mark "came from the group";
      • a reaction — "put 🔥 under the post" as a trigger for a catch-up mailing;
      • a new post — a reminder in a chat or duplicating to another channel;
      • reactions under a CHANNEL post — only the counter _reactioncount: in a channel
        they are anonymous (who put them cannot be known). _reactioncount_message_id —
        the post number, _reactioncount_total — the total, _reactioncount_emoji —
        "👍:7,🔥:2". The subscriber is the channel itself; write the counter to the post by the number
        the publication returned (_group_message_id);
      • a boost — a bonus for supporting the channel.

    ENTERING A GROUP BY A FORM (the ready bot "Group entry by a form").
      • On a request (_joinrequest = 1) the bot writes to the person AT ONCE with the scenario's
        exit: Telegram allows the first message in a private chat within
        5 minutes after the request. Then the person answers themselves — the conversation is open.
      • The form step by step — with the waiting marker "form~number~step"; an empty answer
        is asked again, not accepted.
      • The decision: approvejoin / declinejoin with channel = _joinrequest_chat_id
        (save it for the time of the form) and userid = the id of the applicant. The moderator may
        be from another messenger: if userid is not their own id,
        the operation is performed by the chain's Telegram bot. The same for
        "add" to a MAX group by a moderator from Telegram.
      • Removing from a group someone whose paid membership ended: Telegram —
        "ban" and right away "unban" (otherwise they cannot enter by the link again), MAX —
        "delete". Like approvejoin and add, they are performed by the bot
        of the needed messenger if userid is not its own: a duty robot from MAX removes
        a member of a Telegram group, and vice versa. Deletion in MAX closes entry by the link — a paid
        MAX group needs a group without a link, the bot adds the members.
      • MAX has no join requests: the person comes by a link to the bot
        (?start=join-CODE), after approval — the "add" operation of the
        "MAX groups and channels" action with userid = their [ClientID].
      • A form in advance (without a request): send an approved person a link
        "with approval" — their request is then approved by itself, by the row
        "approved" in the list, without a second form.
      • A link "with approval" is made by "createinvitelink" with
        creates_join_request: true. An empty term and limit — unlimited in time and
        number.
    A channel post has no author, so the channel itself acts as the subscriber:
    its id becomes the ClientID. Answering such a "subscriber" is useless —
    the scenario must send the message to the group or to a specific person.

    THE "COPY TEXT" BUTTON (action "copy", the copytext field) puts text
    into the subscriber's clipboard. Put it wherever a person would otherwise select
    text with a finger: a promo code, an order number, a card number for a transfer, an address.
    Substitutions work in copytext, so the value of a
    variable can be copied. It works in Telegram (with inline buttons on) and in MAX;
    in other messengers the button is not shown, so in a multichannel
    bot duplicate the value in the message text.

    TELEGRAM BUSINESS ACCOUNT: THE BOT ANSWERS ON BEHALF OF THE OWNER. Clients write
    to a master, a salon or a shop in a private chat, not to the bot — and this conversation
    has been outside automation so far. The owner connects the bot in the Telegram
    settings (Settings → Telegram Business → Chatbots), and client
    messages come to the bot as ordinary ones: the same chains, the same blocks, the same
    keywords. The answer goes on behalf of the owner, not of the bot.

    SUCH A CLIENT CAN BE TOLD BY #{_business_chat} = 1. Write to them as
    a person, not as a bot: without "Hello! I am a bot assistant", without keyboards
    over the whole screen. The usual scheme — the bot answers frequent questions (prices,
    address, free time), and on everything else stays silent and calls the owner.

    CONNECTING AND DISCONNECTING are caught by #{_business} = 1: the event has
    the connection number (_business_id), the owner (_business_user_id), whether it is
    on (_business_enabled) and whether the bot may answer (_business_canreply).
    This is a convenient moment to send the owner an instruction.

    WHAT NOT TO DO: do not run mailings and autofunnels in a business conversation —
    it is the owner's personal conversation, not a subscriber base. Messages the owner
    wrote themselves do not come to the bot: it answers only clients.

    AFTER PUBLICATION A POST CAN BE EDITED RATHER THAN REPUBLISHED. Sending
    puts the post number into #{_group_message_id}; save it into your own variable
    if you are going to return to the post later — the next sending
    overwrites it. Then "mesedit" (the new text in the
    message field; for a post with a photo the caption is edited), "pin" and "unpin", "mesdelete" work by the number.
    Deleting and publishing again for the sake of an edit is not needed: views,
    reactions and the link to the post would be lost.

    RECORD THE POST NUMBER ONLY IF THE POST WENT OUT. Give the sending a resultvar
    and check it with a branch ('1' — went out). It did not — #{_group_message_id} keeps
    the number of the PREVIOUS post, and a product or an order would later edit someone else's post.
    Take the reason (#{_error}) into your own variable right after sending:
    the next channel operation overwrites it.
    TELEGRAM'S REFUSAL WHEN SENDING TO A CHANNEL DOES NOT REACH THE SCENARIO: the bot is not
    an administrator, there is no right to publish photos — #{_error} stays empty,
    and so does the post number. Before sending reset both variables, after it —
    an empty number means "the post did not go out"; write to the person "check that the bot
    is an administrator of the channel", not "Telegram did not answer".
    The number also comes for a post with a photo or a file, and a Telegram channel can be
    given by @name.

    A CLIENT FROM ANOTHER MESSENGER. Operations with the channel or group itself
    (a message, an edit, deletion, pinning, data, administrators) for a subscriber
    from another messenger are performed by the bot of the needed messenger of the same chain.
    A buyer from Telegram
    placed an order — the post in a MAX channel is edited. There is no such bot in the chain —
    resultvar = '0' and an explanation in #{_error}. Editing a MAX post changes only
    the text: the photos and buttons stay the same. Each messenger has its own File ID:
    a photo sent from Telegram cannot be sent to a MAX channel — there the post is text.

    SALES FROM A POST. The post button is a link to the bot's private chat with a start
    parameter: https://t.me/<username>?start=buy-CODE, in MAX
    https://max.ru/<username>?start=buy-CODE. The start block reads #{start}
    and leads straight to the product card. Build the post text and button with ONE function
    from the product row and edit the post after each order, reservation cancellation
    and restocking: "In stock: 1" → "Sold", the button → "Pre-order".
    A sample: the ready bot "Sales from channel posts".

    FORWARDING AND COPYING. "forward" keeps the caption "Forwarded from…",
    "copy" sends it as its own message. Both have: fromchannel — from where,
    channel — to where, mesid — what. The number of the new post also goes
    into #{_group_message_id}.

    A POLL. "poll": the question in message, the options in polloptions one
    per line (from 2 to 12). pollanon — anonymous (yes by default),
    pollmulti — several can be chosen.

    FORUM TOPICS. "forward", "copy" and "poll" have threadid — the topic number
    in a supergroup. Empty — it goes to the general stream.

    CHANNEL DATA. "chatinfo" puts _chat_title,
    _chat_description, _chat_link, _chat_membercount and _chat_pinned (the number
    of the pinned post) into system variables, and the whole messenger answer into chatinfovar.

    LINKS. "createinvitelink" gives a link, "revokeinvitelink" closes it
    (the revokelink field). Those who already entered stay.

    SENDING TO A GROUP CAN DO THE SAME AS SENDING TO THE BOT. The "message" operation
    has attachments besides the text and buttons:
      messagemedia    an array of the bot's file names — photos, documents, audio.
                      They go out in one publication. A whole link also fits
      messagefileid   the File ID of a file of this bot (any type) (the variable #{_fileId}
                      after uploading a clip to the bot)
    The text is not mandatory then: a publication may consist of one media item.
    A paid post ("starpayment") has its own attachments — the media field of pairs
    { fileid, mediatype }; messagemedia is not read there.

    THE SUBSCRIPTION CHECK WORKS ONLY IN TELEGRAM. In the other messengers
    the action is silently skipped and DOES NOT TOUCH the result variable — so
    reset it before the call and check it after, otherwise the value
    from the previous subscriber stays. Take the channel from the bot settings, do not hard-code it
    in the action, and do not call the action at all if the channel in the settings is empty.
    Anyone who belongs to the channel counts as subscribed: a member, the creator,
    an administrator and a restricted member (they are in the channel, they are just
    forbidden to write). Usually the current subscriber is checked; if the
    userid field is filled, whoever is given in it is checked — this is how a manager
    or the author of a request is checked.

    THE RESULT GOES INTO resultvar FOR ANY OPERATION: "1" — it worked,
    "0" — it did not, the messenger's refusal reason — in _error. For the subscription check
    resultvar means something else: "1" — subscribed, "0" — not subscribed.

    THE SUBSCRIPTION CHECK COUNTS A CHANNEL ADMINISTRATOR — both in Telegram
    and in MAX. No workarounds need to be written. But the engine does not distinguish
    a SERVICE REFUSAL from "not subscribed": in both cases "0" goes into resultvar. Tell
    them apart yourself by _error — it is non-empty only on a failure. Reset _error before
    the check and right after it put it into your own variable: the next
    request overwrites it. Three outcomes instead of two — "subscribed", "not subscribed",
    "the service did not answer" — are mandatory wherever a refusal closes the entry for
    a person: otherwise a messenger failure looks like a refusal, and the person leaves.

qb.cfg.maxchannel("subscription", { channel: "…", resultvar: "subscribed" })
    MAX groups and channels:
      "message"               Sending a message to a channel/group
      "subscription"          Checking the subscription to a channel/group
      "delete"                Removing from a channel/group
      "add"                   Adding to a channel/group
      "mesdelete"             Deleting a message from a channel/group
      "mesedit"               Editing a published message
      "pin"                   Pinning a message
      "unpin"                 Unpinning a message
      "chatinfo"              Channel/group data
      "getadministrators"     Get the list of administrators
      "comments"              Comments to a channel post
      "comment"               Sending a comment to a post
      "commentdelete"         Deleting a comment

    The attachments of "message" are the same as in Telegram: messagemedia (an array of the bot's file
    names or links, for example from media_urls) and messagefileid (the File ID
    of a file). The text is not mandatory. The text and the files from messagemedia go out as ONE
    message: the text is the caption, several photos — an album (up to 10 per message),
    videos and documents are uploaded to MAX by the engine itself. A separate action for
    each photo is not needed. A repost from a Telegram channel: files larger than 20 MB
    Telegram does not give to bots — such a video cannot be downloaded; say so in the text
    of the MAX post with a link to the post (#{_channelpost_url}).

    COMMENTS UNDER A CHANNEL POST are incoming requests: a person asked about
    the price where they saw the announcement. They are addressed by the POST NUMBER (mesid), not by the
    chat ID: this is the very _group_message_id the sending returned — keep
    it in a list next to the announcement. "comments" puts the number of comments
    into _comments_count, and the last one into _comment_id, _comment_text,
    _comment_user_id, _comment_user_name; the whole list goes
    into commentsvar. "comment" replies under the post; with commentid — into the thread of this
    comment (a reply to it). "commentdelete" removes a comment by
    commentid. MAX comments have no mentions, links or attachments — only
    text. The bot needs channel administrator rights to read all messages
    and to delete.

    NEW MAX COMMENTS COME BY THEMSELVES — as the event #{_maxcomment} = 1.
    Comments are turned on in the MAX channel settings, the bot is an administrator
    of the channel; a linked chat, like a discussion group in Telegram, is NOT needed.
    Comments come under ANY post of the channel, not only under the bot's posts.
    Variables: _maxcomment_text, _maxcomment_id, _maxcomment_post_id,
    _maxcomment_chat_id, _maxcomment_user_id (empty — on behalf of the channel),
    _maxcomment_name, _maxcomment_username, _maxcomment_reply_id. The event
    is QUIET: it is parsed by the start block of the chain (the first block of the first step),
    and only if it or its scenario mentions #{_maxcomment}; in the same
    scenario this event is distinguished from group messages (#{_maxgroup}) and the private chat.
    The subscriber of the event is the comment author; send them nothing in a private chat, the answer is
    "comment" with mesid #{_maxcomment_post_id} and commentid #{_maxcomment_id}.
    Polling "comments" on a schedule is needed only if what was missed must be re-read.

    DO NOT CONFUSE IT WITH TELEGRAM: there comments live in a linked discussion group
    and come as a message from that group (#{_tggroup}).

    ANOTHER PERSON'S SUBSCRIPTION. "subscription" (both MAX and Telegram) has
    userid: empty — the subscriber themselves is checked, an ID given — that person.
    This is how an organiser re-checks whether the invited people stayed in the channel.
    If userid holds the ID of a person from another messenger (an organiser in
    Telegram checks a MAX subscriber), the check is performed by the bot of the needed
    messenger of the same chain.

    AN INVITATION CONTEST. Telegram: a participant has their own "createinvitelink" link
    with creates_join_request — the request brings it in _joinrequest_invite_link,
    approve it and count it; _chatmember_left — subtract it. MAX: there are no join requests —
    a link to the bot ?start=r-CODE; a friend already subscribed before the link is not new
    (check the subscription at once), subscribed after it — "I subscribed" and count it.
    One's own link and a repeated person are not counted.

    A GIFT FOR SUBSCRIBING (A LEAD MAGNET). The gift — only after "subscription".
    Not subscribed — create a row "waiting": subscribed later — this is a new
    subscriber for the gift, subscribed right away — "already was". Telegram:
    _chatmember_joined — check the subscription to YOUR OWN channel (the entry may have been
    into another chat) and give it out yourself; _chatmember_left — the promo code burned. MAX
    has no join events: the subscription is checked by the duty robot before each touch
    (the lead's userid) and by the manager when redeeming the promo code. Touches before the request — by
    the duty robot's round, only in the daytime; the lead's stage — by a tag to the recipient (tagto).

    ADS IN A CHAT BY A TEMPLATE. The engine parses group messages only
    with the FIRST block — wherever the person stands in the private chat. So the submission form
    in the private chat goes by a waiting marker through the parsing: if a person wrote in the group
    in the middle of the form, the marker stays, and the next answer in the private chat gets through.
    A free-form ad (a price, a phone, a sign word, a photo; not a reply
    and not a moderator) — "mesdelete" by _tggroup_message_id/_maxgroup_message_id,
    a hint into the group with a link button to the bot (keep the hint number
    and delete the previous one — one hangs), the text to the author — by the exit message: from
    a group it goes to their private chat. Mark your own posts and hints in the text
    ("· ad №12") and skip them: MAX sends them to the bot too. Telegram
    does not let a bot delete messages older than 48 hours — check removing an old
    ad by resultvar and say that it must be removed manually.

    COMMUNITY MEETINGS. The announcement in the group — from the bot, with a link button "Going"
    (?start=ev-CODE); the post number — into the meeting row. Edit the counter "Going: 12 of 20"
    with "mesedit" each time someone signs up or cancels:
    Telegram changes the text and the button, MAX — only the text. A place is taken by
    "going", "invited" and "came"; when they run out — a waiting list. A cancellation — to the first
    in the queue "invited" with a deadline, expired — the duty robot gives it to the next one.
    A reminder to everyone signed up — with ONE sending: clientid/messenger/botid
    as arrays. Check-in on the spot — by the meeting code that the
    organiser names, in the window "an hour before — 4 hours after"; those who did not check in after
    the results — "did not come".

    VOTING WITH A QUORUM (homeowners' associations, garden partnerships). Only members of the register vote:
    the "Register" list (object, share) is filled by the owner, a person binds
    an object with a personal link ?start=r-CODE — as a row with their own autofields
    (one object — one account). A vote weighs the sum of the shares of their objects;
    one vote row per person and question — edit it, do not add. In
    a Telegram group — "poll" with pollanon: false: the answer comes as the event
    _pollanswer (mention #{_pollanswer} in the scenario of every screen), the
    question number — by _pollanswer_poll_id saved from _poll_id at
    publication (clear _poll_id before the poll — otherwise it holds the previous one).
    Do not count an answer from outside the register. The quorum and the decision — by shares,
    the minutes — as text to the members and into the group.
    AN AI RESPONDER IN A CLIENT CHAT. It answers only by a knowledge base: the list
    "Knowledge base" (Question, Answer) is read whole, the question gets the
    records closest to the question by words (up to ~12,000 characters), and the rule
    "the answer is not in the base — return exactly NO_ANSWER, invent nothing".
    The base is empty or the limit of questions per hour from a person is reached — do not ask the AI model,
    go straight to the employees. First write the question to a list
    (number — the largest + 1, status "waiting"), the answer — tgchannel "message"
    with replyid: the number of the question message (#{_tggroup_message_id}) and the
    question number in the text; the number of the bot's post from #{_group_message_id} — into the question
    row. MAX has no reply — address the person by name. No answer,
    resultvar "0" or it did not go out — to employees by sendto with "Reply" (the waiting
    marker answer~number through the parsing) and "Do not reply"; the employee's answer —
    into the chat as a reply to the question, "To the base" — as a knowledge base row. "employee"
    in reply to the bot's answer: in Telegram _tggroup_reply_to_message stays
    0 (they replied to the bot), the text of the bot's answer — in _tggroup_reply_to_message_text,
    take the question number from it; in MAX — _maxgroup_reply_message_id, look up the question
    by the number of the bot's post. MAX sends the bot's own messages back to it:
    start them with a sign (🤖, 👤) and skip them in parsing.
    TASKS FROM A WORK CHAT. In a group "#task for Ivan by Fri order paper"
    (without "#" — only as a reply to a message: "the task was hard" is not
    a command). The performer is the first word: a member's name in any case
    (the name stem without the last vowel + up to two letters) or @username; not given —
    the author. Two with the same name — ask for the @username. Extract the deadline from the text
    (check "today/tomorrow [by 12:00]" BEFORE "by 12:00", otherwise "tomorrow"
    stays in the text); a date without a time — by the end of the working day, keep it
    in milliseconds. A member registers themselves by the link ?start=t-CODE from the memo
    in the group (without this the bot cannot write to them in a private chat); keep the performer
    in the columns "Perf ClientID/Messenger/BotID", the author — the row's autofields.
    The card — tgchannel "message" with replyid to the command; "done" as a reply to
    the card: the number from _tggroup_reply_to_message_text. Edits "done/
    cancelled/new deadline" — only for rows "in progress". The duty robot on
    the manager: overdue ones without the mark "Reminded" — to the performer in one
    message and the mark; a morning summary in the chat once a day (the day — as a number).
    A POINT'S SHIFT REPORT (chains of outlets). The shift — the local date minus 6 hours, as a number
    YYYYMMDD (a report at 01:30 is for yesterday); a point's report deadline "01:00" is the next
    calendar day. The report is a draft form in a list (a waiting marker
    through the parsing, a photo — #{_fileId}): cash, cashless, paid out, the till by
    recount, a photo, incidents. The till as a chain: expected = the point's till
    (the last fact) + cash − paid out, the fact becomes the point's till;
    do not accept a second report for the same shift. An alarm to the managers — only
    on a discrepancy from the threshold or an incident. An administrator connects
    by the point's link ?start=p-CODE. The duty robot on the manager: after the deadline —
    a reminder to the point's administrators, later — escalation, the marks
    "Reminded/Escalated" = the shift; in the morning a summary for yesterday. A point is responsible
    for a shift only if it was added before its deadline (the time of adding — ms).
    SHIFTS AND TIMESHEET. Arrival and departure — by location: the "Send
    location" screen is a separate block with ITS OWN text and btnsettings
    tg { location: true, inline: false } and max { location: true }, without
    a scenario; the waiting marker "geo~in.CODE" is set by the step BEFORE the transition to
    it, the point comes to the parsing (_islocation, client_latitude/longitude).
    Text instead of a point — set the marker again and back to the screen. Accept
    the check-in only if the distance to the point (haversine) is not greater than the
    radius. Lateness and early leaving — with a tolerance; hours — departure minus
    arrival. An employee's schedule — "mon-fri" or a cycle "2/2 from 01.09". A swap:
    a "Swaps" row → the colleague's consent → approval by the manager; on that
    day we wait for the colleague. The duty robot: did not check in N minutes after the start,
    a forgotten shift — "without departure" N hours after the end; the month's timesheet
    by employees. A new employee and a new point are not responsible for past
    days.
    EMPLOYEE ONBOARDING. The programme — a list: programme (position), day,
    order, topic, material, link, question, options "a; b; c", the number
    of the correct one; no programme for the position — the general one (with an empty programme). A step —
    the key "day.order". The mentor writes "Name; 01.10; Seller" — a row
    "invited" and a link ?start=n-CODE; the newcomer binds their own
    row by it (editing only from "invited", to a stranger — "already used").
    The onboarding day — from the start date, in working days or all days; days ahead
    are closed. Progress — a row per step: "studied" or "test" with the answer, once.
    Give the material link as a link button only in the branch where it
    exists. The mentor's question and answer — by waiting markers through the parsing.
    The duty robot: at the issue hour — to the newcomer "Day N" (once a day, the mark
    "Issued"), the debt of past days — to the mentor (once a day, "Lagging"). All
    steps passed — "passed" and the result with scores to both.
    ACKNOWLEDGING REGULATIONS. Documents — a list: code, name, link
    or text, to whom (a department code or empty — everyone), days, version, status
    (draft / sent / archive), the sending day and the deadline as a number YYYYMMDD.
    An employee comes by the department link ?start=e-CODE and writes their last and first name
    with a marker through the parsing. Questions — a separate list; answers accumulate in a personal
    variable "code~version~1,3", all correct — a log row (document,
    version, employee, department, time, attempts, on time) by the autofields,
    an error — the numbers of the wrong ones and again. Acknowledged — there is a log row of the
    CURRENT version. An employee's deadline is the document's deadline, but someone who came later gets
    their own days. Sending — after confirmation, by an edit with the condition "the same
    status and version", to all recipients in one sending as arrays. A new
    edition — version +1 and sending again. The duty robot: a reminder the day before
    the deadline and after it (once a day), a summary of those overdue to the responsible people.
    APPROVING PAYMENT REQUESTS. Roles — by codes (employee,
    head, CFO, accounting) in the "People" list. The form is a
    draft in personal variables (to whom, sum, for what, deadline), step by
    step with markers through the parsing. The invoice comes as a file to the parsing (_ismedia,
    _fileId — the first element if it is an array) — and only THERE can it be
    forwarded with "Receive and forward media": after "Launch block" the file
    is no longer in the incoming message. Stages — as a string "1,2": the head; from a threshold —
    also the CFO; no heads except the author — the CFO;
    then payment. Send the card to those in the same bot as the author by
    sendto with sendtofileid (a File ID only fits its own bot),
    to the others — without the file, and the invoice itself to them — by forwarding on submission.
    A decision — by an edit with the condition "the same number, status and stage" and the marker
    "Move" (time:ClientID), then re-read the row: someone else's "Move" — "already
    decided". Prepare notifications BEFORE the edit. "Paid" — at once; wait for the payment order
    with a marker and forward it to the author from the parsing. The author withdraws before payment —
    tell those who decided. The duty robot: to the approvers — a request waiting longer than
    N hours, to accounting — a deadline before tomorrow; once a day by "Reminded".
    AN INTERNAL HELPDESK (IT, facilities, passes). Sections — a list:
    order, name, deadline in hours, the performer code, "Show".
    A performer writes a section code — the section is appended as a string "IT;
    Facilities" to their "People" row; the employee code is empty — anyone submits.
    The form (the section by a button, where, what, a photo) — a draft in personal
    variables, the answers by markers through the parsing; a photo is forwarded ONLY
    in the parsing. A new one — to the section's performers, none — to the head.
    Keep the performer in YOUR OWN columns "Perf ClientID/Messenger/BotID":
    the row's autofields belong to the author. Statuses new → in progress →
    done → closed / cancelled; "Take" — by an edit with the condition "number
    and status new" and "Move", re-read: someone else's "Move" — "already taken by Name".
    A rating s~N~1..5 — only from "done", once; "Not done"
    with a reason — back in progress with the same performer. The duty robot:
    not taken longer than N minutes, overdue, done without a rating longer than
    N hours — close; "Reminded" by letters (r, o), so as not to repeat.
    A PROMO CAMPAIGN BY RECEIPTS. Parse the receipt photo IN THE PARSING (block 0): media
    intake with an empty recipient list → "AI model" with the type «Анализ
    изображения» and imgurl = #{media_url}. The link lives about a minute,
    and after "Launch block" the file is no longer in the incoming message — call the AI model
    in the same scenario. BEFORE the request check that the campaign is on and the daily limit of receipts
    is not exhausted: each request costs money. The AI model only
    reads — ask for an answer as lines "FIELD: value" (RECEIPT, DATE, SUM,
    INN, FN, FD, READABLE), "no" — if not visible; JS decides: not a receipt —
    refusal, not readable or resultvar = 0 — to the moderator, outside the period,
    the sum below the minimum, someone else's INN — refusal with a reason, otherwise chances.
    One receipt — once: the key FN+FD (without them — date, time, sum, INN)
    among accepted and pending ones. A card for the moderator with sendtofileid
    — only to the same Telegram bot as the photo; forward the photo to the others
    in the parsing. The draw is run by the duty robot on the organiser: a lot weighted
    by chances, the winners with codes — as rows in the draw row (the autofields
    of the rows the duty robot writes belong to the organiser).
    REPOSTING A TELEGRAM CHANNEL TO MAX AND VK. The bot is a channel administrator;
    a post comes as the event _channelpost to the start block. The formatting —
    from the post markup: JSON.parse(#{_request}).channel_post, the fields text/
    caption and entities/caption_entities; build HTML in JS (<b>, <i>, <u>,
    <s>, <code>, <pre>, <a href>; escape the text &amp; &lt; &gt;) and send it with
    "MAX groups and channels" → message. Photos and videos: in JS
    _channelpost_download = 1, then "Receive and forward media" (image,
    video) without recipients, the links from media_urls — into messagemedia. To the VK
    wall — the "HTTP request" wall.post (owner_id = -the community ID, from_group=1,
    the key of a USER who is an administrator): text only, append the address to links.
    Cut off a repeat of the same post with the key "channel:number"
    in a variable. VK answers 200 even with an error: save response.post_id,
    error.error_code and error.error_msg and, if there is no post number, write
    the reason to the owner in their language. Code 27 ("Group authorization failed") —
    a COMMUNITY key from "Working with API" was entered, while the wall accepts only
    a user key: the most frequent reason for "created the API, but there is no post".
    5 — the key is outdated; 7, 15, 203, 214 — the key owner has no rights to the
    wall. Check the key in advance with an owner command — wall.get with the same
    key rejects a community key in the same way.
    A COMMENT MONITOR IN A SEPARATE CHANNEL. Telegram: record the automatic forward of a post
    to the discussion group (_tggroup_is_automatic_forward) as a row "thread
    → channel post", find a comment by the group and the thread; the link is
    t.me/<channel>/<post>?comment=<comment number>. VK — the event
    _vkcomment, the link in _vkcomment_url. MAX — the event _maxcomment
    (comments are turned on in the channel settings, the bot is an administrator;
    without _maxcomment_user_id — on behalf of the channel), the link — only to a post
    the bot published (_group_message_url at publication). Under the monitor
    post: a link button "Open" and a button "✅ Viewed" (the data —
    the row number). A button under a CHANNEL post comes as a callback from the presser,
    _tggroup_id — the channel; the engine puts the button caption into the block's answer, the text
    is not empty. The mark — "Telegram groups and channels" → mesedit with the same
    channel and post number; the edit's buttons — only links, the others
    disappear by themselves.
    SEVERAL CHANNELS — AS BUNDLES IN A LIST: a row — a Telegram channel, its
    discussion group, a MAX channel, a VK community, a monitor. Any event
    first finds its bundle: a post — by the channel (ID or @name), a Telegram
    comment — by the discussion group, and if there is none — by the post of the thread; MAX —
    by _maxcomment_chat_id; VK — by _vkcomment_owner_id (with a minus). Record the discussion
    group into the bundle by the automatic forward of a post. Repost — only
    from Telegram: the bot's post to a MAX channel comes back as a channel event, and a repost
    from there would loop the channels. Accept "Viewed" only from the chat where
    the card went.
    AN ELECTRONIC QUEUE (public service centre, clinic, service). Services — a list: the ticket
    letter, minutes per person; windows — a list: window, services by letters separated by ";"
    (empty — all), the window code. A ticket = letter + the day's number for the service
    ("А-017"); read the day's tickets with the filter "Day = today" (calculate the day in a step
    BEFORE the read, as a number YYYYMMDD), a numeric filter by day — with both
    bounds. The service queue — "waiting" by "Order" (the time of taking), then
    by number; someone who returned after a no-show ("I am here" within N minutes) —
    "Order" = time − 10¹², ahead of everyone. Waiting = ahead × minutes /
    working windows of this service (not on a break). A call — by an edit
    with the condition "number and status waiting" and "Move", re-read; the ticket's operator —
    in the columns "Op ClientID/Messenger/BotID". To the first waiting person — "you are
    next" once (a flag in the row). "Done" — from the status that
    was (called or at the window). A QR at the entrance — the link ?start=q: mark in a personal
    variable "came today" if a ticket is given only by QR.
    A WAITING LIST FOR RESTOCKING. Products — a list (a Latin code: it is in the link
    ?start=w-CODE; variants separated by ";"); availability — a list "code, variant,
    in stock" — the stock on the shelf, not the arrival; one row per product
    and variant: read by code and variant — if it exists, edit; if not, a new one.
    Free = in stock − set aside (offered + reserved); the free quantity
    goes to those waiting by the time of the record. Calculate the distribution in JS "in the mind":
    first close expired offers and reservations, then distribute —
    what was freed goes out in the same pass. Edits of a waiting record — only
    from a status + "Move". A refusal and "did not pick up" immediately offer it to the next one.
    THE NAME OF A FIELD VARIABLE ≠ THE NAME OF THE "ROWS FOUND" FLAG: if the read's flag
    is p_found, do not read the "In stock" field into p_found — the values overwrite each
    other, and the rows "are not found". Name the field differently (p_qty).
    A SCREEN VARIABLE ≠ A READ FIELD VARIABLE: you read requests into r_ — the field
    "Text" lands in r_text. Do not put your screen's text there and do not take
    r_code for your own needs: while there are several rows, it holds an array, and the substitution
    shows the subscriber ["row1","row2"]. Your own variables get their own
    prefix (rs_text).
    AN ELECTRONIC WARRANTY CARD. The QR on the box — ?start=g-MODEL CODE.
    Registration in three steps (S/N, purchase date, receipt photo) — a draft
    in personal variables, the steps — by markers through "Parsing"; each step
    checks that the previous ones are passed (/start erases the draft). S/N —
    uppercase without spaces, Cyrillic look-alikes (А, В, Е, К, М, Н, О, Р, С,
    Т, Х) — as Latin. One S/N — one card: a read by model, S/N and
    "valid" at the S/N step and once more before writing. The card dates — as days
    YYYYMMDD, add months by the calendar (31.01 + 1 = 28.02); a filter by
    day — with both bounds. The receipt photo — the File ID and the bot: show it by
    File ID only in the same Telegram bot. Forward the photos and videos of a request
    in "Parsing" (after a jump there is no incoming file), the caption
    of a photo — in _caption. A request: new → in progress → ready → handed over,
    a refusal — with a reason; edits from a status + "Move".
    COLLECTING PRIMARY DOCUMENTS FOR ACCOUNTING. A document directory: code (up to 8 Latin
    characters — it travels in a button), name, what to send, "for" (everyone or
    a feature: till, staff), mandatory. A client needs the documents "for everyone"
    and for their features; take the features for the ticks from the same column. The period —
    YYYYMM: collection for the previous month, the deadline — a day of the next month.
    ONLY ADD receipts as a row (received / there was none / returned),
    the document state is the last row: the client's two contacts
    and the accountant have nothing to share, there are no edits. Forward a file to the accountant
    in "Parsing" by the marker files~CLIENT:DOC:YYYYMM, text by the same marker —
    a note. "Return" carries the time of the last row (in base 36): if the client
    managed to send something new — refusal "look at the new one first". Reminders —
    a ladder of days from the deadline; "Reminded" for a client as a string "YYYYMM:-4,-1,z"
    is written before sending; do not catch up on missed steps — send the last one.
    A MANAGEMENT COMPANY AND A HOMEOWNERS' ASSOCIATION. A flat — by the personal account: a link
    ?start=a-ACCOUNT from the receipt or the account + the flat number (count the attempts).
    Meter readings — only in the submission window ("15-25"), the flat's meters in
    turn by the marker "reading~ACCOUNT:i", the draft — in personal variables. Less than
    the previous ones (the last BEFORE this month) — the same meter again, a big
    consumption — a note in the summary; a summary with buttons also needs a marker: the text
    there answers "press the button". Readings are only added; the bill uses
    the last row of a meter for the month. A request: category, description, photo —
    to employees in "Parsing"; statuses — from what was read + "Move". "👎 Not
    solved" returns it to work; after a repeated "done" a rating is needed
    again — "already rated" only with "👍". An announcement — into the house chat (bound
    by an employee's command "/house CODE" in the group) and to the residents of the house in a private chat with one
    sending.
    NEW BUILDINGS: SELECTION AND RESERVATION. The apartment grid — a list of flats (code, building,
    floor, number, rooms, price, status free/sold/closed); a reservation
    lives in its own list: the flat status "reserved" IS CALCULATED from an active
    reservation whose term has not run out, so an expired one frees the flat at once.
    Two reservations of one flat: write the row, re-read the active reservations of the
    flat — the first by time holds, the late one removes their own (an edit
    by number and "Move"). The same with a viewing place in a slot. Ask for the phone
    in the middle of a reservation with the marker "phone~case" (b:CODE or s:DAY:HHMM:CODE); after
    saving — a jump back with the same case. A mortgage — the annuity
    S·r/(1−(1+r)^−n), r = rate/1200; the down payment and the term — not lower and not longer than the
    programme conditions. In a flat number discard "apt.", "flat", "№".
    A BUSINESS CENTRE AND COWORKING. Roles — by codes as text: a company code from the list
    of tenants (a row "tenant" with the company code; changed company —
    the last row is valid), a security code, an administrator code. A guest
    pass: the name by the marker "guest~", the day by a button, a 6-digit code not
    among the issued ones; the company's pass limit per day without cancelled ones. The guard
    writes the code as text (6 digits, remove a space) — the scenario itself decides whether it is
    security; "let in" — an edit from "issued" + "Move" and re-reading, to the employee —
    a message by the pass's autofields. A meeting room: the room's working hours, the reservation
    step; starts — without overlapping active reservations, the duration — up to
    someone else's reservation and to closing, within the company's monthly hour limit. A reservation —
    a row, re-read the room's reservations for the day: the one not overlapped by an earlier one
    holds; the late one marks theirs "late". Choose a room by code
    from all that were read, in JS: the engine's filter compares case.
    A PHARMACY: AVAILABILITY AND RESERVATION. Lists: pharmacies (code, the pharmacists' "Entry code"),
    products (code, name, INN — the active substance, prescription), availability
    (pharmacy, product, stock, price — one row per pair), reservations, waiting.
    Any text of a buyer is a search: all words of the query (the word stem without
    the ending, the dash removed) in the name or INN; one found — the card at once.
    Free = stock − reservations "new" and "assembled". A reservation — a row,
    re-read the product's reservations in the pharmacy: the first by time hold while the
    stock is enough; the late one marks theirs "late". The pharmacist: "assembled",
    "handed over" (stock minus packs), "not in stock" (stock = what was assembled
    by others). Reading and editing an availability row — ALWAYS by pharmacy AND product.
    The duty robot removes a restocking wait: the product is free in its pharmacy or
    in any — a message with a reservation button, "notified".

qb.cfg.referal("get", { resultvar: "referrallink" })
    the referral system:
      "get"                   Get a referral link
      "add"                   Add/Subtract points
      "addref"                Add/Subtract points by levels
      "pay"                   Make a payout
      "payref"                Make payouts by levels
      "count"                 Get the number of referrals
      "getbalancereferral"    Get the total balance of referrals
      "getbalancereferer"     Get the total balance of referrers
      "getpaidreferral"       Get the total payouts to referrals
      "getpaidreferer"        Get the total payouts to referrers
      "getreferral"           Get the referrals' data
      "getreferer"            Get the referrers' data
      "getlevel"              Get the number of levels of the referral system
    several operations in a row: qb.cfg.referalOps([ { type: "add", balance: "10" },
    { type: "count", resultvar: "referrals" } ])

qb.cfg.gsheets({ account: googleAccount, sheetid: "…", listindex: 0, ops: [
    { type: "find", where: [ { col: "Phone", value: "#{phone}" } ],
      save: { "Name": "name" }, last: true, filtervar: "found" },
    { type: "add", add: { "Name": "#{name}", "Phone": "#{phone}" } },
    { type: "edit", where: [ { col: "ID", value: "#{id}" } ], edit: { "Status": "paid" } }
  ] })
    Google Sheets. Operations: find, add, edit. The row filter is the same as for lists:
    sort: { col, asc }, unique, last, from, to, filtervar

qb.cfg.gcalendar({ servicejson: "…", ops: [ { type: "eventinsert", calid: "#{calendarid}",
                   eventstart: "#{start}", eventend: "#{end}", eventsummary: "#{service}" } ] })
    Google Calendar:
      "eventget"              Event: get
      "eventinsert"           Event: add
      "eventdelete"           Event: delete
      "eventupdate"           Event: change
      "eventlist"             Event: get a list
      "eventmove"             Event: move to another calendar
      "eventwebhook"          Event: notification settings
      "eventwebhookrenew"     Event: renewing notifications
      "eventwebhooksync"      Event: synchronisation
      "freebusy"              Checking busyness
      "calget"                Calendar: get
      "calinsert"             Calendar: add
      "caldelete"             Calendar: delete
      "calupdate"             Calendar: change
      "listlist"              Lists: get all

    LEAVE THE SERVICE ACCOUNT KEY (servicejson) EMPTY. It is the Google access JSON
    file, the owner pastes it into each calendar action
    by hand: substitutions do not work in this field, writing a variable there
    is useless. So make as few calendar actions as possible —
    the key will have to be pasted into each of them.

    IF THERE IS NO KEY OR ACCESS, THE ACTION SILENTLY DOES NOTHING and does not touch
    the variables. So BEFORE the call set the success flag to "0" in a separate
    step (the variable from isopvar), and AFTER it check it with a branch. The bot must
    work without the calendar too: save the booking in your own list in any case,
    and tell an employee that the event did not get into the calendar.

    "EVENT: GET A LIST" (eventlist) RETURNS EVENTS WITHOUT A DATE FILTER:
    the engine passes Google only the calendar, eventstart and eventend are not
    taken into account — up to 250 events come, starting from the oldest. For "what is today" and
    "is it free" take "Checking busyness" (freebusy) for the needed day, and
    keep the meetings the bot creates itself in your own list.

    BUSYNESS (freebusy) — ONE REQUEST PER DAY, not a request for each
    time: eventstart "#{day} 00:00", eventend "#{day} 23:59", eventtimezone
    from the settings ("Europe/Moscow"). isbusy is the flag "is ANYTHING at all busy
    in this interval", it is useless for a time grid; parse the answer
    from responsevar: there calendars[id].busy is the list of busy intervals,
    and timeMin is the start of the requested day. The time comes in RFC3339,
    and the bounds and the intervals may be written differently ("+03:00"
    and "Z"), so in JS code bring both to UTC minutes and count
    from timeMin — then the server's time zone does not affect anything.

    THE EVENT DATE — "dd.mm.yyyy hh:mm" (a date only without a time = an all-day
    event). Calculate the end from the start: eventend "[AddMinutes(#{day}
    #{time}, #{service_duration})]" — a hard-coded hour breaks all
    services except hour-long ones.

    PUT THE NUMBER OF THE CREATED EVENT (resultvar of eventinsert) INTO THE LIST ROW
    next to the booking AT ONCE: by it the event is later moved (eventupdate)
    and cancelled (eventdelete). Without it there is nothing to cancel in the calendar with.
    A change rewrites the event AS A WHOLE, so fill in the name and description
    of eventupdate again — otherwise an empty line stays in the calendar.

qb.cfg.gdrive({ account: googleAccount, ops: [ { type: "create", filename: "…",
                                               fileurl: "#{media_url}", fileurlvar: "link" } ] })
qb.cfg.yadisk({ account: yandexAccount, ops: [ { type: "create", … } ] })
    disks: create — upload a file, delete — delete

qb.cfg.crm("send", { account: crmAccount, send: [ … ] })
    CRM. The contents of send/accept are set by the integration recipe — do not invent them yourself

qb.cfg.doc([ { type: "variable", name: "name", value: "#{name}" } ], { file: "…", url: "", filename: "", pdf: true })
    a document from a template. Elements:
      "variable"              Variable
      "cycle"                 Loop
      "cond"                  Condition
      "pic"                   Image
      "link"                  Link
    Write the type as a string — the builder itself turns it into the object { label, value }
    that the engine and the editor read.
    Table rows (invoice items): { type: "cycle", name: "items", vars: [
      { name: "product", value: "#{inv_product}" }, { name: "sum", value: "#{inv_sum}" } ] },
    where each variable is a JSON array of one length (JS: JSON.stringify([...])),
    and in the template the table row is: {#items}{product} … {sum}{/items}.
    A QR code as a picture: { type: "pic", name: "qr", width: "150", height: "150",
    imgtype: "qrcode", qrdata: "#{inv_qr}" }. A QR FROM AN EMPTY STRING DROPS THE WHOLE
    DOCUMENT — always put a non-empty string into qrdata (no details — the invoice text).
    To whom to send the finished document — in the second object:
      docto, doctomes, doctobtn, doctosubscribers      to subscribers in the bot
      emailcheck, email, emailmes                      by email
      messengercheck, messengerall, messenger,
      messengermes, messengerbtn                       to an employee
    The same fields with "x" (docxto…, emailx…, messengerx…) send the original .docx
    before it is turned into pdf. The rule for choosing recipients is the same as for sending
    to an employee: messengerall true, if the description does not say to whom exactly

IF A BUILDER LACKS THE NEEDED FIELD

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.7. done — THE "DO" BLOCK
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

done describes what happens after a step besides the transition. Give only
the needed fields, the rest are filled in by themselves.

THE FIELD NAMES HERE ARE A CLOSED LIST, given below. An invented name
does not exist: the assembly does not record such a field and returns the remark "unknown
field". A frequent invention is one compound field instead of two: a jump to a block
is set ONLY by the pair exittype: "startmes" and startmesnum, there are no
exitstartmes and exitstartmesnum.

THE FIRST RULE OF "DO": EACH CAPABILITY HERE HAS ITS OWN CHECKBOX
SWITCH, and without it the filled fields are not read at all. No error is
visible anywhere: in the editor everything looks configured, and the bot is silent.
  exit: true            — opens the WHOLE exit: exitmes, exitbtn, exitmedia,
                          exittype: "startmes", startmesnum, startmestime
  addtagcheck: true     — opens addtag
  deltagcheck: true     — opens deltag
  tagtocheck: true      — the addtag/deltag tags go NOT to oneself but to the recipients
                          tagto: [{ clientid, messenger, botid }]
  sendto: true          — opens sendtomes, sendtobtn, sendtosubscribers
  messengercheck: true  — opens messengermes, messengerbtn, messengermedia
  webhookcheck: true    — opens webhook
  emailcheck: true      — opens email, emailmes
  metrika: true         — opens metrikaid
                          (together with it metrikaname — the Yandex account
                          and metrikacounter — the counter number)
Filled a field — set its checkbox. This is the most frequent silent breakdown.

  exit: true                 end the scenario and return to the dialog
  exitmes: "Request accepted" a message to the subscriber on exit
  exitbtn: [ ... ]           buttons for this message
  exitmedia: [ ... ]         media for this message
  exitfileid: "#{photo}"     send a file by File ID (Telegram, MAX):
                             a picture, a video or a document saved
                             earlier from #{_fileId}
  exitfileidnote: true       send this file as a VIDEO NOTE (sendVideoNote,
                             Telegram only). Set it only when
                             exitfileid holds a video note identifier: Telegram does not accept
                             an ordinary video with this flag, and a video note
                             without the flag goes out as a rectangular video. A video note
                             has NO caption — exitmes goes out as a separate message
  exittype: "startmes"       instead of returning — go to a block
  startmesnum: "3"           which block to launch (with exittype: "startmes").
                             This is the block's NUMBER (counted from 1) or its ID. The block
                             name does not work here: the engine looks only for
                             a number or an id, finds nothing and goes
                             nowhere — silently, without an error
  startmestime: true         launch this block NOT now but later
  startmestimedata: "120"    when exactly. ATTENTION, here the count goes FORWARD from
                             the current moment: a number — in that many minutes
                             from "now". This is not the same as the booking reminders
                             in the calendar, where the number means "how many
                             minutes BEFORE the appointment". Do not confuse them.
                             A string like "dd.mm.yyyy hh:mm" — launch at that
                             time. Substitutions work, so the moment can
                             be calculated from anything:
                             "[AddMinutes(#{date_time}, 180)]" — three hours
                             after the appointment, "[AddMinutes(#{date_time}, -60)]" —
                             an hour before it.

  backnotify: true           A NOTIFICATION. Set ONLY together
  backnotifynum: "id"        with exittype "back": the branch both answers the subscriber
  backnotifytimedata:        now and schedules a block for the future. Without this
    "[AddMinutes(#{date_time}, -180)]"   mark you would have to choose one
                             of the two — there is one exittype.

                             It takes THE SAME slot as "Launch block"
                             (startmesnum + startmestime): they never come together
                             in one branch, and the next setting overwrites
                             the previous one. So set a notification
                             about the nearest event, not about each in a row.

                             The subscriber's actions do NOT cancel it — this is what
                             distinguishes it from startmesnotify below. The block is looked for
                             in THE SAME chain where the scenario ran.

                             The fields are read as for "Launch block": the number or ID
                             of the block and the time — as a number (minutes ahead) or as a string
                             "dd.mm.yyyy hh:mm" after substitutions. Hence
                             the usual form "three hours before the event":
                             "[AddMinutes(#{date} #{time}, -180)]".

                             Make the notification block an ordinary block with [Actions]
                             and a "before sending" scenario: when it comes,
                             the scenario checks whether the event is still alive and writes
                             to the point.

                             A BLOCK THAT CAME BY TIME BECOMES THE CURRENT ONE, even
                             if the scenario stayed silent (BotsController.js,
                             sendMessage: answers.push). Presses of value buttons
                             (nextdata) and text are parsed by the current block, so
                             a subscriber who was on another
                             screen at that minute "loses" their buttons there. Give the
                             notification block the same branches as the screens where
                             the subscriber may be by that time (cancellation,
                             rating), and a fallback — to a meaningful screen.
                             This is how the check of an order without a driver is built
                             in "Taxi Service AI": after waitcancel minutes
                             a "new" order is cancelled, removed from the drivers,
                             and the passenger gets "search again".

                             TEXT STARTED ON AN INPUT SCREEN ALSO COMES HERE.
                             The person pressed "Write to the driver", is writing — and at this
                             minute the notification fired: their message
                             is parsed by the notification block, not by the conversation screen.
                             Input screens set a waiting marker (the variable
                             "waiting_text" = "chat~time" or "review~time"), the menu
                             and sending remove it, the notification block's scenario
                             turns a fresh marker (half an hour) into flags, and the block
                             itself has answervar and branches by the flags: the text
                             goes to the other party or into a comment. This is how the taxi works.

                             A notification is also set with an EMPTY exit message.
                             The engine does not send an empty message at all, so
                             the subscriber sees nothing, and the block for the future
                             is scheduled. This is the way to set a reminder
                             silently: the branch answers nothing, only schedules.
                             If an answer is needed, just fill in the exit
                             message, one does not interfere with the other.

  startmesnotify: true       A REMINDER ABOUT A FORGOTTEN STEP. This is not "launch
  startmesnotifynum: "id"    a block by time": the reminder lives only while
  startmesnotifytimedata:    the subscriber IS SILENT. ANY action of theirs in the bot —
    "60"                     a message, a button press, a return to the menu —
                             removes it entirely, and the block does not come.

                             It is set together with an ordinary answer: the branch answers
                             the subscriber (exittype "back" with exitmes) and at the same time
                             schedules the reminder. The fields are read as
                             for startmestime: the number or ID of the block and the time as a number
                             (minutes ahead) or as a string "dd.mm.yyyy hh:mm"
                             with substitutions. The block is looked for in THE SAME chain.
                             There is one slot: the next setting overwrites the previous one.

                             It fits: "did not finish the form", "did not pay the invoice",
                             "chose a service and vanished", "put into the cart
                             and did not check out" — everything where the subscriber abandoned a step
                             in the middle and must be brought back.

                             It does NOT fit to remind about an event days later:
                             the client opens the bot at least once in that time,
                             and the reminder is erased. For an event — see the rule
                             about chains above.

A JUMP TO A BLOCK MUST NOT CARRY ITS OWN MESSAGE. With exittype "startmes" the engine
first substitutes exitmes into the target block's text and only then launches
that block's scenario. The target scenario substitutes its own answer ONLY if the text
stayed empty. So a message on a jump OVERRIDES the target block's answer —
together with the buttons it builds from variables: the choice screen comes
without a single button, and no error is visible anywhere.
FORBIDDEN: done: { exit: true, exittype: "startmes", startmesnum: "service_choice",
                exitmes: "There is no such service, choose from the list" }
CORRECT: put the reason for the return into a variable and jump silently:
  qb.step("no_service", { config: qb.cfg.setVar("reason", "There is no such service."),
    done: { exit: true, exittype: "startmes", startmesnum: "service_choice" } })
and the choice screen's scenario shows it above its text and clears it right away
(in JS code: build the text from the variable "reason" and write an empty string into it).
A message of your own on a jump is allowed only when the target block
has no scenario of its own: there is nothing to override there.

JUMPS IN A ROW WORK UP TO FIVE. The target block's scenario can also lead away
with the exit "Launch block" — the engine runs the scenario of the next block too, and so on
up to five jumps in a row (beyond that — a loop, and the subscriber never gets an answer).
The pattern "text parsing → handler → next screen" relies on this: the parsing
block leads by a marker to the handler, and the handler after writing — to the payment
or sending screen. A delayed jump (startmestime) in such a chain is put
into the scheduler and shows nothing now. All the more do not put your own message (exitmes)
in such a chain — see the rule above.

A DELAYED LAUNCH — HOW IT DIFFERS FROM AN ORDINARY ONE. With startmestime the message
is NOT sent now at all: the scenario only remembers which block to launch and when,
and ends. So exitmes, exitbtn and exitmedia next
to startmestime are what the subscriber will NOT get: neither text nor buttons.
It looks harmless (the answer is written and the reminder is set), but the client
is left without an answer. Answer in one step, set the delayed launch in the next one. The simplest — in one scenario: a "Send to bot" step to the subscriber themselves ([ClientID], [Messenger], [BotID]) with a confirmation, the next step — an exit with a delayed launch. Or like this: the confirmation to the subscriber and the scheduling
are two DIFFERENT blocks: the first answers and with dontwaitanswer passes control
to the second, and the second silently schedules. The second block must NOT be left empty —
empty blocks are forbidden; give it [Actions], and let the scenario exit
without a message: the bot stays silent, and the block stays correct.
A subscriber has ONE place for a delayed launch: the next one overwrites the previous one.
So build a chain of delayed messages in turn — each step
schedules the next one, not all at once.

A REMINDER ROUND AND HOW TO STOP IT. A block that schedules ITSELF
gives an endless reminder: "do not forget" comes every N minutes until
the person has done the thing. It is built with one branch of the block's scenario:
exittype: "startmes", startmesnum = the ID OF THIS SAME block, startmestime: true,
startmestimedata = "#{interval}".

Cancelling a scheduled launch by scenario means is IMPOSSIBLE — there is no mark "remove
the delayed launch". There are two ways:
  • reschedule — set a delayed launch of a harmless block, it
    overwrites the previous one. Crude, but without keys;
  • call the constructor API with the "Data exchange" action:
    POST https://api.quescha.com/api/message/next/clear, a JSON body
    { "key": "#{api}", "clientid": "[ClientID]", "botid": "[BotID]" }.
    The constructor removes the subscriber's delayed launch (nexttime: null).
The ready bot "100 days of 100 push-ups" is built this way: the reminder runs
in a round, and the "Turn off reminders" button calls this method.

IT IS BETTER TO BUILD THE ROUND SO THAT IT GOES OUT BY ITSELF — then neither a key nor a method
is needed, and the bot works right after installation. The trick: the scheduling is done not by the block
that reminds, but by a SEPARATE SCHEDULER BLOCK right after it.

  the "Reminder" block   [Actions], dontwaitanswer, the scenario decides:
                         participation closed or the period ended → #{plan_minutes} = 0
                         the day is already marked → silent, #{plan_minutes} = 1440
                         otherwise → a message with buttons, #{plan_minutes} = 1440
                         transition → the "Scheduler" block
  the "Scheduler" block  [Actions], the scenario: #{plan_minutes} = 0 — does
                         nothing; otherwise the exit "Launch block" to
                         "Reminder" with the delay #{plan_minutes}

The round lives while the scheduler is given minutes, and goes out as soon as it is not given any.

OPEN A BLOCK WITH dontwaitanswer BY A BUTTON, NOT BY THE "LAUNCH BLOCK" EXIT. If
the scenario of another block (for example, parsing the word "start") exits with startmes
to a block with dontwaitanswer, the engine shows this block and STOPS on it:
the transition to the scheduler does not run, and the person's next message — text,
a location point — goes to this block as an answer and is lost silently. A button,
a delayed launch and a tag lead through dontwaitanswer as they should. So parse a command
word for such a block in a menu with a button, not with a jump.
A separate block is mandatory here for another reason: next to a delayed
launch the exit message is NOT sent, so the reminder and the scheduling
physically cannot be in one step.

The ready bot "Habit tracker with a stake" is built this way.

A REMINDER BY CONSUMPTION ("the water is running out", "the food is nearly gone", "time to change
the lenses"). A subscriber has ONE place for a delayed launch, and between
scheduling and waking up much changes: the client ordered earlier, the order
was delayed, reminders were turned off. So a reminder that woke up CHECKS
AGAINST THE FACT in the client's card and only then decides:
  • reminders are off or there is no card → #{plan_minutes} = 0, silent;
  • the order is still on the way → silent, check again in a day (1440);
  • the term by the last ACTUAL delivery has not come yet → silent,
    reschedule exactly for the term;
  • there were already 2 reminders without an order → 0, the round goes out (the counter in the card
    is reset by a new order);
  • otherwise → the message "repeat like last time" + "in 2 days" +
    "do not remind", #{plan_minutes} = 2880, counter +1.
The first launch is set by placing an order: the term = the delivery day + quantity ×
consumption − 1 day. Refine the consumption at each delivery by the real interval
between deliveries, rather than taking it from the form forever.
The ready bot "Water delivery: repeat in one tap, containers and deposit" is built this way.

STATUS BY A RECEIPT ("are my clothes ready", "where is my order", "what about the repair").
The record is created by an EMPLOYEE, so the ClientID of the row in the list is theirs, not
the client's. Selecting "my receipts" by ClientID is impossible: the client sees nothing.
  1. The record has the columns "Client ClientID", "Client Messenger", "Client BotID";
     they are empty on creation. The client writes the number to the bot — the scenario
     finds the record by the number and, if the columns are empty, writes their
     [ClientID], [Messenger] and [BotID] there —
     "Client ClientID", "Client Messenger", "Client BotID" (the autofields
     ClientID/Messenger/BotID of this row belong to the employee). The client's selections
     go by three conditions on these columns, notifications — by "Send to bot"
     from them too. Gluing them into one field with "|" is forbidden: see FILTER AND
     CONDITION — WITHOUT "|" AND "&".
  2. The number can be given AS A LINK: https://t.me/BOTNAME?start=NUMBER (for MAX
     https://max.ru/BOTNAME?start=NUMBER). The bot name — #{_botuser}, the messenger —
     #{_bottype}. The number comes into #{start} of the first block; erase the variable
     at once, otherwise on every return to the menu the bot connects the receipt
     again.
  3. The client columns are already taken by someone else — show the status, but do not
     overwrite the owner.
  4. The status is changed by a button "st~number~status" with a check of an allowed transition,
     and limit the list edit ALSO by the previous status: a second employee
     who got there first is not overwritten.
  5. Keep the date of the change with the status and show it to the client ("ready since 12.09");
     name an overdue readiness deadline plainly. A status someone forgot
     to change otherwise looks like the truth.
  6. A status from the accounting system is accepted by a scenario with "Waiting for data": number, status,
     a key from a global variable. A repeat of the same status is not written to the client.
The storage period is the same reminder round with a fact check, only it decides
by milestones: N days after readiness, 3 days before the end of storage, after
the end. Keep which milestones were announced in the client's card as a JSON string {number: milestone},
merge milestones missed in a row into the last one; receipts in progress keep the round
alive (a check on the readiness day), handed-over ones do not.
The ready bot "Dry cleaning: status by receipt and storage period" is built this way.

THE DUTY ROBOT: A SCHEDULE FOR ALL SUBSCRIBERS ("ask 2 hours after the visit", "remind the manager if nobody took it", "a summary on Mondays"). A delayed launch exists only for the subscriber THEMSELVES, but you need to write to other people when the person does nothing themselves (the visit was marked by an employee, a webhook came from the CRM). The pattern:
  1) the event does not write at once but creates a queue row: "Status" = "waiting", "Ask after" = the time as a string "dd.mm.yyyy hh:mm";
  2) one manager is the duty robot's carrier: on entering with a code the scenario writes into the global variable "On duty" the string "[ClientID]|[Messenger]|[BotID]" and sets ITS OWN delayed launch of the "Round" block in 1 minute (through a scheduler block, as in the reminder round);
  3) "Round" — [Actions], dontwaitanswer, a transition to the scheduler, which stands in the scheme right after it.
