The round's scenario: the global "On duty" is not me — the round goes out (0 to the scheduler); quiet hours — sleep till morning; otherwise read the queue, select those whose time has come (up to 5 at a time), for each — a step editing the row "waiting" → "asked" (the filter condition INCLUDES the previous status and filtervar) and a "Send to bot" step by the parts of this row's "Contact"; still more left — wake up in 2 minutes, none — in 15;
  4) the time of the next round — into a global variable; the manager's account shows "Turn on the duty robot" if the round overslept by more than 20 minutes (the server was down for a day — the engine drops overdue launches);
  5) the duty robot's carrier is a live person: the round shifts their current block. So a bot with a duty robot has not a single value button, and text — through the "waiting" marker (see CROSSING FLOWS), and the round's scenario does not touch this marker;
     the exception is a console bot, where EVERY block, including the scheduler, leads the answer to the parsing by the first block: there value buttons and free text work for the duty robot's carrier too. But turning the duty robot on is an ORDINARY block (waits for an answer, leads to the parsing): "You are on duty" — by sending to oneself, the first round is set by its own scenario. A "do not wait for an answer" block that wrote to the person itself takes their next press for itself and leads to the scheduler — the press is lost. A scheduler that got an answer instead of a scheduled launch (text_in is not empty) gives it to the parsing;
  6) the "Send to bot" step — without branching, the limit is 100 actions per run: slots of 4 steps × 5 plus reads fit with room to spare.
Count time from UTC, not from the server clock: local time — by the account time zone, the offset from '[CurrentDate]|[CurrentTime]' in code (see THE ACCOUNT TIME ZONE).

A DUTY ROBOT IN A GROUP BOT — NOT ON AN EMPLOYEE. If the bot works in groups, both employees and clients must stand on the START block: the engine discards a message in a group from someone standing on another block. A duty robot round on a manager would switch them off from the group. The pattern:
  1) the timer — a delayed launch of THE SAME start block (exittype "startmes", startmesnum — the start block's id, startmestime, startmestimedata — minutes) on the subscriber of the group member whose message turned on the waiting. The person stays on the start block, the round is a branch of this block's scenario;
  2) THE TIMER LAUNCHES THE BLOCK WITH THE OLD VARIABLES: #{_tggroup}, #{_tggroup_text}, #{_tggroup_message_id} remain from the last event, and the timer is easily taken for a new message in the group. Remember the key of the parsed message in a variable: "tg:" + #{_tggroup_id} + ":" + #{_tggroup_message_id} (for MAX — #{_maxgroup_id} and #{_maxgroup_message_id}). The same key again — it is the timer. After a private message the timer is recognised more simply: an empty [Answer], no file and no #{_tggroup_id};
  3) the round looks at ALL waiting rows at once and writes one message as a list. Keep which reminder for which row has already gone out as a map in a global variable ("row key → mark"): a scenario does not edit several list rows in a loop;
  4) count the deadline in WORKING minutes: a question at 21:00 and an answer at 9:10 are 10 minutes of waiting, not 12 hours. A loop over days — a for with a ceiling.
The ready bot "Control of answers in client chats" is built this way.

AN AI AGENT: AN EMPLOYEE WHO WORKS ON A SCHEDULE AND REPORTS IN A MESSENGER. It is recognised by the description: "every morning check the stock and write what to order", "make sure managers answer requests", "once a day collect brand mentions", "on Mondays send an advertising report". This is not a chat bot that waits for a question: it wakes up itself, takes data, gives it to the AI model, takes an action and reports. Build it from eight nodes — without any of them the agent either spends the balance silently or does extra things outside:
  1) THE SCHEDULE AND "RUN NOW". The round — by a duty robot round (see THE DUTY ROBOT: A SCHEDULE FOR ALL SUBSCRIBERS): the owner becomes the duty robot's carrier on entering with a code, the frequency — the global "Round every, minutes", the report hour and Monday — by the account time zone (see THE ACCOUNT TIME ZONE). The "▶️ Run now" button sets the same round in a minute and writes into the global "Manual run" whom to send the result to; the round, having seen the mark, sends the result to the presser and clears it.
  2) THE AGENT'S INSTRUCTION — AS A SETTING, NOT AS CODE: the globals "Agent's task", "Tone", "What counts as important", "What not to do" with meaningful default values — the owner changes the behaviour with words, without editing the scenario. The question to the AI model is built in a JS step from these settings and the run's data; the answer is strict JSON (see AN AI MODEL THAT DOES NOT ANSWER BUT ASSEMBLES AN ORDER), parsed by code in try, empty and extra fields are discarded. The AI model is silent — its own branch on resultvar = '0'.
  3) A HUMAN IN THE LOOP. Everything that goes outside (an answer to a client, a post, a letter to a supplier, an ad bid) — first as a draft: a row of the "Drafts" list (Number, To whom, Text, Status "waiting") and to the owner the buttons "✅ Send / ✏️ Edit / ❌ Not needed" with the values "ok~number", "ed~number", "no~number". Sending edits the row ONLY from the "waiting" status — a second press sends nothing. "Edit" sets the "waiting" marker with the draft number (see CROSSING FLOWS): the owner's next line is a remark ("shorter", "suggest a call on Thursday"), the AI model rewrites the draft with it. The "autonomous" mode — the global "Agent mode" (draft / autonomous), by default — draft; in the autonomous mode the owner gets a copy. A report to the owner themselves or into the work group does not need a draft.
  4) THE "RUNS" LOG. A round in which there was something to do writes a row: Number, When (ms), Day ("YYYYMMDD"), Kind (round / manual / check), what was done — as numbers by kinds of work, Calls, Failure. The "📒 Runs" button — the last ten. On Mondays — "🧾 Week": what the agent did and how much time it saved (minutes per unit of work × how many times: a draft — 5, a parsed record — 3, a summary — 15). This is what the subscription is renewed for.
  5) A SPENDING LIMIT: the globals "Runs per day" and "AI calls per day". Today's calls — the global "AI: today" as a string "YYYYMMDD:number", rounds — by today's rows of "Runs". The limit is reached — the agent stops until tomorrow and writes to the owner ONCE a day, instead of spending the balance silently.
  6) A FAILURE — NOT SILENTLY. The service did not answer, the key expired, the AI model stayed silent — to the owner "the agent could not: reason" once a day per kind of failure (a mark in a global), a retry — in the next round, the reason — in "Failure" of the log. The "🔌 Check the connection" button: a test request to each source, to the AI model — "Answer with one word: READY", the bot's time by the account zone, whether the duty robot works.
  7) WHERE TO REPORT: the globals "Report: Telegram group" and "Report: MAX chat" — the ID of the work group, the bot is a member of it; empty — to the owners in a private chat by "Send to bot". In VK one can write only to those who wrote to the community themselves.
  8) A RETRY DOES NOT BREED DUPLICATES. The agent remembers what it has already seen: the record key (a review number, a purchase link, a deal id) — as a column in a list; before parsing read the keys already seen and take only new ones. The summary and the week remember the sending date in a global — they do not go out a second time in a day.
DATA — WHERE IT ALREADY IS. A service with an API — by "Data exchange" (qb.cfg.http): the key and the address — as global settings (see THE KEY AND THE ACCOUNT ADDRESS LIVE IN THE SETTINGS LIST), the key is put into a variable only for the time of the request and cleared at once; the answer lands in resultvar as a string — JSON.parse in try, reset the answer variables before the request (the engine does not overwrite them with an empty value). A table — CSV by a Google spreadsheet link; feeds — RSS; a work group — its messages as list rows; the bot's own lists; an accounting system that sends by itself — by "Waiting for data" (see AN INCOMING STREAM IS ACCEPTED BY A SCENARIO WITHOUT A BLOCK). The AI model without your own key goes from the balance — this is how text requests and «Анализ изображения» work; «Аудио в текст», «Анализ аудио» and drawing pictures — only with your own key from a global, in the card description — as a line "⚙️ Set up after installation".

WEATHER WITHOUT A KEY — OPEN-METEO. A forecast — "Data exchange" GET without a key and without payment: qb.cfg.http({ url: "#{address}", method: "get", headers: { "Accept": "application/json" }, resultvar: "answer" }); the address is built by code:
  1) the forecast: https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=55.79&longitude=49.12&hourly=precipitation,wind_speed_10m,wind_gusts_10m,temperature_2m&wind_speed_unit=ms&forecast_days=8 — do not pass the timezone parameter: the time in the answer is in UTC, match it with the record's ms (the bot's local time — by the account zone), and the zone and the forecast do not diverge;
  2) a point by a city name: https://geocoding-api.open-meteo.com/v1/search?name=<city>&count=1&language=ru&format=json — results[0].latitude/longitude; the name is substituted as it is (the engine encodes the address itself; remove & # ? + % = from the name); it does not find street addresses, only settlements. Keep the found point in the global "City|latitude|longitude" and search again only if the city was changed;
  3) the answer comes as a string in resultvar: JSON.parse in code, hourly.time — "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM", the arrays precipitation (mm per hour), wind_speed_10m (m/s), temperature_2m; once parsed, clear the answer variable, so 8 KB strings do not pile up;
  4) the work rule — by a formula, not by the AI model: for the booking hours the sum of precipitation, the strongest wind, the coldest and the hottest against the service thresholds (empty — the rule does not apply); the AI model only writes the text to the client — with one call for all clients of the round, the answer is JSON by numbers, without it — a template;
  5) in a round — no more than a few requests per run (each request is a step, the engine limit is 100 actions): collect the different points of the bookings by code, identical ones — with one request;
  6) the request may fail (the server does not open the address, Open-Meteo did not answer) — the answer is empty, parsing returns null: do not mark such a booking as checked, repeat in the next round; "Check the connection" makes the same request from the bot's server.
The ready bot "AI weather dispatcher" is built this way.

A DRAFT FOR THE OPERATOR — THE BOT DOES NOT ANSWER THE CLIENT ITSELF. When a live person answers the client, and the AI model only prepares their answer:
  1) "Parsing" leads the client's question to the "Request" block; its scenario ALWAYS exits with a short message to the client ("I passed your question to an operator", "📝 Added to your question"): a block whose scenario exits without a message and without buttons does not wait for the person — its transition fires by itself and returns the event to "Parsing";
  2) to the AI model — in one call: the suitable articles of the "Knowledge base" (selected by code by the stems of the question's words), the previous conversation and the client's text; the answer — JSON {"essence", "order", "urgent", "draft", "articles"}; in the task — write only by the articles and the conversation, do not invent deadlines, prices and order statuses, but "we will clarify and get back to you". The AI model did not answer or the daily limit ran out — a fallback parsing by code: the order number (after the words "order", "number", "№"; not a phone and not a sum), urgency by words ("urgent", "refund the money", "does not work"), the draft — the article's answer if it matched confidently;
  3) to the operator — a card: the essence, the order, urgency, how long it has waited, the draft and the buttons "✅ Send", "✏️ Edit", "💬 Own answer", "✔️ Close". The button data holds the request number and the draft VERSION (sd~12~3): the client added something or the draft was rewritten — the version in the row is different, and the old button shows the fresh card instead of sending the old text. The status "answered" in the row — to a second operator "already answered: name, time";
  4) "Edit" sets the "waiting" marker remark~number; the operator's text — to the AI model together with the draft and the articles (its own and those selected for the remark), the new draft — into the row by an edit with the condition "Version = the previous one": if the client managed to add something, the edit does not touch the row;
  5) the answer to the client — "Send to bot" by ClientID, Messenger and BotID from the request row; a person's text — with the invisible protection character against substitutions, escaping markup < > & — only for Telegram and MAX, in VK — as it is;
  6) each answer — as a row of "Answers": how many minutes the client waited and how they were answered (a draft without editing, with editing, own) — the week comes from it: answer time and the share of answers without editing;
  7) a reminder about unanswered ones — with one edit by conditions (Status = new, Reminded = 0, Came ≤ threshold), not by numbers: new rows do not get into it, their "Came" is later than the threshold. A numeric field in a filter — both bounds.
The ready bot "AI operator prompter" is built this way.
FIT A ROUND INTO 100 ACTIONS. The order of steps: am I the duty robot → the limit → the time (report hour, Monday) → data → new by key (no more than 5–10 per round) → the AI model → actions in cells with literal variable names (a cell: "exists?", the mark before the message, the message) → the log → the next round. More work than cells — the next round in a minute, until it runs out. Branch conditions — by flags '1'/'0', not by the AI model's text (see item 32 of the checklist).
TO THE OWNER — THE AGENT'S MENU: the mode, the frequency, the last round, today's rounds and calls out of the limit; the buttons "▶️ Run now", "🔌 Check the connection", "📒 Runs", "🧾 Week" and the drafts waiting for a decision. In the card description — "⚙️ Set up after installation": the owner code, the agent's instruction, sources and keys, where to report, the time zone in the Account settings.
The ready agents of the catalog (the "AI agents" category) are built this way: "AI sales head" — the bot's requests and CRM deals; "AI work chat secretary" — the group's conversation; "AI morning business summary" and "AI purchasing and stock agent" — MoySklad and a Google spreadsheet; "AI Yandex Direct agent" and "AI Metrica analyst" — the Yandex API; "AI brand mention monitoring", "AI tender agent" and "AI industry digest" — RSS feeds; "AI competitor price monitoring" — website pages; "AI call review" — a recording by link and own transcription; "AI channel editor" — publications by approval; "AI salon administrator" — booking in words by its own lists "Masters" and "Bookings"; "AI spoken language trainer" — «Анализ аудио» with a fallback transcription; "AI inspection at hand-over and return" — "before and after" shots with two analyses and a comparison by code; "AI weather dispatcher" — the Open-Meteo forecast without a key and rescheduling by a weather rule; "AI operator prompter" — an answer draft for the operator from the knowledge base, sending, editing with a remark or own answer; "AI clinic registrar" — the specialty by the complaints table, alarming signs — "call 103", the mark "came / did not come"; "AI car service receptionist" — works and a price range by symptom and photo, closing with a sum and mileage, maintenance by mileage; "AI last-minute slots" — free time tomorrow with a discount for the waiting list and for those who have not come for a long time, booking with the "Take" button with a re-check of the slot; "AI membership retention" — a QR at the entrance and marking by a trainer, a letter to those who started coming less often, freezing, a trainer's call, renewal; "AI sales trainer" — the AI model plays a client with objections, the answer as text or voice, a review by a checklist and a weighted score, the weekly rating; "Voice reports of field employees" — a voice message after a site is laid out into the report fields, a problem and a repeat visit — to the manager at once, the day's result; "AI customer interviews" — the guide questions by link with voice or text, the AI model clarifies general answers, a report with topics, frequency and quotes; "Order by voice or a photo of a list" — a supplier's client orders by voice, a photo of a list or text, items by the price list, the sum — by the bot's calculation, to the manager "Accepted", to the warehouse — picking; "AI crew translator" — the foreman's task to each in their language as message slots with "Understood" in that language, the worker's answer — to the foreman in Russian, the important — with a reminder; "Act and payment by voice on site" — the master dictates the works, an act PDF by the price list (an own row — only with the price the master named), the client by link "I accept" and pays right away, to the office — payments and the day's result; "AI speech and presentation coach" — the student dictates the task as a voice message, the AI model evaluates the structure and the voice, the pace and filler words are counted by the bot, a comparison with the previous attempt, to the coach — the group's week; "AI body repair estimate by photo" — the client sends a photo of the damage, the AI model names the part and the kind, the works — only from the shop's price list, the range is calculated by the bot, to the masters — a card with "Take"; "AI spare part by photo" — the buyer photographs a part or a nameplate, the AI model names what it is, the items in stock are selected by the bot from the product list, a reservation until the evening with a code for the till, not found — to the seller with the photo; "AI garden centre agronomist" — the buyer sends a photo of a plant, the AI model says what it looks like, the products with dosage from the list are selected by the bot, a reservation or delivery, a week later — "how is the plant?"; "AI clothing store stylist" — a photo of one's own item, the occasion and the size by buttons, the AI model builds looks only from stock in the size, the codes are checked by the bot, a fitting with a code, after the purchase — what else to wear it with; "AI mystery shopper" — the task by link, a form by a checklist with photos, voice and "yes-no", the AI model checks the answers against the standard, the weighted score is calculated by the bot, the chain accepts the report, to the point manager — a review; "Store catalog by photo" — the seller photographs a product and says the price, the AI model writes the card, the bot takes the price only from the numbers in the words, publication into "Products" and a post with a photo in the channel, an order from the card; "AI review of managers' conversations" — client dialogs through the bot, the AI model reviews a closed one by a checklist with quotes from the manager's words, the weighted score is calculated by the bot, to the head — a rating and the worst dialogs; "AI upsell after a purchase" — rules "what goes with what" set an offer for a term, at the term the AI model writes the client a personal message with "Take" — a request to the manager, buying the offered item is counted by itself; "AI wholesale client order by cycle" — each client's order rhythm by the median of intervals, at the term "repeat the last order?" with the contents at today's prices, "Repeat" — an order in one tap, silent for two cycles — to the manager "we are losing them"; "AI intelligence: what changed at the competitors" — competitors' pages once a day, what disappeared and what appeared — by code, the essence and "for us" — by the AI model, the important — to employees at once, an error page — a failure, not a change; "AI website and request watchdog" — the website every half hour, an alarm from the second failure in a row and when a phone or a price disappears, the phone — by digits, "works again" with the downtime, a test request through the form once a day, the AI model — only advice for an alarm; "AI team status reports" — on the report day three questions to each, the AI model lays out an answer as text or voice, a reminder to those who did not submit, a summary to the head with "the main thing", a repeated obstacle — by code by word stems; "Team pulse: an anonymous survey" — once a month a score and "why", in the answer row "-" instead of the subscriber, the result: an index, departments from five, topics of "why" with quotes checked against the answers; "AI shift schedule by wishes" — wishes in words into constraints by the AI model, the schedule by norms by a formula, a draft for the head, swapping shifts with consent and approval, the week's table in a mini app; "AI HR officer: applications in words" — the kind and the dates by the AI model, the check, the balance and overlaps by code, the head's decision by a button, an application PDF by a template; "AI regulations collector" — an expert's story by voice into a draft, step-by-step instructions and questions about gaps by the AI model, approval or return with a remark, a knowledge base for the mentor; "AI candidate interviews" — a link from the job ad, answers as text and voice, assessment against the requirements by the AI model, an invitation to a free slot by buttons; "When it suits everyone" — a meeting and answers in words by the AI model, the intersection of windows by a formula, voting and a reminder; "AI project hours tracking" — an evening question by the duty robot, a story about the day as text and voice by the AI model into rows by projects, the week against the budget, a report to the client as PDF; "AI dish cost (food cost)" — the recipe and purchase prices in words by the AI model, the cost and the markup by a formula, dishes below the markup — to the owner with a new price in one button; "AI markdown by expiry dates" — the date from a label photo, a discount by a rule, the post "discounts today" to the channels, to the VK wall and to subscribers, a write-off list; "AI repricing by exchange rate and supplier price list" — the central bank rate and the price list from a Google spreadsheet, the purchase in rubles by a formula, a draft of new prices and "Apply"; "AI sales analyst from a table" — a CSV sales export, ABC, pairs in a receipt, silent products, three pieces of AI advice and the revenue before and after a week later; "AI shift anomalies" — the shift result in words or from a table, the shares of refunds, discounts, cancellations and cash against the point's median, the odd — to the owner personally; "Tax calendar for sole proprietors and LLCs" — a form of the regime by buttons, deadlines from the accountant's list 7 and 2 days before with "Done", the simplified tax by revenue by a formula, answers only by the memo, otherwise — to the accountant; "AI networking" — a form in words, pairs once a week in cells by the AI model with a top-up by a formula, without repeats, contacts after two "yes", the result a week later; "AI marathon host" — sign-up with stars, by link or for free, the task of the day, a report with a photo or text with crediting by the AI model, a rating, the result and a certificate; "AI questions from the audience" — an event with a link and a QR, questions into the bot, the AI model merges similar ones, votes, from the stage and an answer afterwards to everyone who asked; "AI "who will help"" — a form in words, a request, the AI model chooses helpers by the forms, contacts after consent, "did they help?" and the top helpers; "AI interview trainer" — questions by the vacancy text, a review of each answer as text and voice, a repeat of the weak ones, the first interview free, then a pack for stars or by link; "AI fairy tales with illustrations" — a child's form, every evening a chapter of a tale with a continuation by the summary, a picture with own key, a reminder, a subscription for stars or by link; "AI essay and composition checking" — a composition as text or a photo of handwriting, scores by the owner's criteria, errors with quotes, checking a corrected fragment, the week to the parent, a pack for stars or by link; "AI ad check before buying" — an ad for a car or a flat as text and photo, risks, questions to the seller and documents without a price estimate, a request for an inspection to a specialist with a hidden contact, a reminder, a pack for stars or by link; "AI gardener by subscription" — the plot point and what grows, a forecast for the plot without a key and works by the weather, morning summaries on an alarm, a review of a plant by photo with partners' products, a question to a gardener, a subscription for stars or by link.

BOOKING IN WORDS: THE CLIENT WRITES AS TO AN ADMINISTRATOR (a salon, a clinic, a service — "tomorrow after six to Olya for colouring"). The AI model only PARSES the message, free time is calculated by code:
  1) The question to the AI model: today's date with the weekday and the next days as a string "Sat 17.10 = 20261017", services and masters with NUMBERS, the client's previous request, their bookings and the message itself. The answer — JSON {"intent": "booking|move|cancel|question|other", "service": number, "master": number, "day": "YYYYMMDD", "from": "HH:MM", "to": "HH:MM", "booking": number, "answer": "…"}. Parsing accepts only numbers from the lists, a day not in the past, "to" later than "from", an answer — only for "question".
  2) Without the AI model (a call limit, a failure) — parsing by words: today, tomorrow, the day after tomorrow, "on Friday", 17.10, "October 17", "after six" (hour 1–8 without "morning" — after noon), "in the morning" — until 12:00, "in the evening" — from 17:00, "at 15:30" — an hour window; the master — by the name stem and the case ending ("to Olya", "Katya"; "Oleg" is not "Olya"), two match — nobody; the service — by the stems of the words of the "Words" column, a tie — none. The AI model's answer is more important, words fill in where it was silent.
  3) Free time — by a formula: the master's schedule (weekdays, hours, days off) minus this master's live bookings (the overlap of the intervals "Start"—"End" in ms), the service duration, the "Booking step", "Booking not earlier than" from now, "Booking days ahead". Up to three windows — as value buttons "bk~service~master~YYMMDDHHMM" (with the number of a booking being moved — not more than 64 bytes), the nearest other days — as buttons. Empty in the requested window — the nearest after it and a waiting list button.
  4) RE-CHECK the pressed window before booking: read this master's bookings for this day — the window is still free, there is no overlap with the client's own bookings, the client's future bookings are not more than the limit. Taken — "just taken" and a new selection.
  5) A clarification ("what about Saturday?") — the previous request lies in a personal variable as JSON with the time of recording and lives 3 hours: what is not in the new message (the service, the master, the window) is taken from there; another service — the master anew.
  6) Pass directories parsed before the AI model on as JSON in a variable: the step that parsed the read erases it. Read the bookings for the windows AFTER the AI model, in the step that calculates the options.
  7) A cancellation frees the window — for those who wait for it in the waiting list: the same service, this master or "any", the same day, the time in their window; with the button of this window, up to three waiting ones, whoever presses first gets the time (the reservation re-checks).
  8) Booking by an administrator on a call — by the same parsing, the client's name — from the message. Such rows have the administrator's autofields: do not take them into the client's "own bookings" (source "admin"), otherwise they give a false overlap and a false limit.
The ready bot "AI salon administrator" is built this way.

BOOKING A DOCTOR: THE SPECIALTY BY THE COMPLAINTS TABLE, ALARMING SIGNS — BEFORE THE AI MODEL (a clinic, a medical centre). Everything in "Booking in words", plus:
  1) alarming signs — phrases separated by commas in a global ("pressure in the chest, cannot breathe, face drooping"). CODE searches for them before calling the AI model: the words of a phrase in order, each next one no further than three words from the previous one, a word of 4+ letters — by the start of the stem ("pressing" — "pressed"). Found — to the patient "⚠️ … Call 103", without a booking and without the AI model, to the administrators — a message with the patient's text. The AI model also returns the field "alarm": true — the same, no booking is offered;
  2) the specialty — only a NUMBER from the clinic's "Complaints" table (how patients say it → the specialty number) and the "Specialties" list; in the question to the AI model the table as lines "toothache → 2" and a ban: do not advise a diagnosis, treatment or medicines. Parsing discards a number that is not in the list. Without the AI model — by the words of the complaints (a complaint match weighs double) and the specialty's "Words" field; a tie or nothing — do not guess, but show the specialties as buttons;
  3) a disclaimer ("This is not a doctor's consultation: I only book an appointment") — a global, as a line in every booking answer; empty — do not output the line;
  4) the complaint from the first message is kept in the previous request (JSON in a personal variable) and is written as a booking field: the doctor sees it in the booking message and in the day plan. The preparation memo is a specialty field: in the booking confirmation and in the reminder;
  5) visit marking: the statuses "пришел" and "неявка" (came / no-show) without "ё" — conditions compare them. The buttons "✅ Came / 🚫 Did not come" — only for an appointment that has started; the doctor of this booking or an administrator can mark it; a marked one — "already marked". "No-show" — to the patient "book again?" with a button, except bookings by phone;
  6) in the evening from the "Result hour" the duty robot sends the administrators the day's appointments without a mark with marking buttons — once a day, the sending date in a service global.
The ready bot "AI clinic registrar" is built this way.

REPAIR BY SYMPTOM: WORKS AND A PRICE RANGE, A PHOTO, MAINTENANCE BY MILEAGE (a car service, tyre fitting, a workshop). Everything in "Booking in words", plus:
  1) the service's "Symptoms" table — how clients say it, what to check and up to three work numbers of the price list (the first is the main one). The AI model returns the symptom number and work numbers only from the table and the price list; no works but a symptom — the symptom's works are taken. Without the AI model — by word stems: a symptom match weighs double against a work word; a tie — do not guess, but show the works as buttons;
  2) an order — up to three works: minutes and the price range ("From", "To" in the price list) — as a sum. A bay fits if it does the main work; the window — for the sum of minutes. The data of a window button — the works separated by a dot, the bay and the time: bk~2.3~1~2610191800 (up to 64 bytes). In the answer to the client — what we will check, the estimate, "💰 Guide: 1 700 – 3 500 ₽" and the line "exactly — after the inspection"; the AI model does not name an exact price or a definite fault;
  3) a photo — an "image" intake with keeping (the link will go to the receptionist), the caption — from _caption, a call of «Анализ изображения» with imgurl and the same question, in the question — "a photo is attached to the message", in the JSON — the field "photo" (what is visible). The file type is more important than the extension: look at the extension only when the type is empty or "file" — otherwise a voice message with a ".jpg" path goes into the photo;
  4) "ТО" (maintenance) in capital letters is a word too: compare short words (two characters) only as whole words, otherwise "тормоза" (brakes) matches "ТО";
  5) dangerous signs ("does not brake", "smell of petrol", "steam from under the bonnet") — as phrases in a global, CODE searches for them before the AI model: to the client "do not drive further" and the service phone, without a booking, to the administrators — a message with the text and the photo link;
  6) the client's cars — an own list (number, name, mileage and when, mileage and time of the last maintenance, "reminded"): on booking — an own row by name or a new one; by phone there is no row (it would have the administrator's autofields). The receptionist closes the order with a line "7400, 86 000" by the marker "check~number": the sum — into the booking, the mileage — into the booking and the car, and if the works include "Maintenance work" — the maintenance mileage and time anew. To the client — "the car is ready", the result and when the next maintenance is;
  7) maintenance by mileage — by the duty robot: km per day by two measurements (maintenance and the last visit) or "Mileage per month" / 30, an estimate of the current mileage, it is time — after "Maintenance interval, km" or "Maintenance interval, months" from the previous maintenance. The "reminded" mark — the time of the base maintenance: once per maintenance; the "Do not remind" button writes "never". The week — the receipt against the range: within the range, above, below, the average receipt at the average range.
The ready bot "AI car service receptionist" is built this way.

LAST-MINUTE SLOTS: FREE TIME TOMORROW — TO THE WAITING LIST, TO THOSE WHO HAVE NOT COME FOR A LONG TIME, WITH A DISCOUNT (a salon, massage, a barbershop, a photo studio, a bathhouse — any booking by time). Empty slots are filled by the agent, not by calling around:
  1) booking — by buttons: service → master or "any" → day → time by the master's schedule ("Days", "Hours", "Does not work"), the service duration and the "Booking step"; before booking the slot is re-checked. Taken — the waiting list: service, master (0 — any), day, part of the day in minutes "From"–"To", status "waiting / offered / booked / removed". The client's card ("Clients") — master, service, last visit, visits, "Convenient" (morning, afternoon, evening), "Do not offer";
  2) slots — free intervals tomorrow for each master: working hours minus live bookings; an interval shorter than the master's shortest service is not a slot. To whom: first the waiting list (the day, the master or "any", the time in their part of the day), then those who go to this master and have not come for longer than "Has not come for long, days" (the oldest first), then those for whom this time of day is convenient. Do not offer: "Do not offer", a booking already exists ahead, offered more recently than "Not more often than, days". One offer per client; per slot — up to "Candidates per slot", in total — up to "Offers per day";
  3) the time for a candidate — the first free one on the same booking grid as the buttons, inside their part of the day, the service — their previous one if it fits into the slot, otherwise the shortest: otherwise "Take" will not pass the re-check;
  4) the AI model — one call for all: a JSON array of greetings by candidate numbers; the time, the master, the service and the discounted price — as the bot's line, the AI model is forbidden to name them and to promise other discounts; did not answer or "AI calls per day" ran out — a template;
  5) the mailing — with fixed cells of the round (a cell per offer: the client mark, the list mark, a message with buttons), so "Offers per day" is limited by the number of cells. The client's card holds the day and the key of the offered slot; the "✅ Take" button carries the service, the master and the time (hw~1~2~2610171200) and passes only if the key matches the card: someone else's and old — "not valid". The slot is re-checked: already taken — say so; free — a booking at the discounted price, source "slot", the list row — "booked". "Not now" and "Do not offer" — also by buttons;
  6) to the administrator — "Slots for tomorrow": the same calculation without sending, to whom and why; "Send now"; a summary after the mailing; the "Runs" log — slots, offered, calls. The week: bookings, cancelled, slots found, offered, filled, revenue from bookings from slots.
The ready bot "AI last-minute slots" is built this way.

MEMBERSHIP RETENTION: WHOEVER STARTED COMING LESS OFTEN — A LETTER, A FREEZE, A CALL (fitness, a sports section, a school, a studio with a membership for a term or for sessions):
  1) a membership is a row created by the administrator: the client in it — with their own fields "Client ID", "Client messenger", "Client bot" (the row's autofields are the administrator's). Binding — by a personal link with a start marker (t.me/<bot name>?start=ab12_482915, the bot name — _botuser, in MAX — max.ru/…) or "number and code" as text; the code — six digits, count wrong attempts and after five within an hour — a pause; do not rebind someone else's bound row. The start marker is in start only in the start message (_isstart), it is read in "Parsing" and cleared right away;
  2) a visit — a QR at the entrance (a bot link with the marker vz), by the trainer with a line "12, 15, Ivanov" or with a button from the card; once a day; several memberships of one client — "who came?" by buttons. A name in the line is searched by the beginnings of words: an exact match is more important, a live membership is more important, several similar — ask for the number. Editing the membership row — with a cell per visit, the "Visits" log — with one record as arrays. Do not give your own variables the endings of a button set (_names, _blocks, _data): the set overwrites them;
  3) the rhythm and the last visit lie in the membership row: the rhythm — a moving average of the interval between visits, from three visits; after a freeze the interval does not go into the rhythm. The round does not read the visits log. A visit during a freeze removes it, the unused days return to the term;
  4) the risk with a live unfrozen membership with a remainder: "has not started" — not a single visit for longer than "Has not started, days"; "less often than usual" — has not come for longer than max("Gap, days", 2 × rhythm); "vanished" — fewer than three visits and has not come for longer than "Gap, days". "Has not come" is counted from the last visit, but not earlier than the start of the term and the end of a freeze;
  5) write — a risk, the client is bound, no "Do not write", after the previous letter there was a visit and "Not more often than, days" passed; call — the client asked; or we wrote and there has been no visit for "Call after, days"; or "Do not write" and not bound — until they are called after the last visit; renew — "Remind about renewal, days" before the end or at the last session, once per end of term. The severity — days without a visit divided by the threshold;
  6) the AI model — one call for all who vanished: JSON "number → text"; the term, the remainder and the freeze — as the bot's line; it is forbidden to promise discounts, gifts and free sessions and to give health advice; without an answer — a template, about renewal — always a template. Letters — with fixed cells of the round; the mark in the row — with one edit of "Written" and "Reminded", the second field — with its previous value;
  7) a visit after a letter or a call — "Returned" and a message to the trainer. The week: live ones, visits, at risk, written to, called, returned, renewed, ended without renewal and the share of those who quit — ended without renewal to the sum with the renewed ones.
The ready bot "AI membership retention" is built this way.

A SALES TRAINER: THE AI MODEL PLAYS A CLIENT, A REVIEW BY A CHECKLIST (a sales department, a call centre, a salon — training managers without live clients):
  1) client roles and the checklist — the owner's lists: a role — who the client is, objections separated by ";", the goal (what counts as success), the first phrase, difficulty 1–3, whether it is on; a checklist item — the text and the weight 1–3. Entry — "Team code" (a manager) and "Head code";
  2) the training's course — a personal variable (the role, the client's and the manager's lines, the start, the voice mark, notes on delivery) and the "training" marker for half an hour from the last move; text during the training is given by "Parsing" to the move with the data tx, and the text itself — in a separate variable, a voice message — with the data vo. /start resets the personal variables — the training breaks off without consequences;
  3) voice: the file intake (audio, then file) and «Анализ аудио» — in one scenario, the link lives minutes; your own RouterAI, AITunnel or Gemini key and a model that accepts sound; the answer — JSON "text, tone"; did not work — «Аудио в текст» with the same key; no key — "answer with text"; do not parse a recording longer than three minutes;
  4) the client — a separate AI call: keeps the role, raises objections one by one, answers briefly, agrees ("deal") only if the manager found out the need, handled the objections and offered a next step; rudeness and pressure — "refusal"; the answer is JSON "line, end, result"; at the move limit — "make a decision", did not decide — "interrupted". No first phrase in the role — the AI model starts the conversation;
  5) the end, the limit or "Finish" — a review with a separate call: JSON "fulfilled (item numbers), where they lost it, how to do better (up to three phrases)"; numbers outside the checklist are discarded; the score is calculated by the bot — the weight of the fulfilled ones to the weight of all, do not trust the AI model's score. The review did not work — what was played stays, "Finish" once more. The training row — with one record: manager, role, start, end, moves, score, result, fulfilled and missed numbers, voice, delivery, review;
  6) limits — answers in a training, trainings per day per person, AI calls per day for everyone (a training needs at least three); the move calls are counted by a counter and go with one record into the global "day:number";
  7) the head — reviews for 30 days and the training card; on Mondays the duty robot sends the week: trainings, by managers — the number, the average and the best score, frequent checklist misses, who did not train; to them — a reminder in one message. An interrupted one without a single answer does not go into the average score.
The ready bot "AI sales trainer" is built this way.

A VOICE REPORT FROM A SITE: A STORY → FIELDS, A PROBLEM TO THE HEAD AT ONCE (installation, service, cleaning, sales reps — a report after a site by voice):
  1) the report fields — the owner's list: field, hint (what to look for in the story), mandatory. Entry — "Team code" (an employee) and "Head code". Any employee text that is not a button and not a code is part of the report (data tx), a voice message — data vo;
  2) the draft — a personal variable: the parts of the story, the fields, the problem, the repeat, the seconds of recordings, the start and the last edit; it lives two hours from the last edit. Each new part is appended at the end, and the AI model lays out the WHOLE story anew — a later part corrects an earlier one;
  3) voice: the file intake (audio, then file) and «Аудио в текст» with your own RouterAI, AITunnel or Gemini key — in one scenario; no key — "write as text"; longer than "Seconds per recording" — shorter; the transcription seconds — into the day's count in the global "day:number", "Transcription minutes per day" ran out — as text;
  4) the AI model: JSON "field number → value, problem, repeat"; what is not in the story — an empty string, inventing is forbidden; "not specified", "—", "no problems" — empty. Did not answer or answered not in the format — the story is saved anyway, the fields are updated with the next part;
  5) the draft to the employee: fields, problem, repeat, "did not hear: …" for the mandatory ones; "✅ Send" is possible with empty ones too; "🔁 Again" erases the draft. Sending — a "Reports" row with one record: the fields in one cell "Field: value" separated by " | ", problem, repeat, status (submitted or "problem" if there is a problem or a repeat visit), seconds, story;
  6) a problem or a repeat visit — a message to the heads (except oneself) with the buttons "Report" and "Solved"; "Solved" — the status "solved", who solved it, and a message to the author by their messenger;
  7) the duty robot from the "Result hour" once a day — the result: reports, by employees, open issues, who submitted within two weeks but is silent today; without reports and open issues — silently; on Mondays — the week.
The ready bot "Voice reports of field employees" is built this way.

CUSTOMER INTERVIEWS BY A GUIDE: QUESTIONS ONE BY ONE, THE AI MODEL CLARIFIES GENERAL ANSWERS, A REPORT WITH TOPICS (customer development, customer research, a meaningful survey):
  1) the guide — the owner's list: number, question, goal (what we want to find out), clarify (yes / no), active. A respondent comes by the link t.me/<bot>?start=<marker> or max.ru/<bot>?start=<marker> — the start marker is only in the start message, "Parsing" clears it at once; the bot name — #{_botuser}. The researcher's entry — "Researcher code";
  2) the interview course — an "Interviews" row (number, status going / done / abandoned, the current question, the clarification and the first answer while waiting for the answer to the clarification, answers, voice seconds, the last answer, bonus, reminded) and a personal "waiting" marker for a day; text or a voice message with a live marker is an answer (data tx / vo), /start during an ongoing interview — continue from the same question or clarification;
  3) voice: the file intake (audio, then file) and «Аудио в текст» with your own RouterAI, AITunnel or Gemini key — in one scenario; no key — "answer with text"; limits — answer seconds and transcription minutes per day in the global "day:number";
  4) the AI model for each answer: JSON "enough, clarification, essence". Clarify only if the question allows it and it has not been clarified yet: one short question (why, how exactly, an example), without pressure and hints; "enough" or a clarification shorter than five characters — without clarification. The AI model is silent or the call limit ran out — the answer is recorded without clarification, the essence — in the respondent's words;
  5) an answer — one "Answers" row: the answer, the clarification, the answer to the clarification (skip — "(skipped)"), the essence, whether by voice; "⏭ Skip" without waiting — without a row. The last question — the interview is "done", a free promo code from "Promo codes" with the mark "Issued" (the interview number), thanks and the bonus text;
  6) the report: the question to the AI model — the essence of the answers by the included questions for the last 30 done ones, each answer with the respondent's number; the answer — JSON "topics (topic, frequency, quotes), unexpected, conclusions"; the frequency is not greater than the number of respondents, up to eight topics, up to two quotes — by the bot's count; a "Reports" row and the global "how many were done at the previous report";
  7) the duty robot every round: silent for a day — one reminder with a "Continue" button and the mark "Reminded"; three days — "abandoned"; done ones since the previous report not fewer than "Report every, interviews" — a report to the researchers; on Mondays — the week: started, done and the share, abandoned.
The ready bot "AI customer interviews" is built this way.

AN ORDER BY VOICE OR A PHOTO OF A LIST: ITEMS BY THE PRICE LIST, THE SUM — BY THE BOT'S CALCULATION (suppliers of cafes and shops, repeat orders from their own clients):
  1) the owner's lists: "Price list" (code, product, unit, price, other names, active) and "Clients" (number, name, entry code, discount 0–50%, active). A client enters with their own code from "Clients" — in "People" the role "client" and the client number; the manager and the warehouse — with codes from the settings. A stranger's text is a check of a client code;
  2) the draft — a personal variable: the items "code and quantity", the unclear, the source (voice, photo, text, repeat), the texts of the parts; it lives two hours from the last edit. Voice — the file intake (audio, photo, file — in a row) and «Аудио в текст» with your own key, then the AI model by the text; a photo — «Анализ изображения» by the link right away with the price list; everything — in one scenario;
  3) the AI model gets the price list (only active items, with other names), the client's previous order, the draft and the message and returns the whole order: JSON "items (code, quantity), unclear" (for a photo — also "read"). Codes not from the price list — into "unclear", zero quantities and repeats — by the bot's count; the sum and the discount — by the bot at the price list prices, not by the AI model;
  4) "Like last time" — a draft from the previous order without the AI model, prices — by the current price list; below the minimum order it cannot be confirmed; "Confirm" — an "Orders" row with one record (items "code:quantity" separated by ";", contents, sum, source, unclear, transcript) and a message to the managers with the button "Accepted";
  5) "Accepted" — only a manager: the status, who accepted, to the client "accepted", to the warehouse — the contents for picking;
  6) the duty robot every round: new orders older than "Accept within, minutes" — one reminder to the managers and the mark "Reminded"; from the result hour once a day — the day's result (orders, sum, by sources, not accepted), a day without orders and unaccepted ones — silently; on Mondays — the week.
The ready bot "Order by voice or a photo of a list" is built this way.

A CREW TRANSLATOR: A TASK IN EACH ONE'S LANGUAGE, "UNDERSTOOD", AN ANSWER IN RUSSIAN (construction sites, warehouses, cleaning, production with workers from different countries):
  1) the owner's "Languages" list: a Latin code, the language in Russian and captions in this language — the name, "Understood", "Passed on", a hint; up to eight are on. A worker enters with the "Crew code" and chooses the language with a button (data ln~code) — the language goes into their "People" row; the foreman — with the "Foreman code";
  2) the foreman's task — text or a voice message («Аудио в текст» with your own key in the same scenario); one AI call for all languages that have workers: JSON "important, translations by codes", numbers, sizes and names do not change; "important" — also by words ("stop", "dangerous", "forbidden", "urgent");
  3) one message sends one text to all recipients — so the messages go IN SLOTS: slot 0 in Russian (no language chosen or it is off), slots 1–8 by the enabled languages; each slot has its own recipients, its own text and its own "Understood" button caption as a variable (a button with the caption #{slot_un} and the data ok~number); an empty slot — the message is skipped;
  4) no translation or the limit ran out — it goes in Russian with a note; a "Tasks" row with one record: the text, the translations as JSON (under protection), important, workers, languages, failure;
  5) "Understood" — an "Understood" row (task, who, when); a repeated press — without a row; everyone understood — a message to the foremen. A worker's answer — a translation into Russian with one call, to the foremen — the translation and the original, an injury or a breakdown — with a note; to the worker — "passed on" in their language;
  6) the duty robot every round: an important one without "Understood" for longer than "Wait for confirmation, minutes" — a reminder to those who did not understand in the same slots in their languages (one task per round, the mark "Reminded"); twice as long — to the foremen "not confirmed: …" and the mark "Reported"; from the result hour — the day's result, on Mondays — the week.
The ready bot "AI crew translator" is built this way.

AN ACT AND PAYMENT BY VOICE ON SITE: THE MASTER DICTATES THE WORKS — AN ACT PDF BY THE PRICE LIST, "I ACCEPT" AND PAYMENT AT ONCE (plumbers, electricians, handymen, on-site IT):
  1) the "Price list" list: code, work or material, unit, price, other names; the master enters with the "Master code", the office — with the "Office code"; the act draft is a personal variable JSON (items by codes, own rows, unclear, customer, address, the texts of the parts), it lives several hours from the last edit;
  2) the master's voice message — «Аудио в текст» with your own key in the same scenario, text — at once; the AI model gets the price list, the current act and the message and returns the whole act as JSON "items (code, quantity), own (work, quantity, unit, price), customer, address, unclear"; codes not from the price list — into unclear; an own row — only if its price as a number is in the master's words (numbers from all parts of the draft: "1 500" is one number, "3 thousand" — 3000), otherwise "name the price in digits"; the sum is calculated by the bot;
  3) "The act is ready": the number after the last one (a global), a 4-character link key, an "Acts" row with one record with a snapshot of the rows and prices as JSON under protection; an answer to oneself "preparing the PDF", then "Document from template" with the PDF to oneself and only then the answer with buttons; the link to the client — t.me/<bot>?start=act<number><key> or max.ru by the master's messenger, the bot name — #{_botuser};
  4) the client by the link (the start parameter): the key is checked; "Client" (cid|mid|bid) and "Opened" — into the act, to the master "opened" once; the act as text, a PDF and the "I accept" button with a value that "Parsing" recognises for anyone; "I accept" — the status "accepted" and right away "Payments: getlink" for the act sum, the act number — in the payment data, the previous payment's link is cleared before the request; no link — "pay the master" and the details for a transfer;
  5) the "Payment received" block with payment acceptance: acceptpay, the status "paid" and the method "online" by an edit with a lock on the previous status, a message to the master and the office, a repeated callback — silently; cash and transfer — by the master's or the office's buttons; the master's own act — by the row's autofields;
  6) the duty robot from 9 to 21: the oldest unpaid act that the client opened and that is older than "Remind the client after, hours" — one reminder to the client ("Reminded" is written before sending); from the result hour — the day's result to the office (acts, paid by methods, waiting for payment, by masters), on Mondays — the week with the days until payment.
The ready bot "Act and payment by voice on site" is built this way.

A SPEECH AND PRESENTATION COACH: A VOICE MESSAGE — PACE, FILLER WORDS, STRUCTURE AND A SCORE, COMPARISON WITH THE PREVIOUS ATTEMPT (public speaking schools, sales trainers, experts before going on air):
  1) the "Tasks" list: number, task, kind, text, seconds (the target), criteria, active; the student chooses a task with a button (data tk~number), the current one — in their "Students" row; a task the coach turned off — as if not chosen;
  2) the student's voice message — a marker in "Parsing" and "Launch block" into "Answer": there the file intake (audio, then file) and «Анализ аудио» with your own key by the service from the setting; the question — the task, the criteria and the previous attempt at this task with its advice, the answer — JSON "transcript (verbatim, with fillers), structure, thought, pauses, confidence (score and comment), strong, improve (three), better_than_previous, score"; the model did not answer — «Аудио в текст» with the same key and parsing the transcript as text without pauses and confidence, with the line "the voice was not assessed";
  3) the pace and the filler words are counted by the bot, not by the AI model: words — by the verbatim transcript (in the fallback path — by «Аудио в текст»), the pace — by #{media_duration}, fillers — by a comma-separated list from a global: a phrase is counted whole and its words are not counted a second time, "uh-uh" — twice; per minute — by the duration;
  4) a limit of attempts per day — the counter "day:attempts" in the student's row, free and by subscription; an attempt is charged only for a parsed performance; shorter than 10 seconds and longer than the limit from the setting — not parsed; an attempt — an "Attempts" row with one record (words, pace, fillers, score, result, transcript under protection);
  5) the subscription: in Telegram — an invoice in stars with the marker rc:pd:<student number>, crediting by the payment event with a lock on the payment number; in MAX — getlink with the student number in the payment data, "Payment received" leads to the same crediting; renewal — from the end of the current subscription; /terms and /paysupport answer;
  6) the duty robot: a day before the end of the subscription — a reminder to the student with a renewal button by their messenger (the mark — the end day), on Mondays from 9:00 — to the coaches the group's past week: attempts, the average score, the growth from the first to the last attempt, fillers per minute.
The ready bot "AI speech and presentation coach" is built this way.

A BODY REPAIR ESTIMATE BY PHOTO: THE PART AND THE DAMAGE FROM THE SHOT, WORKS AND A RANGE BY THE PRICE LIST, BOOKING AN INSPECTION (body shops, paint shops, detailing):
  1) the client's photo — in "Parsing" without a button and a marker: "Launch block" into "Photo", there a picture intake, then a file, with keeping (keep) and «Анализ изображения» in one scenario; the estimate draft is a personal variable JSON (car, photos [{link, damage}], when), it lives three hours; text with the "photo" marker — the make and model into the draft;
  2) the question about the photo — only what is visible, the part and the kind of damage from the directories, the size, the confidence, the answer is JSON "visible, damage"; the answer is mapped to the directories by code, an unknown kind — out; not visible — "shoot closer", the photo does not count; photos per estimate — not more than the setting;
  3) "Calculate" — Response: the price list (code — work — for which kinds of damage — per part or per piece) and the damage by numbers, the answer is JSON "works: code, qty, d (damage number)"; a code not from the price list and a work not for its kind of damage — out, "per part" — once per part; the "from – to" range is calculated by the bot, a work with price 0 — "after the inspection"; the overall confidence — the lowest of the damages;
  4) the estimate — an "Estimates" row with one record (photos, damage and works — JSON under protection); "Book" — the marker "book" with the estimate number, the phone from the text as digits, the rest — "when convenient"; booking — by an edit with a lock on the status "estimate", to the masters — a card with photo links and "Take";
  5) "Take" — by an edit with a lock on the status "booking": to the first master — the client's phone, to the client — who took the request; to a second master — "already taken";
  6) the duty robot: an estimate without a booking older than the term — one reminder to the client from 9 to 21 (the mark is written before the message), a booking that nobody took older than the term — to the masters once more, on Mondays — the shop's week.
The ready bot "AI body repair estimate by photo" is built this way.

A SPARE PART BY PHOTO: WHAT PART IT IS, THE SAME ONE IN STOCK, A RESERVATION UNTIL THE EVENING, NOT FOUND — TO THE SELLER (plumbing, electrical, spare parts and tool shops):
  1) the buyer's photo — in "Parsing" without a button: "Launch block" into "Photo", there a picture intake, then a file, with keeping (keep) and «Анализ изображения» in one scenario; the photo caption — from _caption (the subscriber's answer is empty for a file), into the AI question — neutralised;
  2) the question about the photo — only what is visible, do not guess the article, the answer is JSON "visible, what, category, brand, model, size, marking, words, confidence"; not visible — "shoot closer", no selection is started;
  3) the selection by code from the "Products" list, the AI model does not choose items: the article or the model in full — the main score (case-insensitive, without spaces and dashes, similar Cyrillic — as Latin), then the brand, the category, the size (a different size — minus) and the words; available = stock minus live reservations; up to five items in stock as buttons "bk~selection number~item"; a "Selections" row with one record;
  4) a reservation — a "Reservations" row: a four-digit code for the till that is not among the codes of live reservations, the term — until the hour from the setting today, if it has passed — tomorrow (by the account zone); the same item a second time — "already set aside"; "Handed over" and "Remove" — by an edit with a lock on the status "reservation";
  5) not found — to the sellers a card with the photo link and "Reply": the marker "rep" with the selection number, the seller's next text — to the buyer in their messenger, the selection — "answered";
  6) the duty robot: reservations whose term has passed — "expired", a line to the buyer (up to three per round), from the result hour — the day's result once a day, on Mondays — the week.
The ready bot "AI spare part by photo" is built this way.

AN AGRONOMIST BY PHOTO: WHAT IT LOOKS LIKE, A PRODUCT FROM THE RANGE, A RESERVATION OR DELIVERY, A WEEK LATER "HOW IS THE PLANT?" (garden centres, agro shops, nurseries):
  1) the buyer's photo — in "Parsing" without a button: "Launch block" into "Photo", there a picture intake, then a file, with keeping (keep), reading the range and «Анализ изображения» in one scenario; the photo caption — from _caption;
  2) the question — only what is visible, "looks like", not a diagnosis: up to three options with a kind (pest, disease, nutrition, care) and confidence, care without products; the AI model does not name products and dosages; the question gets the problem names from the range's "Against" field, so that it names them the same way; the answer is JSON;
  3) the selection by code from the "Products" list: the problem as in "Against" in full — the main score, by a word — less, general words ("deficiency", "rot", "spotting") do not count; the kind must match; the "Plants" field — a product only for its own plants; options with low confidence give no products — the photo goes to an agronomist; available = stock minus live reservations; the dosage — from the list, with the note "check the instructions on the package";
  4) a reservation — a code for the till and a term until the hour from the setting; "Deliver" — the marker "adr" with the reservation number, the next text — the address: an edit of the reservation with a lock on "reservation" and "pickup", the term — a week;
  5) N days after the request the duty robot asks in the daytime "how is the plant?" with the buttons "better / the same / worse" (the mark "Asked" with a lock on "-" is written before the message), the answer — by an edit of "Result" with a lock on "-"; "the same" and "worse" — to the agronomists a card with the photo and "Reply";
  6) "waiting for an agronomist" in the menu reads the requests for a month: "worse" comes for a request older than a week.
The ready bot "AI garden centre agronomist" is built this way.

A STYLIST BY PHOTO: A LOOK FOR ONE'S OWN ITEM FROM STOCK IN THE SIZE, A FITTING WITH A CODE, AFTER THE PURCHASE — WHAT ELSE TO WEAR IT WITH (clothing and shoe shops, showrooms):
  1) a photo of an item — in "Parsing" without a button: "Launch block" into "Photo", there an intake with keeping (keep) and «Анализ изображения»: what the item is, category, colour, cut, style, JSON; a "Selections" row with the status "occasion" and the occasion buttons; two AI calls per selection, check the limit for two;
  2) the occasion and the size — by buttons with their own selection number; the occasion — by an edit with a lock on "occasion", the status "size"; sizes — from a global size grid, as in the catalog's "Sizes" field ("S:2; M:1");
  3) the size — candidates by code: catalog items in stock in this size minus live fittings, except the category of the item itself; shoes and accessories — without the buyer's size; Response — the item, the occasion, the size and lines "code | category | name | colour | style | price", the answer is JSON "looks: codes, why";
  4) checking — by code: only the sent codes, categories do not repeat in a look, up to four items, identical looks — out, up to three; candidates between steps — JSON in a variable; read "People" after the AI model, otherwise the read's parsing erases them before the needed step; not a single look — to the sellers a card with the photo and "Reply";
  5) a fitting — a row with the item codes, the size, a code for the till and a term; "Bought" writes the purchase time; the next morning, in the daytime, the duty robot sends "what else to wear it with" — by code: items of the same style of other categories in stock in the size, the mark "Advice" with a lock on "-".
The ready bot "AI clothing store stylist" is built this way.

A MYSTERY SHOPPER: A TASK BY LINK, A FORM BY A CHECKLIST WITH PHOTO AND VOICE, A CHECK AGAINST THE STANDARD, A SCORE BY CODE (chains of cafes, shops, salons, outlet checks):
  1) a task — a "Tasks" row from the chain, a link to the checker with the start marker "tpN" (t.me or max.ru by the chain's messenger); by the link — "take": an edit with a lock on "issued" and an empty checker, the checker — in three columns CClient, CMessenger, CBot; read their tasks by them;
  2) the form — the steps of the "Checklist" list (what, how: photo, voice, yes-no, standard, weight); after "Start" and each step — the "step" marker with the task number: the next photo, voice message or text in "Parsing" without a button goes to "Step"; "yes-no" — by buttons with the task number AND the step number, an old button of a previous step — "already behind";
  3) "Step" — a photo intake with keeping (keep), a voice message by link and a file with keeping in one scenario; a photo — «Анализ изображения» with the step's standard, JSON "visible, fulfilled, comment"; a voice message — «Аудио в текст» with your own key by the service, then Response with the standard, JSON "clear, fulfilled, comment, quote"; not visible or not understandable — ask again, the marker stays;
  4) the task, the step, the outlet, the checklist and the previous answers — JSON in a variable from the AI question to the record; an answer — an "Answers" row, the task — the next step by an edit with a lock on the current step and "taken"; the last one — the score by code (the weight of the fulfilled to the weight of all), "submitted", to the chain a report with "Accept" and "Do not accept"; the "do not accept" branch — only by its own button, not by "else";
  5) "Accept" — with a lock on "submitted", to the checker "accepted", to the managers of this outlet — a review with what was not fulfilled; "Paid" — with a lock on "accepted"; the duty robot: on the task day in the daytime one reminder (the mark "Reminded" with a lock on "-" before the message), the day passed — "overdue" and a message to the chain.
The ready bot "AI mystery shopper" is built this way.

A CATALOG BY PHOTO: A PRODUCT BY PHOTO AND VOICE, A CARD DRAFT, PUBLICATION INTO "PRODUCTS" AND A POST TO THE CHANNEL (small shops, craftsmen, an empty showcase):
  1) the seller's photo — in "Parsing" without a button: "Launch block" into "Photo", there an intake with keeping (keep) and «Анализ изображения»: the name, the section from the shop's sections or a new one, a description of what is visible, up to five characteristics — JSON; the AI model does not invent the price; the draft — with one record;
  2) the price and the stock — the photo caption (`_caption`) or the next voice and text by the marker "pr" with the draft number; tell voice by `_media_type` (voice, audio), a seller's photo — a new draft; first parsing by code ("690 ₽", "5 pcs": "\b" after Cyrillic does not work in JS — use "р(?![а-яё])"), did not work — Response "price, stock, edits";
  3) take the price from the AI answer only if its number is in the seller's words ("1 500" is one number), otherwise — "name it as a number"; the draft — by an edit with a lock on the previous status; edits — by the marker "ed";
  4) "Publish" — an edit of the draft with a lock on "ready", a "Products" row with an article by the product number (keep the number as a number), a post to the channel: `tgchannel` and `maxchannel` "message" with `messagemedia` — the link of the kept photo; the result — by `_group_message_id` and `_error`; a link to the product — a start marker, only in the channel of its own messenger;
  5) a card for the buyer — the photo as an attachment `[Files(link)]` in the exit text; an order — an "Orders" row, the stock — by an edit with a lock on the previous value (the engine cannot subtract), "Refuse" returns the stock; the duty robot — a draft without a price for longer than a day: one reminder in the daytime, a week — "deleted" with a lock on the previous status.
The ready bot "Store catalog by photo" is built this way.

REVIEWING CONVERSATIONS: CLIENT DIALOGS THROUGH THE BOT, A REVIEW BY A CHECKLIST WITH QUOTES, A SCORE AND A RATING OF MANAGERS (sales and support departments):
  1) a client's message — into "Messages" (dialog, who, text, when), a dialog — as a "Dialogs" row; a new one — to all managers with "Take" and "Not a client", "Take" — an edit with a lock on "new" and re-reading the row: who took it; the manager's address — three fields (client, messenger, bot), not a glued "Contact";
  2) the manager's answer — the marker "from" with the dialog number: the text goes to the client and into "Messages", the marker is set again — several messages in a row; there is nothing to answer the client — a silence marker and "Launch block" into "Parsing", which exits silently (the block itself cannot exit silently: the transition returns the event to "Parsing");
  3) "Close" — the outcome by a button, an edit with a lock on the previous status, then "Launch block" into "Review": the screen for the one who closed — from there (a message before a jump is eaten);
  4) the review — Response: the checklist and the conversation → JSON "items: fulfilled, quote, advice", the agreement, the result; an item counts only if the quote is the manager's words in a row in the conversation (compare by stem: case, "ё", punctuation); the score — the weight of the fulfilled to the weight of all — by code; the manager never answered — zero without the AI model; the review — as a JSON row with the item texts, the record — by an edit with a lock on "closed";
  5) the duty robot — the client wrote last and is waiting: a reminder to the managers, twice as long — to the head, the marks — by an edit by conditions; silence longer than N hours — "closed" by silence; closed ones — up to three per round in identical cells (the same code as in "Review"), after three AI failures — "not reviewed".
The ready bot "AI review of managers' conversations" is built this way.

AN UPSELL AFTER A PURCHASE: RULES "WHAT GOES WITH WHAT", AN OFFER TO THE CLIENT AT THE TERM, "TAKE" — A REQUEST TO THE MANAGER (shops, salons, services):
  1) a purchase — by an employee's line ("purchase +7… 2500 what" or the client number) and by the till's webhook ("Waiting for data": the request in _request, the answer in _response) with the same steps: the client by the phone key with a letter in front ("т79271112233" — without a letter the evaluator writes the number as a number), their offers, the rules, a record into "Purchases" and an edit of the card;
  2) the rules "What goes with what" — the words "Bought" separated by commas, a match by word stems (endings cut), all the stems of the phrase — in the purchase; one rule is not set again while the previous one waits for its term; new offers — with one record as arrays of one length (one record — as many rows), the term — the purchase date plus "After days", the client's address — three fields, not a glued "Contact";
  3) the duty robot in the sending hours: offers whose term has come — up to three per round in identical cells (the AI model → its text or the fallback from the rule → an edit with a lock on "waiting" → a message to the client with "Take", "Not needed", "Do not write", the offer number — in the button data); the daily limit and the AI calls — by the "Runs" log for today;
  4) "Take" and "Not needed" — an edit with a lock on "sent": a repeated press changes nothing; "Take" — a request to the managers with "Sold" (the sum — by a marker and the next text) and "Refused"; "Do not write" also removes the offers that wait for their term;
  5) buying the offered item within N days after sending — "sold" by itself (an edit with a lock on the previous status); the week to the head — offered, "Take", sold, upsell revenue, the share of clients with a repeat purchase.
The ready bot "AI upsell after a purchase" is built this way.

AN ORDER BY THE CLIENT'S CYCLE: THE ORDER RHYTHM, "REPEAT THE LAST ORDER?" AT THE TERM, "WE ARE LOSING THEM" TO THE MANAGER (wholesalers, B2B suppliers):
  1) an order from the client's draft, by a reminder, by a manager's line ("order 12 A-101 x 500" or a phone) and by the accounting webhook ("Waiting for data": the request in _request, the answer in _response) — the same steps: the client for themselves, by the phone key with a letter or by number, their orders and reminders, the price list, writing the order, the rhythm and the totals — by an edit of the card;
  2) the order contents — as text with a letter in front: 'с' + JSON {"п": [[article, quantity, price], …]}, on reading strip everything up to "{"; "Write to list" parses a JSON value: an array is laid out into several rows, an object is saved nested and read back as "[object Object]"; compare articles in uppercase, Cyrillic look-alikes (А, В, Е, К, М, Н, О, Р, С, Т, Х, У) — as Latin; round the quantity up to the pack, "not in stock" — do not include in the total;
  3) the rhythm — the median of the intervals between order days (the last 7, cancelled ones do not count, limits 3–120 days), from the second order; recalculation — on every order and cancellation, into the card's "Cycle" field — the round reads it (a numeric filter — as an interval: from 3 to 9999999999999);
  4) the duty robot: the term = the last order + the cycle; one reminder per cycle (a reminder later than the last order — do not write any more), up to three per round in identical cells: the client's previous order (reading the last row), price list changes by code, the AI model writes only the text, the bot appends the contents and the total at today's prices itself, the "Reminders" row — before the message; the buttons "Repeat", "Change", "Not now" — with the reminder number in data;
  5) an order within N days after a reminder — "ordered" by an edit with a lock on the reminder's previous status; silent for longer than two cycles — to the managers "we are losing them", one per round, a date mark in the card: a new order makes it old, and the order is marked "returned".
The ready bot "AI wholesale client order by cycle" is built this way.

COMPETITOR INTELLIGENCE: PAGES BY LINKS, WHAT DISAPPEARED AND WHAT APPEARED, THE ESSENCE FROM THE AI MODEL, AN ALARM ON THE IMPORTANT (shops, services, clinics, schools):
  1) a page — "Data exchange" GET with a browser User-Agent header; the answer — as a string (often in JSON quotes — parse it); on a server refusal the engine puts the BODY of the error page into the variable, and without an answer — the error text ("timeout…"): a short text without "<" — a request failure, a page with "404", "Bad Gateway", "Access denied", "not a robot" or a text five times shorter than before — a failure, not a change;
  2) the visible text by code: remove script, style, nav, header, footer, comments, tags; closing block tags — a line break; lines without letters and repeats — out; keep up to 6000 characters in the page row, through protection with the invisible character (quotes, "[", "#{");
  3) a text fingerprint (case, "ё", spaces do not count) with a letter in front; a new page — only remember it; the same — no changes; otherwise lines that are not in the other text — "disappeared" and "appeared" up to 1500 characters; only the order changed — not a change;
  4) the AI model gets only "disappeared" and "appeared" and answers JSON "essence, for_us, important, type"; look for the JSON in the answer by the curly braces; silent or the limit ran out — the essence from what appeared, "important" — by words (discount, sale, %, free, opening, new service, vacancy);
  5) the duty robot: from the check hour up to three pages per round in identical cells, each once a day (Checked — not today), the page row — by an edit by number, a change — as a new row, the important — to all employees; five failures in a row — "aside"; on Mondays — a digest.
The ready bot "AI intelligence: what changed at the competitors" is built this way.

A WEBSITE AND REQUEST WATCHDOG: AVAILABILITY, PHRASES ON THE PAGE, A TEST REQUEST THROUGH THE FORM (a small business with a website):
  1) the website — "Data exchange" GET with a browser User-Agent header; on a server refusal the engine puts the body of the error page into the variable, on a break — the error text: a short text without "<" — a failure, a page shorter than 600 characters with "502", "404", "Bad Gateway", "domain expired", "account blocked" — a failure, an empty visible text — a failure;
  2) the first failure — the status "doubt" without an alarm, the second in a row — "down" and an alarm from the start of the downtime (the time of the first failure), then without repeats; came back — "works again" and how long the downtime lasted;
  3) phrases that must be on the page — separated by commas in the website row; compare without case and "ё"; a phone — by the last 10 digits of all numbers on the page, so that brackets, spaces, "8" and "+7" do not interfere; a phrase disappeared — an alarm at once and once;
  4) a test request — "Data exchange" POST to the address of the form handler with the marker "Watchdog check №N" in a field; the form fields are given as a string "name=value; …" and are assembled into JSON by code; JSON — method "post-json", json: "#{body}"; an ordinary form — the same request, then the config's getdata.sendmethod = { label: "POST-data", value: "post-data" }: the engine parses the JSON and sends it as form fields (x-www-form-urlencoded); the form handler forwards requests to the bot scenario's webhook: with the marker — the test arrived, without the marker — a real request to employees; did not arrive within N minutes — by an edit "waiting" → "did not arrive" with a lock on "waiting" and an alarm;
