  5) the AI model only writes 1–2 phrases for an alarm: a likely reason and the first step; whether to raise the alarm is decided by code; silent or the limit ran out — an alarm without advice; up to three websites per round in identical cells, the ones not checked for the longest first; each check — as a "Checks" row, the log — only when there was something.
The ready bot "AI website and request watchdog" is built this way.

TEAM STATUS REPORTS: THREE QUESTIONS ON THE REPORT DAY, A SUMMARY TO THE HEAD, RECURRING OBSTACLES (team leads):
  1) report days — a global string ("fri", "mon, wed, fri", "mon-fri", "every day" — weekdays, "all days"), parsed by code into weekday numbers; the status term: today is a report day — today, otherwise the past report day if there is no status for it, otherwise the next one;
  2) the duty robot on the report day: from the survey hour — three questions to all employees without a status for today, once a day (the global "Survey: date"); after N hours and until the summary hour — a reminder to those who did not submit; from the summary hour after the survey — a summary to the heads;
  3) an employee's answer — text or a voice message («Аудио в текст» with your own key), in parts into a personal draft variable; the AI model lays out the whole story anew into JSON "done, plan, obstacle, risk"; what is not there — empty, "nothing is in the way" — empty; "✅ Send" — a row in "Reports" for the term, a second time for the same term — by an edit by number;
  4) a repeated obstacle — by code, without the AI model: word stems (the first five letters, words of four letters or more, without function words) of "obstacle" of this and the person's previous status, two in common — a repeat; a repeat — to the heads at once with a "Removed" button (an edit "Obstacle" = "removed" with a lock on "open"), in the previous status the obstacle — "carried over";
  5) the summary: who submitted and who did not, at risk, obstacles, by people — by code; the AI model writes only "the main thing" in 2–3 phrases by the statuses; silent — the summary without it. Parsing a read clears the read variables: the step after it must read again.
The ready bot "AI team status reports" is built this way.

TEAM PULSE: AN ANONYMOUS SURVEY, AN INDEX AND "WHY" TOPICS (companies of ten people and more):
  1) anonymity by design: an answer — "Write to list" into "Answers" with an explicit "-" in ClientID, Messenger, BotID and CreationDate (the engine fills autofields only if the field is empty); in the row — the month, the department, the question number, the score, "why"; there are no messages and no edits of "Answers";
  2) who answered — a month mark in one's own "People" row, without the answers themselves; one can answer once per survey; the answer draft — a personal variable of one's month, questions one by one: a score with buttons 1–10, "why" as text or "Skip";
  3) the answers with one record — as arrays by questions, "Write to list" makes a row from each;
  4) the duty robot once a month from the survey day and hour — an invitation to the employees; after N days — a reminder to those who did not answer; the term ended or everyone answered — the result to the heads and a "Results" row;
  5) the result by code: an index 0–100, the difference from the previous month, averages by questions; a department — from five respondents, the whole result — from three; the AI model only groups "why" into 2–6 topics with the tone, the number and quotes, the bot keeps only quotes that are verbatim in the answers (without case, "ё" and punctuation); silent — the result without topics.
The ready bot "Team pulse: an anonymous survey" is built this way.

A SHIFT SCHEDULE BY WISHES: WISHES IN WORDS, A SCHEDULE BY NORMS, SWAPPING SHIFTS, THE WEEK'S TABLE (cafes, shops, warehouses):
  1) the AI model only turns words into constraints as JSON (forbidden days and shifts, "only" by day, wanted shifts and days, a shift limit); shifts — from the "Shifts" list, names are matched by code; the bot shows "how I understood it", "✅ Right" — the week's "Wishes" row (the constraints — JSON with a letter in front), a second time — by editing one's own;
  2) the schedule is set by a formula, not by the AI model: days in order, shifts by start; allowed — whoever is not forbidden the day and the shift, "only" is respected, one shift a day, shifts and hours per week below the norms, rest between shifts not less than the norm, days in a row not more; the choice — first whoever wants this shift, whoever wants another one — last, then fewer hours, then in order; not enough — a shortage;
  3) the draft — as "Schedule" rows with one record as arrays with each employee's three fields (ClientID, Messenger, BotID from the data, not the "[ClientID]" of the presser); the week's previous draft is deleted; "Publish" — an edit of the status of the week's rows, a message to each employee;
  4) a swap: one's own shift ahead (the day in the button is continuous, 1–7 this week, 8–14 the next) → to whom it can be given by the norms and wishes of their week → the colleague "Accept" (a lock "waiting for the colleague") → the head "Approve" (a lock "waiting for the head"); before "Accept" and "Approve" — a check against the schedule: the shift is still with the giver, ahead and allowed for the colleague; approved — the schedule row is rewritten by the week, the day, the shift and the giver's three fields; statuses with "ё" — in a filter as written, in code compare them normalised on both sides;
  5) the week's table — a mini app with its own code, the data — by a scenario on a webhook: one's own person — by the subscriber from the quescha object, only what is published, names on the page — via textContent.
The ready bot "AI shift schedule by wishes" is built this way.

AN HR OFFICER: APPLICATIONS IN WORDS — LEAVE, A DAY OFF, UNPAID LEAVE, SICK LEAVE (companies without an HR officer):
  1) the AI model gets today's date with the weekday and answers JSON "kind, from, to, reason" with dates DD.MM.YYYY; the check — by code: the date is real, not in the past (sick leave — up to three days back, its "to" is empty), the end is not before the start, not longer than 60 days; a past date without a year — the next year;
  2) the bot shows "how I understood it" — the kind, the dates and days, the reason, the leave balance for the year (leaves "waiting" and "approved" by the start year), overlaps: other people of the same department in force with overlapping dates, ⚠️ from the limit; "Submit" — only after that; the draft — a personal variable for a day;
  3) "Submit" re-checks the date, writes an "Applications" row (status "waiting"), sends the heads a message with the buttons "Approve / Move / Refuse", as the last action before the answer — "Document from template" as PDF to oneself and to the heads: a tariff without documents cuts the scenario at it;
  4) the decision and the withdrawal — by an edit of the status with the lock "waiting" (a status with "ё" in a filter — as written, in code compare it normalised); a withdrawal — only of one's own, with a filter by one's own three fields;
  5) the department — by buttons from the "Departments" list, the position — as text by a personal waiting marker, the marker goes out on the very first answer.
The ready bot "AI HR officer: applications in words" is built this way.

A REGULATIONS COLLECTOR: AN EXPERT'S STORY BY VOICE → STEP-BY-STEP INSTRUCTIONS → APPROVAL (knowledge in people's heads):
  1) the draft — a personal variable JSON: the topic, the parts of the story, the assembled instructions, the number of the returned one; the first text is the topic, the next ones — parts; a voice message is received and transcribed («Аудио в текст» with your own key) by the same scenario that parses it: the file link lives minutes;
  2) the AI model gets the parts in order ("a later one refines an earlier one") and answers JSON "name, steps, warnings, questions": steps starting with a verb, up to 25; warnings and steps — only from the story; up to 5 questions about gaps; the expert's answers — as new parts, the assembly anew; parsing — by code: remove numbers at the start of a step, empty ones and non-strings — out; did not parse or the AI model is silent — the story and the previous assembly are intact;
  3) "For approval" — a row with the status "for approval", a message to the heads with the text and the buttons "Approve / Return"; "Return" sets the head a personal marker "waiting for a remark" with the number: the marker works only for the next message — any button or the menu clears it, otherwise their next text returns the regulation with someone else's remark;
  4) a returned one "Take into work" — a draft with the previous version and the remark as parts; a repeated submission — by an edit of the same row (the lock: the number, the status "returned" as written, one's own three fields) with version +1;
  5) approved — an edit of the status with the lock "for approval", a knowledge base row (Section, Text): delete a section with the same topic first;
  6) the month's result by the duty robot — with a month mark in a global; the first month after installation the bot did not see — only mark it, do not send.
The ready bot "AI regulations collector" is built this way.

CANDIDATE INTERVIEWS: A LINK FROM THE JOB AD → ANSWERS AS TEXT AND VOICE → ASSESSMENT → AN INVITATION TO A SLOT (a stream of candidates):
  1) the link `?start=v<vacancy number>`: the engine puts the parameter into "start" (Telegram and MAX), just in case — from "/start …"; in VK the vacancy — by a button; the vacancy's questions — in one field separated by ";", a vacancy without questions does not open;
  2) the form — a personal variable for a day: the name → questions one by one → about oneself → the phone (can be skipped); a voice message — «Аудио в текст» with your own key in the form's scenario, the name and the phone — only as text; while the form has been started, text and voice go into it, the head's commands from a candidate — into the menu, not into the answer;
  3) "Send" — the AI model assesses the answers against the requirements as JSON "score 1–10, strengths, risks, conclusion out of three, briefly", only by the answers, without gender and age; the row is written even when the AI model is silent or the limit is exhausted — with "-" in the assessment; for the same vacancy — once in 30 days;
  4) "Invite" — an edit of the status with the lock "waiting for a decision", to the candidate the free slots by buttons `sl~slot~candidate` (not earlier than in an hour, up to six); the choice — the slot is taken by an edit with the lock "Status != taken", the time — by an edit of one's own candidate row with the lock "invited";
  5) the duty robot: every round — a reminder to one candidate whose interview is within the next 3 hours (the nearest first), the mark "Reminded"; the morning and the month's result — to the heads.
The ready bot "AI candidate interviews" is built this way.

A MEETING TIME FOR EVERYONE: A MEETING IN WORDS → ANSWERS IN WORDS → INTERSECTIONS → VOTING (teams, clubs, chats):
  1) the meeting description — the AI model with today's date and weekday answers JSON "topic, minutes, from, to"; the check — by code: the duration 15–480, from not earlier than today, not longer than two weeks; the draft — a personal variable, "Create" — a row and an invitation link `?start=m<number>` (the username — `_botuser` of one's own messenger);
  2) a participant by the link writes when they can; the AI model answers with windows "day DD.MM, from, to" only from the meeting days; code merges overlapping windows; "Right" — an answer row, the windows as a string with a letter in front; the "writing an answer" marker — only for the next text, any press clears it;
  3) the calls ran out or the AI model is silent — code parses simple words: weekdays and "except", "today", "tomorrow", "the day after tomorrow", "from … to", "after", "before", "in the morning/afternoon/evening"; short "вс", "ср" (Sun, Wed) — only as a whole word ("всё время" — all the time — is not Sunday);
  4) the options — by a formula, not by the AI model: the start of a common time is always someone's window start, the starts are iterated, who can — whose window fits the whole meeting; better — more people, then earlier; not overlapping, up to three, not earlier than in an hour;
  5) only those with windows vote; "everyone has voted" is counted by them; the appointment — to everyone who answered; a reminder by the duty robot 2 hours before — one meeting per round.
The ready bot "When it suits everyone" is built this way.

HOURS BY PROJECTS: A QUESTION IN THE EVENING → A STORY ABOUT THE DAY → ROWS PROJECT · KIND · HOURS → THE WEEK AND A REPORT TO THE CLIENT (agencies, outsourcing, lawyers):
  1) the duty robot on working days from the "Question hour" sends the question to those who have no rows for today, after N hours — one reminder to the same people; the answer comes as ordinary text or a voice message, "Parsing" sends it into "Day";
  2) the day's story — a personal variable in parts (the day, the parts, the rows, the mark "saved"); after each part the AI model gets the whole story and the projects from the list and answers JSON {"rows": [{"project", "kind", "hours"}]}; a project — only by a name from the list, otherwise "?"; the code checks: hours 0.25–12 in steps of 0.25, up to 16 per day, the same project and kind — as one row; the calls ran out or the AI model is silent — parsing by words by code (hours, "half an hour", minutes, a project by the stem of the name);
  3) a voice message — receiving the link and «Аудио в текст» with your own key in the same scenario that transcribes: the link lives minutes;
  4) "Save" — first deleting this day's rows of one's own subscriber, then one listAdd record with JSON arrays in the fields (N rows at once); the draft stays with the mark "saved": an added part goes to the same story, the day is saved again whole;
  5) the week to the head — hours by projects against the budget by a formula; a report to the client — "Document from template" (PDF) by project and month: kinds of work, hours, the sum by the rate; the document — before the answer.
The ready bot "AI project hours tracking" is built this way.

DISH COST: A RECIPE OR PRICES IN WORDS → A RECIPE CARD → RECALCULATING THE MENU → BELOW THE MARKUP → A NEW PRICE (cafes, bakeries, food delivery):
  1) one AI question per kitchen message with the list of products (name, unit): the answer is JSON {"kind": "recipe", "dish", "portions", "price", "contents": [{"product", "quantity", "unit"}]} or {"kind": "prices", "prices": [{"product", "price", "unit"}]}; a product — by a name from the list if similar; units — g, kg, ml, l, pcs;
  2) the code checks the answer and calculates itself: grams and millilitres — into kg and l, the portion cost = Σ quantity × price ÷ portions, the markup = (price − cost) ÷ cost, the new price = cost × (1 + markup), up to the rounding step; a product is searched by the name in full, by the beginning and by the word stem; the calls ran out or the AI model is silent — parsing by words by code;
  3) "how I understood it" — a draft in a personal variable for a day: a recipe — rows with rubles and "no price" for a product without a price; prices — "was → now"; "Save" or "Apply" — only after confirmation;
  4) prices: known products — by number through IF and listEdit in several slots (the others — with the next message), new ones — with one listAdd record with JSON arrays; the dish cost is not written to a list — it is calculated by the current prices at every display;
  5) dishes whose cost grew and whose markup became lower than needed — a message to the owners with the buttons "Set N ₽" (st~number — listEdit of the dish price, only up); the duty robot once a day sends a summary only if the key "number:cost" of the dishes below the markup changed (the key — in a global), on Mondays — what became more expensive during the week.
The ready bot "AI dish cost (food cost)" is built this way.

MARKDOWN BY EXPIRY DATES: INTAKE FROM THE LABEL → A DISCOUNT BY A RULE → THE POST "DISCOUNTS TODAY" → WRITE-OFF (convenience stores, bakeries, pharmacies):
  1) intake: a label photo — an intake with keeping and «Анализ изображения» with today's date, the answer is JSON {"visible", "product", "best_before", "made", "shelf_life", "shelf_life_unit"} — the code calculates the expiry date itself; the photo caption (_caption) and lines "kefir 20 pcs until 18.10 89 ₽" — parsing by code (date, quantity, price); no price — the product's previous price from the list;
  2) "how I understood it" — a draft in a personal variable for a day; a line without a name completes the last one, the same product — an edit; "Accept" — the ready lines with one listAdd record with JSON arrays, unfinished ones stay;
  3) the markdown rule — the global "days:discount" separated by commas; the discount — the rule with the smallest number of days not less than the remaining days; the discounted price — down to the step; expired — to the write-off;
  4) the post: the AI model writes one phrase without digits (digits, percentages, links in the answer — the default phrase), the lines with prices are written by the code; the text is shared, without markup — the Telegram and MAX channels, the VK wall by data exchange (wall.post with the community key, error.error_msg and response.post_id — into variables), to subscribers from one's own list with a subscription; the VK key — only in the publication and check scenarios, cleared after the request; "publish at once" — no: a draft to the owners with "Publish";
  5) closing a batch — listEdit by number with the lock "Status = open": everything sold, write off everything or a part as a number by the "waiting" marker; the week — sold from the markdown and written off in rubles.
The ready bot "AI markdown by expiry dates" is built this way.

REPRICING BY THE EXCHANGE RATE: THE CENTRAL BANK RATE + A PRICE LIST FROM A GOOGLE SPREADSHEET → THE PURCHASE IN RUBLES → BELOW THE THRESHOLD → A DRAFT → "APPLY" (importers, electronics, spare parts):
  1) the rate — "Data exchange" GET https://www.cbr.ru/scripts/XML_daily.asp without a key: the answer is XML in windows-1251, Cyrillic gets corrupted, but <CharCode>, <Nominal>, <Value> (a comma) are read by code; the rate = Value / Nominal; the answer text — both XML and CSV — lands in the variable as a string in quotes: remove them with JSON.parse before parsing;
  2) the price list — a link to a Google spreadsheet is turned into a CSV export (`/export?format=csv&gid=N`, a published one — `/pub?output=csv`), "Data exchange" GET; the columns — by the header ("article", "price", "currency"); a sign-in page instead of CSV — "access closed";
  3) the purchase = price × rate × (1 + reserve) × (1 + overheads), the markup over the purchase, below the threshold — a new price up to the step, only higher than the current one; the pairing with products — by article without case and dashes;
  4) the AI model writes only a conclusion from the calculated numbers; a number that is not in the question — the default conclusion; the draft — a global as a string with a letter in front and protection against substitution;
  5) "Apply" — several IF slots + listEdit by article (price, previous, day), the remainder — back into the draft; buttons in a report to others — with constant values (the recipient's "Parsing" will not recognise computed ones).
The ready bot "AI repricing by exchange rate and supplier price list" is built this way.

A SALES ANALYST FROM A TABLE: A CSV EXPORT → THE PERIOD AGAINST THE PREVIOUS ONE, ABC, PAIRS IN A RECEIPT, SILENT ONES → THREE PIECES OF ADVICE → "DONE" AND THE RESULT A WEEK LATER (shops, cafes, online schools):
  1) the export — a link to a Google spreadsheet is turned into CSV, "Data exchange" GET; the answer lands as a string in quotes — JSON.parse before parsing; the columns by the header: "date", "product", "sum", optionally "receipt" (without it there are no pairs) and "cost" (margin);
  2) the code calculates everything: this period and the previous equal one, ABC by the cumulative revenue (A — up to 80%, B — up to 95%), pairs of products in one receipt from three receipts, the silent ones — there were three pieces or more, now not one;
  3) the AI model gets the already calculated numbers and answers JSON of up to three pieces of advice {conclusion, do: bundle | promotion | remove | other, products}; the products are checked against the list from the data — foreign ones out, advice without products out;
  4) the advice — with one record as arrays into a list with a number, "before" (the revenue of its products for the week) and the status "new"; "Done" and "We will not" — listEdit by number with the condition "Status = new", the second presser sees "already marked";
  5) a report once a week — the duty robot on the "Report day" from the "Report hour"; done advice — once, in the first report after a full seven days: the revenue of its products for the week before and after the mark.
The ready bot "AI sales analyst from a table" is built this way.

SHIFT ANOMALIES: THE SHIFT RESULT IN WORDS OR FROM A TABLE → SHARES AGAINST THE POINT'S MEDIAN → ODD — TO THE OWNER PERSONALLY (chains of cafes, shops, car washes):
  1) the cashier enters with their point's code from the "Points" list and writes the result in one message; parsing first by words (a field — by the first word of a piece, "к" and "тыс" — thousands, "… на 1200" — the number after "на"), did not work — by the AI model into JSON; a person's text — into a variable under protection, in code — varstore;
  2) a shift — a list row with the key "к" + day + point + employee (with a letter in front — the evaluator does not touch it); the same key on the same day — listEdit by the key, otherwise listAdd; write the day to the list as a number, without the invisible protection character — numeric filtering goes by it;
  3) the shares of refunds, discounts, cancellations and cash of the revenue — against this point's median for N days, from 4 shifts; odd — a difference both by the threshold in percentage points and by a multiple (cash — downward);
  4) the AI model writes only "what is odd and what to ask about" by the calculated shares; check the numbers of its answer against the question whole ("500" is not a piece of "84 500"), anything foreign — the default conclusion; the message — only to the owners, to the cashier — "recorded";
  5) a table of shifts — CSV once a day: new rows by the key with one record as arrays, normal — by the list and the new ones together; "did not submit yesterday" — points that submitted three days or more during the week.
The ready bot "AI shift anomalies" is built this way.

A TAX CALENDAR: A FORM BY BUTTONS → DEADLINES FROM THE ACCOUNTANT'S LIST → REMINDERS 7 AND 2 DAYS BEFORE WITH "DONE" → A QUESTION BY THE MEMO OR TO THE ACCOUNTANT (sole proprietors and LLCs, an outsourced accountant):
  1) the client answers by buttons — the regime, employees, a cash register — into their own "People" row; the accountant enters with a code from a global; the deadlines and rates are set by the accountant as a list and globals, the AI model does not invent them;
  2) a deadline fits a client if the deadline's "Regime" is "all" or their regime (several — separated by commas) and "Condition" is empty or matches (employees, cash register); the date in the list — DD.MM.YYYY, in code — YYYYMMDD for comparison;
  3) reminders — the duty robot once a day from the hour: deadlines exactly in 7 and in 2 days; put the recipients, the text and the data of the "Done" button with the deadline number into slots with literal names (np1_ … np5_) — one "Send message" per slot; the mark — a "Marks" row deadline + key, do not remind those who marked it;
  4) the simplified tax — by a formula from the revenue as a string (thousands separated by spaces, "mln", "thousand", a decimal comma): "income" — income × rate, "income minus expenses" — (income − expenses) × rate, not less than 1% of income; take the rate from a global within bounds;
  5) a question in words — the AI model only by the memo, otherwise "NOANSWER"; an answer with "NOANSWER", a link or a number not from the memo — do not send, a question to the accountant with the button "Answer №N"; keep the number of the question the accountant is answering in their "People" row (after /start personal variables are reset), the answer goes by the question key.
The ready bot "Tax calendar for sole proprietors and LLCs" is built this way.

NETWORKING: A FORM IN WORDS → PAIRS ONCE A WEEK IN CELLS → CONTACTS AFTER TWO "YES" → "DID YOU MEET? WAS IT USEFUL?" (clubs, communities, courses):
  1) the form — questions one by one (who, looking for, will help, contact); keep the question number in the "People" row as the field "Step", not in a personal variable — /start resets it; "Parsing" gives the text to the form until the step is "done";
  2) matching — the duty robot once a week: free participants (a finished form, not on pause, without a pair this week), longer without a pair — first; a cell of up to ten — to the AI model only "who, is looking for, can help", without the contact; former pairs of the cell — into the question as a ban;
  3) check the AI model's pairs: a number from the cell, each once, without former ones; "why" with a link, an email or a phone — by a template; whom it did not pair — complete by a formula by the similarity of the words of "is looking for" and "can help"; silent — pairs only by the formula;
  4) the letters — five slots per pair, a letter to each side about the other with the buttons "yes"/"no" and the pair number in data, the variable names — directly; the "Pairs" rows — with one record as arrays; free ones remain — the next round in a minute;
  5) each side writes the consent and the result into its own field of the pair (Yes1/Yes2, Result1/Result2) with the filter "field = -": simultaneous presses do not overwrite each other; the pair status — from both fields when reading; the contact — when both say "yes".
The ready bot "AI networking" is built this way.

A MARATHON: SIGN-UP WITH PAYMENT → THE TASK OF THE DAY → A REPORT BY PHOTO OR TEXT, CREDITING BY THE AI MODEL → A RATING → THE RESULT AND A CERTIFICATE (experts, schools, fitness clubs):
  1) a stream — by a start date in a global and a number of days; going — days 1…N, enrolment — the stream for which today is not later than the first day; a stream participant — a "People" row with this stream;
  2) payment: Telegram — an invoice in stars with the marker "stream~cid", the _starpay event — as the first branch of "Parsing"; MAX — a getlink link (clear the link before it), the callback — a block with acceptpayment; price 0 — without payment; all three paths — into one crediting block with a lock by the payment number in "Payments";
  3) the task of the day — with one message to all participants (recipients as an array); a report: a photo — «Анализ изображения» with the file address (media keep), text — Response; the AI model answers by the task and the criterion with JSON zachet/otzyv; did not answer or the calls ran out — credited without a check with a note;
  4) points — by the first credit of the day; the place — by points, then by days; in the evening — a reminder to those who have no credit for the day;
  5) the result of a finished stream — each their own text, five slots per round, the mark "Result" = the stream in the "People" row; a participant of the result is also someone who has already bought the next stream if they submitted reports in this one; a field you filter with "!=" next to ">="/"<=" — initialise with the word "нет" (no), not with a dash — a dash against a number does not match in such a filter.
The ready bot "AI marathon host" is built this way.

QUESTIONS FROM THE AUDIENCE: AN EVENT WITH A LINK AND A QR → QUESTIONS INTO THE BOT → THE AI MODEL MERGES SIMILAR ONES → VOTES → FROM THE STAGE OR AN ANSWER AFTERWARDS (conferences, webinars, meetings):
  1) an event — an "Events" row with a number and a link t.me/<bot>?start=evN or max.ru/<bot>?start=evN (the username — #{_botuser}); a participant enters by the link (the parameter — in the variable start), by a QR or writes the code as a word; their event — in their "People" row: /start resets personal variables;
  2) the question number — by time, nine digits, not by a global counter: the audience's questions come at once from different processes, and a counter would hand out identical numbers;
  3) a question's group is kept by a separate "Bindings" list (question → group): the duty robot once a minute gives the AI model up to 30 new questions and up to 40 groups of the event, the answer is JSON {"groups": [{"id": 12, "q": [101]}, {"id": "new", "main": "…", "q": [102]}]}; a group number — only from the list, a question — into one group, a wording with contacts — the question itself, whatever the AI model did not distribute or if it was silent — as its own group; new groups and bindings — with one record as arrays, do not give an empty array to a write;
  4) a vote — a "Votes" row (the group and the person's key), one per group, for one's own question — not allowed; the top — by votes, then by the number of questions in the group;
  5) "answer afterwards" — the group number in the "Waiting" field of the host's row; the host's next text goes to everyone who asked in the group in one message (recipients as an array), the group — "afterwards"; the QR is drawn by a mini app by the event link, its data — by a scenario on a webhook.
The ready bot "AI questions from the audience" is built this way.

WHO WILL HELP: A FORM IN WORDS → A REQUEST → THE AI MODEL CHOOSES HELPERS BY THE FORMS → "READY TO HELP?" → CONTACTS TO BOTH → "DID THEY HELP?" (communities, clubs):
  1) the form — who, how I can help, contact; the form step and "waiting for a request" — in the person's "People" row: /start resets personal variables; only someone whose form is ready can ask — the helper needs their contact;
  2) a request — a "Requests" row; candidates — finished forms not on pause, without the author and without those already asked; to the AI model — only "who, how they can help", without the contact; the answer is JSON {"helpers": [{"n": 1, "why": "…"}]}: a number — from the list and once, "why" with a contact — by a template; not JSON or the AI model is silent — matching by the common words of the request and the form; an empty list — "found nobody";
  3) the asks of one request — with one record as arrays; the request to each — a slot with buttons where the data holds both the request and the ask (hy~N~S);
  4) "yes" — to the helper the author's contact, to the author the helper's contact; the last refusal without a "yes" — to the author at once "nobody could" and "ask others" (without those already asked); silence for two days and "did they help?" three days after the first "yes" — by the duty robot.
The ready bot "AI "who will help"" is built this way.

AN INTERVIEW TRAINER: THE VACANCY TEXT → QUESTIONS BY THE AI MODEL → AN ANSWER AS TEXT OR VOICE → ASSESSMENT AND THE NEXT QUESTION → THE RESULT AND A REPEAT OF THE WEAK ONES → A PACK FOR STARS OR BY LINK (a paid product on the AI model):
  1) "waiting for a vacancy" and the number of the ongoing interview — in the candidate's row: /start resets personal variables; "Parsing" decides by them where the text goes — into the vacancy or into an answer; a voice message during an interview — a voice answer;
  2) questions — by the AI model, the answer is JSON {"questions": [{"q": "…", "type": "…", "expect": "…"}]}: without repeats, at least four; not JSON or the AI model is silent — fallback general questions; the interview's questions — one field as text with a letter in front ("в" + JSON), otherwise the write parses the array into rows;
  3) the answer assessment — JSON {"score": 7, "strong": "…", "weak": "…", "better": "…"}, a score 1–10 is mandatory; a voice message — «Анализ аудио» with your own key by the service, did not work — «Аудио в текст» with the same key and an assessment of the text with a note; the voice message link lives minutes — intake and parsing in one scenario;
  4) the interview step grows only for an assessed or skipped question; the result — the average score and the weak ones below the threshold; "repeat the weak ones" — a new interview from the previous questions, without the AI model and without a charge; available = free + bought − started;
  5) Telegram — an invoice in stars with the marker "candidate~pack", MAX and VK — a payment provider link with the same data in payloads; one record per payment — a lock by the payment number, a repeat — "already counted".
The ready bot "AI interview trainer" is built this way.

FAIRY TALES BY SUBSCRIPTION: A CHILD'S FORM → A CHAPTER OF A SERIES BY THE AI MODEL → A PICTURE BY A BUTTON → A REMINDER IN THE EVENING → A SUBSCRIPTION (paid content on the AI model):
  1) the form — several steps; the form step — in the family's row: /start resets personal variables; compare the step name as it is, without replacing "ё" — "ребёнок" (child) does not match after the replacement;
  2) a chapter — by the AI model by the form and the summary of the previous chapters; the answer is JSON {"title": "…", "text": "…", "summary": "…", "picture": "…"}: the text with length bounds, the summary of the whole story — into the family's row, for the next chapter; not JSON or the AI model is silent — "did not work out", not counted;
  3) access before the AI model: free ones or a subscription, a daily limit, today's calls; the last free one — say so at once and give the subscription button;
  4) a picture — as a separate step by a button: "Image" with your own key by the chapter's scene, the link — into the tale's row; there is a link — show it without drawing; the key — into a variable only for the time of drawing;
  5) the subscription: the marker "family~days", subscription until = max(now, until) + days; one record per payment; the duty robot from the hour by local time — to those who have not had a tale today.
The ready bot "AI fairy tales with illustrations" is built this way.

CHECKING WORK BY CRITERIA: TEXT OR A PHOTO → SCORES AND ERRORS BY THE AI MODEL → A CORRECTED FRAGMENT → THE WEEK TO THE PARENT → A PACK (paid checking on the AI model):
  1) the criteria — the owner's list (number, name, maximum, what to check); into the question — the criteria with numbers and maximum; a photo of handwriting — «Анализ изображения» by the file link (as a picture or a file with a picture extension), the link — only for the time of the call;
  2) the answer is JSON {"topic": "…", "scores": [{"n": 1, "score": 2, "comment": "…"}], "errors": [{"quote": "…", "fix": "…"}]}: a score — an integer within the maximum, a repeated number — the first one, fewer than half of the criteria assessed — "not parsed", the check is not charged;
  3) scores and errors — into the check row as JSON text with a letter in front, remove the letter on reading; what the student is waiting for (a composition, a fragment) and the check number for a fragment — in the student's row: /start resets personal variables;
  4) a corrected fragment — with a second call by the errors of this check: JSON {"fixed": [numbers], "left": [numbers], "comment": "…"}, the numbers — within bounds and without repeats; a check — only one's own (number and student); a fragment is not charged;
  5) a parent writes the student's code — the parent's address into the student's row, a message to the student; the duty robot on the report day from the hour by local time — the week to the parents (checks for 7 days, the average percentage, a week earlier), three per round, the week's mark — in the student's row;
  6) available = free + bought − done; a pack — the marker "student~pack", one record per payment.
The ready bot "AI essay and composition checking" is built this way.

CHECKING AN AD AND A REQUEST TO A SPECIALIST: THE TEXT AS A DRAFT → A PHOTO OR A SCREENSHOT → RISKS, QUESTIONS, DOCUMENTS → "NEED AN INSPECTION" → THE FIRST ONE TO TAKE IT (paid checking and requests to partners):
  1) the ad text — as a draft into the buyer's row (/start resets personal variables); a photo after it — «Анализ изображения» with the draft text; the photo caption — in #{_caption}, a long one is the ad text too; without a photo — a "check the text" button;
  2) the answer is JSON {"title": "…", "risk": "низкий | средний | высокий", "flags": [{"what": "…", "why": "…"}], "questions": ["…"], "documents": ["…"]}: a risk not one of the three or no questions — "not parsed", the check is not charged; the AI model does not name a market price — items with sums (rubles, thousands, millions; "тыс. км" — mileage) are removed by code;
  3) a specialist — by their own code (the kind in the "Specialists" row); a request — with one message to up to three specialists of the kind with recipient arrays, those who took fewer first; the buyer's contact in the offer is hidden (phone, @username, email), it opens for whoever first pressed "take"; to the others — "already taken by someone else"; no specialists — the request to the owner in full;
  4) the duty robot — to those who checked and did not order an inspection, once after the given hours and only in the hours window from the settings; the mark — the check number in the buyer's row;
  5) available = free + bought − done; a pack — the marker "buyer~pack", one record per payment.
The ready bot "AI ad check before buying" is built this way.

A GARDENER BY SUBSCRIPTION: THE PLOT POINT → A FORECAST BY DAYS WITHOUT A KEY → WORKS BY THE WEATHER BY CODE → A MORNING SUMMARY ON AN ALARM → A PLANT PHOTO → A SUBSCRIPTION (a paid service on weather and the AI model):
  1) the point — by location (client_latitude and client_longitude, at any moment) or by a settlement in words through the Open-Meteo place search (the name goes into the address as it is, without encodeURIComponent: the engine encodes the address itself, a second encoding searches for "%D0…"; remove "&", "#", "?", "+" from the name); latitude and longitude — as numbers and within bounds, otherwise it is not a point; into the gardener's row together with what grows;
  2) the forecast — Open-Meteo by days (daily: minimum, maximum, precipitation, wind; timezone=auto — the plot's dates), without a key; the works are calculated by code, not by the AI model: frosts from the threshold in the settings — cover the heat-loving plants from the gardener's list, heat, a downpour, dry weather in warmth — water, strong wind, a dry calm day — spray;
  3) the duty robot from the summary hour — over the plots with access, once a day (the day mark in the row), up to three plots per round: each — its own data exchange in its own slot; write only on an alarm or when the subscription ends within two days; the forecast did not come — do not mark, try again in the next round;
  4) a photo — «Анализ изображения» with the list of plants and the caption: JSON visible, plant, signs, up to three "looks like" with a kind and confidence, what to do — "this is not a diagnosis"; partners' products — by a match of the word stems of "against what", up to two link buttons as separate exits (0, 1 or 2 links);
  5) access = trial days from the first row or a subscription; renewal — from the end of the current subscription, otherwise from now; the marker "gardener~days", one record per payment; a payment with a former subscription — a renewal.
The ready bot "AI gardener by subscription" is built this way.

A REVIEW WITH ROUTING (a cafe, a salon, a car service, a shop). A rating 1–5 by jump buttons with the data "5~VISITCODE". Above the threshold — thanks and link buttons to the OUTLET'S card on Yandex Maps and 2GIS (links as a set: name and url — JSON arrays, an empty array — not a single button, there will be no broken link). Below the threshold — to the outlet's heads at once, to the client the reasons by buttons, a couple of words, the phone by a button; the head "Take" (an edit only from "new"), "Write to the client", "Solved" → the client confirms 👍/👎. THE MAPS LINK IS NOT CLOSED TO A DISSATISFIED CLIENT — show it on the thank-you-for-the-complaint screen too: hiding bad reviews from the maps is manipulation. The review link — with the start parameter "r-OUTLET-sEMPLOYEECODE": the rating is recorded for the employee. An employee does not see others' reviews with text. A question after a visit that an employee marked or the accounting system sent — through the duty robot, not in quiet hours and not more often than once in N days per person. The weekly summary: the share of answers, the average against the previous week, sent to the maps, the dissatisfied and what happened to them, reasons, outlets, employees, those hanging without an answer — by numbers; mark the visit rows with a week ("2026-37"), so that the summary reads three weeks, not the whole list.
The ready bot "Review after a visit: the satisfied to the maps, the dissatisfied to the head" is built this way.

A WHOLESALE PRICE LIST: PERSONAL TERMS (a warehouse, a distributor, a manufacturer).
  1) Registration by INN with a check of the control digits (10 — an organisation, 12 — a sole proprietor; ask for the KPP only for a 10-digit one). The fields one by one through the "waiting" marker, the draft — JSON in a variable. The "Send the application" button carries the draft number: an old button does not create a second row.
  2) The manager approves with a level from the "Levels" list (discount %, minimum order, deferral). COPY the terms into the client's row: the owner edits the discount for a specific client right there. An own price for an article — the "Personal prices" list by INN; it is more important than the discount.
  3) An order as a line "A-101 x 500, B-301 25": normalise the article (uppercase, Cyrillic look-alike → Latin, all dashes → "-", without spaces), round the quantity UP to the pack multiple, check the stock and say "15 in stock — the rest to order", name the shortfall to the minimum order as a sum. The price in a mini app is a hint: the sum is calculated by the scenario with the same formula, the price list to the app — by a scenario on a webhook, the client on the webhook — by the row's ClientID ("[ClientID]"), do not give out others' personal prices.
  4) "Check out" carries the draft number: the draft changed — "check and check out again". The order number — a global counter + 1 with a check that it is free (a fallback with digits).
  5) An invoice to a legal entity — "Document from template": the supplier's details as global variables, empty ones — as a dash (the engine skips an empty value, the placeholder stays), VAT "included": sum × rate / (100 + rate), the sum in words, a payment QR by GOST R 56042: "ST00012|Name=…|PersonalAcc=…|BankName=…|BIC=…|CorrespAcc=…|PayeeINN=…|KPP=…|Sum=<kopecks>|Purpose=…" (remove vertical bars from the values). The invoice was not created — the details as text to the client.
  6) Statuses new → paid → assembled → shipped / cancelled: buttons only for allowed transitions, the list edit is limited by the previous status. After "shipped" — "👍 / 👎" to the client, a problem — at once as a question to the manager.
  7) The client's order and question — to THEIR manager (who approved), if that one is in the managers list, otherwise to everyone.
  8) Stock from the accounting system by a webhook — no more than 10 items per request (the limit of 100 actions per run): read the current rows of these articles, keep a price that was not sent as before, return unknown ones in the answer.
  9) "Time to order" — a round on the CLIENT'S delayed launch by the average interval between their orders. Do not set this round on the manager: their delayed launch is taken by the duty robot.
The ready bot "Wholesale price list: personal prices, order by article and an invoice" is built this way.

STRETCH CEILINGS: THE PRICE BY ROOMS IN WORDS (and any calculation by rooms: renovation, floors, cleaning).
  1) The menu sets the "waiting for rooms" marker at once and shows an example: "Kitchen 3.5 by 4, 6 spotlights; Living room 18, a chandelier". The text calculation works in MAX too, where the bot has one app (it is taken by the measurement calendar); a calculator app — additionally in Telegram.
  2) Parsing by code: rooms — by line, separated by ";" or by a comma before a word that is not a calculation unit (light, chandelier, corner, pipe…). The size — the first "A by B" (x, х, ×, *) or the first number as the area; a side over 50 — centimetres; 0, a side over 30 m, an area over 300 m² — discard. Then "6 spotlights" and "spotlights 6"; a word without a number — one piece where natural (a chandelier, a pipe). Name unclear pieces to the client rather than silently discarding them.
  3) From an area alone the perimeter is calculated approximately (4·√S) — say so in the result. The membrane — by area, the profile — by perimeter, corners — beyond four, the minimum per room — as a price list row. The names of price list groups and options are formula keys: write "do not change the name" in the row's explanation.
  4) The result — a breakdown by rooms and a range ±N% rounded to hundreds, a disclaimer "the measurer will name the exact price". The texture and the profile — as a set of buttons with the option number in the data ("f~2"), recalculating the same rooms. The calculation input — JSON in a variable: it is written both by the text parsing and by the app (take only the sizes and the choice from the app, the scenario calculates the sum).
  5) Each new result — as a row into the "Calculations" list (do not write an identical one in a row): the weekly summary counts how many people calculated the price and how many of them reached a request within a week after the calculation. This is the main indicator of such a bot.
  6) The measurement — cal_record with checkdouble, checkfuture (no more than 2 scheduled) and two reminders; cancellation — cal_del_record by the booking code from the request row. Write the booking code only for a measurement request: for a photo and a question the variable may hold the code of a previous measurement.
The ready bot "Stretch ceilings: price by rooms and measurement by calendar" is built this way.

FURNITURE TO ORDER: A RANGE FOR ALL MATERIALS AT ONCE (kitchens, wardrobes, walk-in closets; the same — any price where the material changes the sum many times over).
  1) Do not make a "choose the material" screen: calculate and show the range at once for each material as a line ("• Chipboard — 169–229 thousand ₽", "• Enamel — 287–390 thousand ₽"). The client sees the spread and chooses themselves, one screen fewer to the goal. A material in the price list — as a coefficient to the price per running metre.
  2) Sizes as text: a kitchen — up to three walls (a wall beyond the first — a corner module), a wardrobe — width and height. Metres, centimetres and millimetres mixed: over 1000 — mm, over 30 — cm. Remove "3 walls" and "depth 60" before collecting numbers, take "height 2.7" by the word.
  3) The product can be named in the text ("a corner kitchen 3.2 and 1.8") — recognise it by the stem of the first word of the name from the price list. Sizes without a product are not a refusal: save them as a draft and show the products as buttons with the data "i~N~d"; only a button with "~d" takes the draft, an ordinary "i~N" does not (otherwise an old draft pops up a week later).
  4) The budget — by sum buttons and in words ("300 thousand", "1.2 mln", a bare "450" — thousands). Compare honestly with three outcomes: the "to" of the range within the budget — fits, the "from" within the budget — on the edge, otherwise more expensive. The budget and what fits into it — into the brief for the designer.
  5) The range does not go below the minimum order: "from" = max(range, minimum).
  6) The main metric — how long the client waits for a meeting. Write into the request row the time "Taken" when taken into work and the visit time; the summary calculates the average from the request to the visit and to "taken", against the previous week. The statuses Contract/Refusal from "In progress", "Installed" only from "Contract" — a list edit with a condition on the previous status; ask for a rating after the installation, not after the contract.
The ready bot "Furniture to order: a range by fronts and a designer's visit" is built this way.

PRINTING: AN EXACT PRICE, THE LAYOUT AS A FILE, PAYMENT (a print shop and any order "the price is known in advance + a file from the client").
  1) Do not hide the exact price behind a form: the product by a button — and at once the order screen with the price and the term. The parameters (format, run, paper, sides, term, finishing) change by buttons, and in the list of options each has its own price: "500 pcs — 2 100 ₽". Show a parameter's button only if there is more than one option. The price list — a row "product + format + run" with the price of the whole run; options — as a separate list: a coefficient "×", a surcharge "%", a sum "₽", the column "For product" separated by commas. The run — only from the price list: what is not there takes the nearest larger one.
  2) The term — in days from the price list or the urgency: after the setting "urgent until, hour" — from the next day, days off from the setting are not counted.
  3) The layout — as a file by the "waiting" marker (#{_fileId} and #{_media_type}: document or photo; video and voice are not a layout, for an album — the first file) or a disk link as text (cut punctuation after the link). A file sent without an order — remember it as a draft for 12 hours and offer it with a button at checkout.
  4) A File ID belongs to the bot and the messenger. Send to employees in two passes: those with the same pair "messenger + bot" as the file — with the file (sendtofileid), the others — as text with the note "the file was sent in another messenger". Accept a file from an employee to a client (a designer's layout) as a file only if the bot is the same, otherwise ask for a link.
  5) Create the order with the status "Waiting for payment" at the moment there is a layout or a brief. The link — qb.cfg.payment("getlink") with the order number in payloads. An empty link (the payment provider is not connected) is not a dead end: the order goes into work with payment on receipt. Payment acceptance edits the status only from "Waiting for payment" — a repeated notification does not send the order into work twice.
  6) No layout — an order with an extra charge for design and a brief. The designer sends the layout for approval, the client answers "All correct — to print" (recalculate the term from this minute) or "Edits needed" (the text by the marker — back to the designer). An unsuitable layout is returned by an employee with a reason by a button or in words, the client sends a new one to the same order.
  7) A button without an order number (a general question) carries its own data, for example "q", not empty: [Data1] is not cleared, and the question would attach to the previous order.
  8) The metric — orders without a call: count "Questions" on the order (the client's questions and the employee's messages about it), in the summary — the share of orders where it is zero.
The ready bot "Printing: an exact price, the layout as a file, payment and status" is built this way.

CATERING AND BANQUETS: A PACKAGE PRICE PER GUEST, A DATE WITH THE DAY'S LOAD, PREPAYMENT (catering, on-site events; the same — any date reservation where the price depends on the number of people).
  1) Parse a free request by code before the buttons: "a buffet for 40 people on October 17 at 18" — the format by the stem of the first word of a name from the list, the date ("17.10", "October 17", "tomorrow"; a past one without a year — of the next year), the time only with ":" or after "at"/"by" ("with 20 guests" is not a time), guests — a number, for a range "40-50" — the larger. No format — save the guests and the date as a draft and show the formats as buttons with "~d".
  2) The price = the package price per guest × max(guests, the package minimum) + options (the unit "per guest", "per event" or "waiter·hour": waiters = guests / "guests per waiter" up, hours — from the package) + the trip, free from a sum. Fewer than the minimum — calculate the minimum and say so. Prepayment — a percentage of the total up to tens.
  3) Only prepayment holds the date: the day's load — by the statuses "with prepayment". Check the date three times: on choosing, at the start of the reservation and when creating the request — while the client was writing the address, someone else may have taken the date. Name the reason for a refusal (passed, we will not make it within the preparation time, far, taken) and offer the nearest free dates; weekends — as buttons with the date in the data.
  4) The request "Waiting for prepayment" → the link qb.cfg.payment("getlink") with "NUMBER~pre" in payloads; the rest — "NUMBER~rest". Payment acceptance edits the prepayment only from "Waiting for prepayment", the rest — only from "Payment: prepayment". An empty link — the manager marks the prepayment with a button. A prepayment came but the day is already full — accept it and warn the employees.
  5) Keep the menu from an employee as a draft WITH THEM (request number, file or text, time) until "Send to the client": the button checks the draft number against the number in the data and the freshness. A new estimate total — as text by a marker, show the client "was". Write an edit of a row from several allowed statuses with conditions "Status != …" joined with "and" — do not mix "and" with "or" in one filter.
  6) Variables read by steps (a webhook, button sets in a mailing) — assign them with explicit names — varstore['p1_blocks'], not varstore['p' + i + '_blocks']: the dry run does not see a computed key, and nobody catches an error in them.
  7) The metric — prepayments from calculations: each new total — as a row into "Calculations", in the summary — the share of people who made a prepayment within a week after the calculation.
The ready bot "Catering and banquets: menu packages, prepayment and menu approval" is built this way.

A SHOWCASE SHOP: AN OWN MINI APP INSTEAD OF A CATALOG, THE PRODUCT OF THE DAY (a grocery, a coffee shop, a small store; the same — any showcase that sells with promotions and discounts, not just a list of products).
  1) The showcase — webapp type "miniapp" with its own code, not "cat": the standard catalog cannot do banners, discounts and "until free delivery". Keep products and promotions AS LISTS ("Products": article, name, category, price, old price, picture as a link, description, packing, label, show; "Products of the day": title, caption, picture, article, promotion price, show from, show until, show) — the owner edits them without code. The data is given to the app by a scenario on a webhook ({{webhook:Name}}): products, today's promotions, settings, whether the shop is open, the client's previous order; do not give the address and the phone to the app.
  2) Banners change by a timer from a global setting, are swiped by a finger and by dots; one banner — without a timer. A product of the day from the list is valid on its dates, "Promotion price" — only if lower than the price. The list is empty or nothing is valid — choose from the catalog: by a score of the discount (the old price against the new one) and of sales for 30 days (cancelled and unpaid ones do not count), take three times more candidates than to show, and go round by the day number — different ones every day.
  3) Prices with promotions and the order sum — with ONE formula in the app and in every scenario (the assembly code substitutes the text of the functions). The app sends only the items through quescha.submit; the bot recalculates the sum, the delivery with the free threshold and the minimum itself. Each JS step where you call a formula function must carry its text: without it the step fails silently, and the dry run does not see it.
  4) Checkout — as a draft with a number: the router asks only for what is missing (the method, the address for delivery, the phone by a button, payment), a single enabled option is chosen by itself. Choice buttons and "Confirm" — jumps with the data "kind~value~draft number": [Data1] is not cleared, so apply a press only if the number is one's own and it has not been applied yet; erase the draft right after writing the order — an old "Confirm" does not create a second one. Check the opening hours at checkout too.
  5) Online — the order "Waiting for payment", a link with the number in payloads; an empty link — the order "New" with payment on receipt. Payment acceptance handles crossing cases: it was waiting — into work; the client already switched to "on receipt" — mark the payment and warn "do not take money"; the order is cancelled — "refund the money"; "Paid: yes" — a repeated notification, touch nothing.
  6) The employee's statuses — jumps with the data "st~number~code" and a table of allowed ones: New → Being assembled → On the way (delivery) or Ready for pickup (pickup) → Completed; cancellation — from "New" and "Being assembled". Edit with the condition "Status = previous", re-read and check "Employee ID" — a second employee learns that the order has already been taken. To the client — every status, after "Completed" — a rating with the order number.
The ready bot "Showcase shop: product of the day, promotion banners and delivery" is built this way.

EQUIPMENT SELECTION AND BRINGING THE CLIENT BACK A YEAR LATER FOR SERVICING (air conditioners, boilers, water heaters, water filters; the same — any sale with installation where maintenance, cleaning or a cartridge replacement is needed a year later).
  1) SELECTION — one screen, not a form. The client writes the main parameter in words ("20", "4 by 5", "350x400" — over 50 counts as centimetres), the rest (height, sun, wall, floor, budget) changes BY BUTTONS right under the calculation: each press recalculates the same screen. Keep the models and the works in TWO lists ("Models": model, series, power, price, show; "Installation": work, option, price, explanation — the groups "Installation" by power class "up to N kW", "Wall", "Floor", "Maintenance"), show the price AT ONCE TURNKEY: the equipment plus installation.
  2) Select not weaker than needed and without an excessive reserve (not more powerful than 1.6 of the need), but always show the smallest suitable one; none is strong enough — say so and call a master. The budget does not hide expensive models: sort "within the budget, then cheaper" and write "2 of 3 fit". Write the calculation as a row into the "Calculations" list — from it the share of those who calculated and reached a request is counted; do not write an identical one in a row.
  3) Selection buttons — jumps with the data "kind~value~SELECTION ID": [Data1] is not cleared, so a new area creates a new id, and apply a press only if the id is one's own and it has not been applied yet. An option that is no longer in the price list (the owner edited the list) — "choose again", not a silent error.
  4) A VISIT — one calendar for installation and for maintenance: cal_record with checkdouble, checkfuture and two reminders. For installation ask for the address, for maintenance take it from the installation — the client is not asked twice.
  5) THE MAIN THING: a closed installation request CREATES A ROW IN THE "Installations" LIST (the client's contact, the model, the address, "Installed", "Maintenance until" = the date plus "Maintenance after, months", "Maintenance times", "Maintenance reminded"). A closed maintenance moves "Maintenance until", increments "Maintenance times" and clears "Maintenance reminded". When adding months, lower the 31st in a short month to the last day.
  6) Call for maintenance a year later BY THE DUTY ROBOT'S ROUND, not by a delayed launch for a year: such a launch is lost during server downtime, while the term lies as a row and is checked every 15 minutes. Call when "Maintenance until" minus "remind before, days" has already come, and exactly once per term: write THE TERM ITSELF into "Maintenance reminded" — after maintenance the term is new, and an invitation is possible again. Set the mark's edit with the condition "Maintenance reminded = the previous value" and before sending the letter.
  7) The summary metric here is not "how many requests" but REPEAT MAINTENANCE: among installations whose first maintenance term has come, how many have attended it at least once. Also in the summary: calculated a selection and reached installation, installed and maintained, how many have the term passed without a booking.
The ready bot "Air conditioners: selection, installation and maintenance a year later" is built this way.

A SET AS A SPECIFICATION AND A CATCH-UP LETTER FOR AN ABANDONED CALCULATION (video surveillance, intercoms, alarms, smart home, networks; the same — any sale where not one item is calculated but a set for an object).
  1) THE ESTIMATE IS A SPECIFICATION, not "from N ₽": as lines "name × quantity — sum", with the total "equipment + works = turnkey". Keep the equipment in ONE list with the columns Group, Name, Where, Parameter, Price, Show: "Parameter" holds what you select by (megapixels, channels, terabytes, how much a power supply can carry), "Where" — outdoors or a room. Works — in a second list (Work, Option, Price, Explanation). "Show: no" removes an item from the selection without deleting the row.
  2) CALCULATE THE MAIN NUMBER HONESTLY. The archive volume = the bitrate by resolution (2 MP — 4, 5 MP — 8, 8 MP — 12 Mbit/s) × 24 hours × days × cameras, motion recording — about 40% of the time. Take a disk not smaller than calculated; the biggest one was not enough — DO NOT KEEP SILENT: write how many days of recording it is enough for and what to change (fewer cameras, a lower resolution, motion recording). It does not fit into one recorder — say that an engineer calculates the set.
  3) Select dependent items minimally sufficient: the recorder — by the number of channels not fewer than the cameras, the power supply — the minimal one that can carry it (not enough — several), the cable — by the metres per unit from the settings, surcharges for the installation place — from the works list.
  4) Parameters — AS BUTTONS UNDER THE ESTIMATE, with the data "g~group~CALCULATION ID" and "v~group~index~ID": [Data1] is not cleared, so a new text parsing creates a new id, and apply a press only if the id is one's own and it has not been applied yet. In the button name write what the parameter gives the person ("2 MP — the car number at the gate"), not only a figure.
  5) WRITE EACH CALCULATION AS A ROW into the "Calculations" list (Number = the calculation id, Cameras, Sum, Set, Invited, Request, Week, Created plus the autofields ClientID, Messenger, BotID — the row is written by the client themselves); do not write an identical one in a row. Booked a visit — put the REQUEST NUMBER into the calculation row: the main metric "requests before the visit" is counted from it, and the client drops out of the catch-up list.
  6) CATCH UP AN ABANDONED CALCULATION by the duty robot's round: it has cooled down for longer than "Catch up a calculation after, hours", there is no request, "Invited" is empty — one letter with THEIR OWN set and a "Choose a time" button. Set the "Invited" mark BY THE CLIENT'S CONTACT (a person may have several calculations, and they must get one letter) and BEFORE sending. In the formula itself collapse the list by contact, taking the freshest calculation.
  7) The service input fields (the calculation id, "pressed") are NOT CARRIED OVER by the parameter normalisation function — when you write a request, take the id from the raw input, not from the normalised one: otherwise the calculation number goes out empty and the metric always shows zero.
The ready bot "Video surveillance and intercoms: an estimate before a call and an engineer's visit" is built this way.

SEASONAL WORKS: AN HONEST "IT IS NOT THE SEASON NOW" AND A WAITING LIST (landscaping, roofing, facades, pools, heating, air conditioners — everything that is not done all year round).
  1) EACH WORK IN THE PRICE LIST HAS ITS OWN MONTHS — the "Season" column: "04-10", two windows "04-06,09-10", all year round "01-12", a window across the winter "11-03" (works on frozen ground). Split the estimate into "we do now" and "not the season", and for the second NAME THE START DATE in words ("we start on April 1"), not "in spring". Promising to sow a lawn in December is forbidden, booking a measurement in advance is allowed, and this is an honest argument: "in spring the queue is longer".
  2) CALCULATE THE VOLUME OF WORKS FROM ONE NUMBER the client knows (the plot area, the roof area), by the "Norm" column: for works in m² it is the share of the plot in percent (a lawn on 60%, paths on 15% — not on the whole plot!), for pieces — how many m² one piece covers, running metres — the perimeter (a rectangle: 4 × the square root of the area). Otherwise the estimate comes out fantastic and the client leaves.
  3) Read a number without a unit in the client's units: on a plot "12" means twelve HUNDRED SQUARE METRES (сотки), not metres. Recognise works by the names FROM THE PRICE LIST ITSELF (the first word of the name, 4–5 letters): the owner wrote their own works — parse by them, and take ONE work per word stem, otherwise "lawn" gives both the seeded and the rolled one at once.
  4) THE PRICE IS A RANGE "from … to …" and a disclaimer of what it depends on. Toggle works by buttons right under the estimate (a tick — on, a circle — off), the button data — "v~w~index~CALCULATION ID": [Data1] is not cleared, so apply a press only to one's own calculation.
  5) THE ESTIMATE AS A FILE — the "Document from template" action: the works table by a loop {#items}…{/items}, the total, a line about the season, the validity term. Put the template into the archive (upload/) and let the owner replace it with their own form with the same placeholders. Put the link to the finished file into ITS OWN field of the request: the client may not have asked for the file, and the dry run rightly complains about a variable that is filled not on all paths.
  6) THE SEASON WAITING LIST is a reason to return that a mailing does not replace: keep THE SEASON START DATE ("Season from") in the calculation row, and when it has come, the duty robot writes ONCE with this client's estimate. Set the "Called" mark by the client's CONTACT (they may have several calculations) and BEFORE sending; booked a measurement — put the request number into the calculation, and the client drops out of the list.
  7) THE SUMMARY METRIC — "visits in season": keep the flag "In season" in the request (at least one work of the estimate can be done now) and count the share of such measurements. Plus: how many calculated an estimate, how many reached a measurement, how many are waiting for their season.
The ready bot "Landscaping: an estimate with the season and a measurement by calendar" is built this way.

SELECTION BY A BRIEF: A GUIDE INSTEAD OF A PRICE, THE ANSWER DEADLINE AND A RESERVATION PREPAYMENT (a travel agency; the same — selecting a flat, a car, property abroad, a wedding contractor — everything where a person names the price after a selection, and the bot collects the request).
  1) A BRIEF FROM ONE MESSAGE: "Turkey in July, two adults and a child of 5, up to 300 thousand, departure from Kazan". Parse it in parts and add to the previous brief rather than erasing it. Look for the month and the destination FROM THE START OF A WORD and take THE FIRST BY TEXT ("Морокко" is not March, "мальчик" (boy) is not the Maldives). Recognise a destination by ANY word of the name from the price list, after the stem allow only a short ending ("Кубань" is not Cuba). Cut out the children first ("two children" are not two adults), do not take a number without a unit below a threshold as the budget, the departure city — with a capital letter after "from", a second word — only for "Nizhny Novgorod".
  2) AN EMPTY FIELD IS "NOT CHOSEN", not zero: destination number 0 is the first row of the price list, and an empty brief would turn into a request for it.
  3) DO NOT PROMISE A PRICE IF IT IS LIVE. Show a GUIDE from the price list ("from N ₽ per week for two", recalculated for the party) and say plainly that the manager will name the exact price. Next to it — the destination's season in this month (windows "04-10", across the winter "10-04"), a visa, the flight and advice: other destinations in season and within the budget, the cheap ones first.
  4) CHECK WHAT WILL BREAK THE DEAL LATER: the passport validity by the six-month rule from the travel month (no month chosen — the trip is soon). Warn the client at once and show it to the manager in large letters in the request.
  5) AN INCOMPLETE BRIEF WITH TEXT IS A QUESTION, not a dead end: the buttons "Question to the manager" and "Pass to the manager" write the text into the brief, and without the mandatory fields such a request goes out with the kind "Question". The manager answers with text, the request gets the status "Answer". A message about an ALREADY SENT request ("Other options", "Write about №") — a button with the number "o~number": the text lands in its "Comment" and goes to the request's manager. The brief is erased after sending — writing there is impossible.
  6) [Data1] IS NOT CLEARED: if one handler accepts both buttons and text by a marker, check the text FIRST (it came from the parsing), then the button — otherwise after "Wishes" the typed text opens the question again.
  7) A SELECTION — one manager message by the marker "selection~number", to the client — with the buttons "I take the tour" (t~number) and "Other options". Edit the status by the previous value; set THE TIME OF THE FIRST SELECTION only into an empty field — a new selection does not overwrite it. "I take the tour" — a reservation from the status "Selection", and WRITE THE PREPAYMENT SUM INTO THE RESERVATION: calculate the link and the payment acceptance from it, not from the setting the owner may have changed. A repeated callback does not pass the edit "Paid = empty" — do not write to the manager a second time.
  8) THE ANSWER DEADLINE IS GUARDED BY THE DUTY ROBOT: on taking, write the time "Taken"; a taken request without a selection for longer than "Selection within, min" — a PERSONAL letter to the request's manager once (the mark "Overdue" before the letter). Do not write the filter "mine AND (in progress OR reservation)": the engine glues "and"/"or" without brackets — filter by the manager, filter the statuses by code.
  9) THE METRIC — THE TIME TO THE FIRST SELECTION for selection requests (questions do not go into it), in the summary — against the previous week. Plus: requests, questions, waiting for a selection now, reservations and paid prepayments.
The ready bot "Travel agency: a brief in a minute, a selection and a prepayment" is built this way.

RENEWALS AND DEADLINES: A REGISTER, A REMINDER IN TWO PASSES AND DOCUMENTS AS PHOTOS (an insurance agent; the same — a vehicle inspection, a membership, meter verification, a domain and hosting, a service contract, a lease — everything that ends on a known day).
  1) THE MAIN NODE IS A REGISTER WITH THE END DATE, not requests. Issued — write a row: the client's contact, what exactly, the object, "Valid until" (the start plus "Term, months" from the price list; 0 months — a one-off service, there is nothing to renew), how many days before to remind, "Reminded", "Renewed", the source. LET THE CLIENT ADD SOMEONE ELSE'S DEADLINE TOO ("compulsory car insurance for the Solaris until 15.11"): a reminder about someone else's policy is a legitimate reason to come to you for a renewal.
  2) REMIND IN TWO PASSES AND DO NOT NAG: "Remind before, days" — calmly, "Second time before, days" — insistently and with a phone. Keep in the "Reminded" field THE DEADLINE ITSELF WITH THE PASS NUMBER ("15.11.2026·1"), not "yes": renewed — the deadline is new, and the right to a reminder returns by itself. Edit the row BY THE PREVIOUS VALUE of this field and BEFORE sending — two rounds in a row do not write twice. Separately guard the case "the owner reduced the second term after sending": check the "·2" mark on its own.
  3) THREE BUTTONS IN A REMINDER: "Renew" (build the request FROM THE REGISTER ROW — kind, object, term — and mark it with the kind "Renewal", keeping the old number), "Already renewed" (source "self": reminders stop, but this does NOT go into the sales metric) and "Do not remind" ("stop" in the "Reminded" field). Without the second and third buttons the bot turns into spam.
  4) THE METRIC — RENEWALS BEFORE THE DEADLINE, not the number of requests: of the rows that ended during the last month, count the share where "Renewed" is not later than "Valid until". When closing a renewal request, mark the OLD row as renewed — that is where the number comes from.
  5) DO NOT PROMISE A PRICE THAT YOU DO NOT CALCULATE (the compulsory insurance tariff is calculated by the insurer, comprehensive insurance — after an inspection). Show a guide "from N ₽" from the price list and say plainly who will name the exact one. But THE DEADLINE IS VISIBLE AT ONCE: fewer than "Urgent, days" until the end — tell the client and mark the request urgent for the managers.
  6) DOCUMENTS AS PHOTOS — ONLY TO THE REQUEST'S MANAGER. While the request is nobody's, do not ask at all: a passport must not go to everyone. Remember the recipient (ClientID|Messenger|BotID) in variables BEFORE the shots, send a "from whom" line BEFORE the files, and the files themselves — with the "Receive and forward media" action (it downloads and uploads anew, so a shot from MAX reaches Telegram). The action has one type, so for a shot and for a file — TWO actions in a row, and "#{media_sent} = 0" — its own "did not arrive" branch. Put the intake block RIGHT AFTER the request block and make TWO receivers passing the client to each other: forwarding works only on the message in which the file came. If there is a link to the consent to data processing, show it before sending.
  7) RECOGNISE THE SERVICE KIND BY THE PRICE LIST, BUT THROW OUT THE GENERAL WORDS of the names ("insurance", "policy"): otherwise "I need insurance" lands in the first kind found. Accept human dates — "until 15.11", "15.11.2026", "from October 1", "tomorrow", "in a month"; without a year take THE NEAREST FUTURE one (in December "until 15.11" is already next year). "until …" — the end of the current term, "from …" — when the new one is needed: these are different fields.
The ready bot "Insurance agent: a policy and a renewal" is built this way.

CONSUMPTION BY WEIGHT: THE REPEAT PURCHASE TERM FROM THE FACT, NOT FROM THE CALENDAR (pet food and pet supplies; the same — baby food by age, fertiliser by area, filters by water consumption, consumables by the number of people, fuel, lenses — everything that runs out at different speeds for different clients).
  1) CALCULATE THE TERM FROM THE CONSUMER: grams per day = the pet's weight × "Norm, g per kg" from the price list (the feeding table on the package), days per pack = the pack in grams / the daily consumption, rounding DOWN (better to remind a day earlier). For what does not depend on weight (litter, vitamins), the price list has the column "Lasts, days". Do not remind "once a month": for one client that is early, for another late.
  2) A CLIENT MAY HAVE SEVERAL CONSUMERS (a cat and a dog) — keep them as a "Pets" list with the client's contact; each has its own food, weight, term and own reminder mark. All buttons carry THE PET NUMBER, and every handler checks that the pet is one's own.
  3) PARSING ONE MESSAGE "кот Барсик 4 кг" (cat Barsik 4 kg): the kind — by words and breeds FROM THE START OF A WORD ("скотч-терьер" is not a cat), the weight — only with "kg" (in free text "Barsik 2" is not a weight; accept a bare number only when you asked for the weight yourself), reasonable weight limits, the name — the first word with a capital letter that is not a kind or a breed. Without a kind and without a weight the message is not a pet — "hello" must not become a cat. Complete what is missing by buttons.
  4) RECALCULATE THE TERM ON DELIVERY, NOT ON ORDERING: the courier pressed "Delivered" — "Runs out" = today + days per pack × THE PACKS OF THIS FOOD IN THE ORDER. Consumption learns from the fact: the interval since the previous delivery divided by the packs of THAT delivery (keep them in the card), average it with the previous estimate within reasonable limits. The order has no food for this pet — do not touch its term. A new weight or a new food recalculate the term FROM THE LAST DELIVERY, not from today.
  5) REMIND ONCE PER PACK: put the end date itself into "Reminded", edit the row by the previous value and before sending. A new pack — a new date, the right to a reminder returns by itself. Do not write about a pet whose order is already on the way. Buttons: "Order" (data with the note "by reminder" — it goes into the order for the metric), "Later" (a shift by N days from today if the term has already passed) and "Do not remind".
  6) THE METRIC — ORDERS BEFORE THE PACK RUNS OUT: when checking out, write the "Term" into the order — the pack's end date at that moment — and count the share of orders created not later than the term. Next to it: how many reminders worked, who is waiting for an order now and whose food ran out a week ago (the shop has probably lost them already).
  7) AN ORDER WITHOUT A MARK BREAKS THE TERM: the delivery window passed, but the order is not "Delivered" — the duty robot writes to the order's courier (no courier — to everyone) once. Do not give advice on feeding and health — only consumption by the shop's price list.
The ready bot "Pet food and supplies: consumption by weight and a repeat order" is built this way.

MEMORABLE DATES: A REPEAT PURCHASE FOR AN EVENT THAT HAPPENS EVERY YEAR (flowers and gifts; the same — birthday cakes, greetings to employees and clients, annual maintenance, renewing subscriptions on the anniversary, memorial dates in funeral services).
  1) KEEP THE DATE WITHOUT A YEAR ("15.03"), and calculate the nearest occurrence by code from today, TODAY INCLUSIVE, by the owner's time zone; February 29 in a non-leap year — the 28th. Discard the birth year from "15.03.1960". Take a month in words only right after a number and in the first such pair ("at 18, May 3" is May 3), check the day with a leap-year calendar (30.02 and 15.13 are not dates). Sort the list of dates by the number of days until the nearest occurrence; today's date is 0 days, not "no date".
  2) PARSE ONE MESSAGE "mum, birthday March 15" by code: TO WHOM — by word stems FROM THE START OF A WORD, short stems take an ending of no more than two letters ("мужу" — муж (husband), "мужчина" (man) — not), from several — the first by text ("подруга" (girlfriend) will not become "друг" (friend)), diminutive forms — by their own stems; THE OCCASION — "др" (b-day) only as a separate word; THE NAME — a word with a capital letter, not "to whom", not a function word and not a month after a number ("Марта" without a number is a name).
