Neither "to whom" nor a date — it is not a memorable date. Take THE BUDGET only with a marker ("up to 5000", "5 thousand", "5k", "₽", "rub") — "1990 year" is not money. Complete what is missing by buttons.
  3) REMIND ONCE PER OCCURRENCE: put the event date itself WITH THE YEAR into "Reminded", the day of the letter into "Reminded when"; edit the row by the previous value and BEFORE sending. Next year the event is new — the right to a letter returns by itself. An order has already been placed for the event ("Order for" matches) — keep silent. Buttons: "Choose a bouquet" (the data has the note "by reminder" — it goes into the order), "Not needed this time" (sets "Reminded" to the nearest occurrence) and "Do not remind".
  4) THE RECIPIENT IS ANOTHER PERSON: ask for the recipient's name, address and PHONE as text — the "Send number" button gives the customer's own number. The card — with a length limit (longer — shorten it and say so). "Anonymously" — an order flag, in large letters for the florist and the courier. After an order for a date, write the address, the phone, the first bouquet, the bouquet sum and "Order for" into the date: next year "like last time" comes first and within the same budget; one withdrawn from sale — say so and select similar ones by price. Do not check out an order without the main product (only a card or balloons).
  5) DELIVERY BY THE MOMENT: narrow windows on a specific day, the nearest — not earlier than in N hours, no more than M orders per window (cancelled ones do not count), the event day — as the first button if it is within "We accept orders for, days". Check the window once more on choosing and at checkout.
  6) BUTTONS PRESSED IN A ROW ("+" and "−") carry THE SCREEN STEP: an action is applied only if the step matches the current one, after applying the step grows — a fresh screen works again, while a stuck [Data1] does not. Buttons that are identical on repeat (a choice, a budget, "anonymously") do not need a step. Parsed text and jumped with "Launch block" into the button handler — set the flag "fresh parse", otherwise the previous button from [Data1] erases the parse. A block that the answer reaches directly, bypassing the parsing (a contact, a photo), recognises the answer by the waiting marker, not by the parse flag.
  7) A PHOTO BEFORE DELIVERY AND THE METRIC: the florist presses "Photo" — the client recipient is remembered BEFORE the shot, the shot is forwarded by the "Receive and forward media" action with a recipient, check whether it arrived, two receiver blocks pass the employee to each other; the first photo — with the line "your bouquet is ready" and moves the order to "Assembled" only from "In progress". The main number of the summary — REMINDERS THAT BECAME ORDERS: of the dates the bot reminded about 30 days before, how many have "Order for" matching "Reminded"; next to it — delivered not later than the end of the window and with a photo.
The ready bot "Flowers and gifts for dates: a reminder, a card and delivery by the moment" is built this way.

A TERM BY MILEAGE AND SELECTION BY A PERSON (car parts; the same — service by engine hours, filter replacement by litres, meter verification by readings, equipment maintenance by operating time).
  1) CALCULATE THE TERM BY THE COUNTER, NOT BY THE CALENDAR: keep two marks in the card — the value and the date. The average consumption per day = the value difference / the day difference, and it LEARNS: average the new estimate with the previous one, keep it within reasonable limits, and discard marks closer than three days and "the counter going backwards" — it is a typo, tell the client. The next service mark is a multiple of the interval and strictly ahead; the date comes from it and the average.
  2) REMIND FOR TWO REASONS AT ONCE: "less than N counter units remain" OR "less than N days remain by the average" — whichever comes first. The second reason is THE SEASON (the owner's dates in the settings). Put THE REASON ITSELF into the "Reminded" field ("mt-90000", "winter-2026"), not a date: a new mark — a new reason, and the right to write returns by itself. An order has already been placed for this reason ("Order for" matched) — keep silent. A new counter mark must REMOVE the mark of the previous reason, otherwise the client does not get the next reminder.
  3) THE WORK SCHEDULE — AS THE OWNER'S LIST: the work, the interval, an approximate price. For a mark show the works whose interval it is a multiple of, and name an approximate sum with the disclaimer "the manager will name the exact price". Do not advise what to replace yourself: this is the master's responsibility.
  4) PARSING A CAR FROM ONE MESSAGE: the make — by word stems FROM THE START OF A WORD, short makes ("ваз", "киа", "бмв") take no ending, otherwise "вазон" (flower pot) becomes a make; the model — the word after the make, a number can be a model ("VAZ 2114") if it is not a year and not a mileage; the VIN — 17 characters without I, O and Q; the mileage — only with "km" or with the word "mileage", accept a bare number ONLY on a direct question, otherwise the year of manufacture becomes the mileage. Neither a make nor a VIN — it is not a car.
  5) A PERSON SELECTS, THE BOT LEADS THE CONVERSATION. Where an exact selection requires supplier catalogs, do not try to guess: collect a request (the car, the VIN, the counter readings, what is needed, a photo) and give it to the manager, and return the answer to the client AS CHOICE BUTTONS. Parse the manager's answer from an ordinary line — "original 45022-SNA-A00 8400 3 days": the kind, the article, the price, the term. LOOK FOR THE PRICE as a separate number: digits inside an article ("GDB3175") do not count as a price. Do not show more than three options.
  6) A SELECTION WITHOUT AN ANSWER IS A LOST CLIENT: count the hours from creation, and if there is no answer for longer than the term from the settings, the duty robot writes to the selection's manager (no manager — to everyone), once, with an escalation mark. In the list of selections show how long the client has already been waiting, and put those who have been waiting longer on top.
  7) THE METRIC — REMINDERS THAT BECAME ORDERS, and THE ANSWER SPEED: of the cards the bot wrote to within 30 days, the share where "Order for" matched the reason, plus the average time from the request to the manager's answer. These two numbers show whether the bot works.
The ready bot "Car parts by VIN: selection by a manager and a maintenance reminder" is built this way.

A QUEUE TO A BAY AND STORING SOMEONE ELSE'S THING (tyre fitting; the same — a car wash, a service with lifts, a left-luggage office, a repair with handover by receipt, a rental from a warehouse).
  1) CALCULATE FREE TIME BY CAPACITY, NOT BY THE CALENDAR: the owner has so many bays (masters, lifts), a work takes so many minutes, the grid goes in steps. The time is free only if a free bay exists FOR THE WHOLE work, not for the first cell: a long work takes several cells in a row. Take the work time from the price list by class (passenger car, crossover, SUV): a large car has both a higher price and a longer time.
  2) RE-READ THE DAY BEFORE CREATING A BOOKING: while the client was choosing, someone else may have taken the slot. Taken — apologise and show what remains ON THE SAME DAY (you did not read the other days' busyness), plus a "another day" button. Book not earlier than N minutes from "now" — the client must have time to get there. Skip the owner's non-working days.
  3) SPREAD THE PEAK WITH EARLY BOOKING: before the seasonal rush give a discount from the settings and say so plainly — "book before October 15: cheaper and without a queue". Put the share of those who booked in advance into the metric: it is the only way to unload those two weeks when the queue stands in the street.
  4) SOMEONE ELSE'S THING IN A WAREHOUSE IS A CELL, A TERM AND A CODE. Keep the cells as a separate list (place, taken, whose thing) and take them BY AN EDIT OF THE ROW BY THE PREVIOUS VALUE ("Taken" is still "no" → set "yes"): two employees at once do not put two things into one cell. Create a cell that is not in the list yourself — an employee may put it where there is room. Count the storage term in months from the intake, a renewal — from the end of the previous term, and a renewal MUST remove the reminder mark, otherwise the bot does not write about the new term.
  5) HANDOVER — BY A CODE, NOT BY A NAME: the client gets a code in the bot (the thing's status "waiting for handover", the place is still taken), the employee enters the code and sees WHAT to hand over and FROM WHERE. Search only among those waiting for handover: an old code must not work. Handed over — the status "handed over", the place is cleared, the cell is freed by its own thing. The client changed their mind — "cancel the handover" returns the status and clears the code.
  6) CALL BY WHAT LIES IN THE WAREHOUSE: under a winter set — for winter, under a summer one — for summer. The season dates are set by the owner (they are adjusted by the first frosts), put THE REASON ITSELF into the "Reminded" field ("winter-2026", "term-14.04.2026"), and if they have already booked for this reason — keep silent. Of two reasons (the season and the end of the term) choose whichever comes first; an overdue term is more important than the season.
  7) CONFIRM THE DAY BEFORE, CLOSE AFTERWARDS: N hours before the booking one letter "is everything still on?" with the buttons "I will come", "move", "cancel" (set the mark BEFORE the letter and only an empty one). Someone who did not come for longer than "waiting for the late one" gets the status "did not come" — the bay is freed, and the employee gets the line "if they did come after all, correct it in the card". Change the status only from the previous one: "arrived" — from the booking, "ready" — from "at the bay".
  8) THE METRIC — REMINDERS THAT BECAME BOOKINGS, and BAY LOAD: of the things whose owners the bot wrote to within 30 days, the share of those who booked for the same reason; next to it — the average bay load for the week, no-shows and how many cells are free. These numbers show whether the bot pays off.
The ready bot "Tyre fitting and tyre storage: booking by free bays and handover by code" is built this way.

A QUEUE IN MINUTES, A STOP OF ORDERS AND THE GUEST'S HABIT (a take-away coffee shop; the same — a bakery, fast food, a barbershop with a flow, a pickup point, a pharmacy with preparation).
  1) CALCULATE THE QUEUE, DO NOT PROMISE "SOON": each menu item has its own preparation time in SECONDS, the place has a number of working hands. The nearest term = (the sum of seconds of the orders that are not ready yet / the number of hands) + its own time, but NOT EARLIER than the travel allowance from the settings. Offer several times with a step and show in how many minutes it will be.
  2) RE-READ THE QUEUE BEFORE CREATING AN ORDER: while the guest was choosing a time, three more people came to the counter. We no longer make it — name a new honest time with a button "I take it at …", rather than confirming the old one. Lying about minutes is worse than naming them plainly.
  3) A STOP OF ORDERS IS MORE HONEST THAN A QUEUE: more orders in progress than the limit — say so ("it is peak time now, 9 in progress"), and name the time anyway. Give the employee a "Pause for N minutes" button: while it is on, orders are not accepted, and the bot tells the guest when ordering opens. Outside the working hours do not accept an order, but say when you open.
  4) A REPEAT IS THE MAIN BUTTON: keep the guest's last order IN FULL (the item, the size, each add-on). "As usual" must lead straight to choosing the time: two screens instead of five. Parse an order in words ("latte 0.4 with oat milk") with the same menu: look for the item by the start of a word, the size — from this item's sizes, an add-on — by ITS OWN word, because "milk" and "syrup" are in half the list.
  5) A STAMP CARD COUNTS BY HANDOVER: every N-th is free, the counter is the guest's. Award a stamp ONLY when the order is handed over: a cancelled or uncollected one gives no stamp, and a free one resets the card. Draw the card with circles — "●●○○○", it is clearer than a number.
  6) THE BOT CALCULATES THE USUAL HOUR ITSELF: by the time of the handed-over orders, by a moving average, and only after N orders — otherwise a random visit becomes a "habit". In the morning, so many minutes before the usual hour, offer to repeat: once a day (THE DAY ITSELF goes into the mark), only to those who allowed it, and silently — to someone who has already ordered today.
  7) A READY ORDER DOES NOT WAIT FOREVER: after "waiting at the counter, minutes" the duty robot moves it to "not collected", writes to the employee and leaves the button "collected after all". Change the statuses only from the previous one: "preparing" — from accepted, "ready" — from "being prepared".
  8) THE METRIC — THE SHARE OF "AS USUAL" REPEATS and THE TIME FROM ORDER TO HANDOVER. The first shows whether the habit has taken root, the second — whether the bot lies about the minutes. Next to them: not collected, revenue and how many drinks were given by the stamp.
The ready bot "Take-away coffee shop: an order to the minute, a queue and a stamp card" is built this way.

A PRICE BY WEIGHT, THE DAY'S LOAD AND A PREPAYMENT THAT HOLDS THE DATE (cakes to order; the same — renovation with materials by volume, printing in runs, services with a daily output norm).
  1) CALCULATE BY THE VOLUME UNIT, NOT BY THE ITEM: the price = the price per kilogram (metre, sheet) × the volume + the option's surcharge per the same unit. Each item in the price list has TWO limits of its own: the minimum volume (the master does not take less) and the production time (they will not make it earlier). Accept the volume both as a number and by people: "for 15 people" is a weight by the norm per guest.
  2) THE DAY'S LOAD SETS THE POSSIBILITY: the master has a daily output limit. A live order takes the day with its volume, and a date can be taken only if the remainder is larger than the new order. Offer DATES WITH A REMAINDER ("9 kg free"), not just a calendar, and skip the owner's non-working days.
  3) CHECK A DATE FROM A MESSAGE, DO NOT JUST ACCEPT IT: it fits only if there is at least the production time until it, the day is a working one and the volume fits. It does not fit — say why and show the free ones. Before creating an order RE-READ the day: while the client was choosing, it may have been taken.
  4) THE PREPAYMENT HOLDS THE DATE, NOT A PROMISE: the order is created with the status "waiting for prepayment" and the field "holding until" (now + hours from the settings). Payment by link moves it into work BY ITSELF — the payment provider's callback changes the status only from "waiting for prepayment", otherwise a repeated call processes the order twice. No payment provider — do not abandon the client: say that the master will write how to make the prepayment, and leave the master a button to mark the payment manually.
  5) RELEASE AN UNPAID ORDER YOURSELF: the duty robot checks "holding until", removes the overdue ones (status "cancelled", the term field is cleared) and writes to the client plainly — the date has been freed, we will check out again. This costs the master most: a taken but unpaid day does not allow taking another order.
  6) A SKETCH — AFTER CHECKOUT, NOT INSTEAD OF IT: first an order with a number, then a "send a sketch" button, and the picture is forwarded to the master (forwarding downloads the file and gives it out anew — this is how a shot from MAX reaches Telegram). Put the mark "sketch exists" into the order.
  7) A REASON TO RETURN — THE ANNIVERSARY OF THE PREVIOUS ORDER: the same day and month a year later. Put THE REASON ITSELF into "Reminded" ("cake-15.10.2027"), and into "Order for" — the reason for which they ordered: matched — keep silent. Offer a repeat with the button "like last time", which leads STRAIGHT to choosing the date: the contents are already known.
  8) THE METRIC — REACHED THE PREPAYMENT: of the checked-out orders, the share of paid ones, next to it — how many fell through, kilograms, revenue and the average load of the week. These numbers show whether the bot holds the master's calendar.
The ready bot "Cakes and pastries to order: a price by weight, the day's load and a prepayment" is built this way.

TWO COUNTERS FOR ONE CLIENT AND A STOCK IN DAYS (lenses and optics; the same — consumables in pairs and sets: cartridges, filters, hearing aids, insoles).
  1) CALCULATE FOR EACH SIDE SEPARATELY, NOT FOR THE PERSON: the right and left eye (each cartridge, each filter) have their own parameters, their own pack and their own day when the stock runs out. Keep ONE ROW PER SIDE in a separate list, and call by the one that runs out first: a person does not need two mailings in a row, they need to know when to come.
  2) MEASURE THE STOCK IN DAYS, NOT IN PIECES, and calculate by the consumable's KIND: for a disposable one, one piece = one day OF USE, and the mode ("how many days a week I wear them") stretches the pack; a planned-replacement consumable lives its term FROM THE MOMENT OF OPENING — by the calendar, however much it is used. Mixing these two rules means lying twofold.
  3) THE TERM STARTS WITH THE HANDOVER, NOT WITH THE ORDER: while the order lies in the salon, the client has gained nothing. The status "handed over" creates the stock: the remainder plus what was bought, the end date from today. Edit the row BY THE PREVIOUS REMAINDER — the client may have marked a loss at the same time.
  4) GIVE A "LOST" BUTTON: a consumable tears and gets lost, and without this button the calculation lies, and then the reminder lies too. Minus a piece — and the term is recalculated from today.
  5) REPEAT THE PARAMETERS, DO NOT SELECT THEM. A prescription, an assignment, a serial number — they are issued by a specialist. The bot accepts a line from the form and checks only the FORM of the record: the sign, the step, the range of each field, mandatory pairs (a cylinder does not come without an axis). Name what is missing in words. No advice on the substance: what to wear and what to take is decided by a doctor, while the bot counts the days.
  6) PARSING BY THE SIDE MARKER: cut the form text by the markers (OD/OS, "right"/"left"), and inside a piece take the numbers BY THEIR SIGNS — BC, DIA, CYL, AX by the word, the power by the sign and only after the marked numbers have been cut out. An answer to a question about one side carries no marker — accept it by what you expect, and give a "Same" button for the second.
  7) BUILD AN ORDER BY SIDES: "one per eye", "two each", "only the right". Only one side is common: the second pack lies at home. The product is chosen BY A BUTTON BY ARTICLE: a search by a word is dead here, because "daily" stands at several items at once.
  8) "LATER" DOES NOT MOVE THE STOCK: postpone the letter (a separate field "silent until"), but do not touch the end date — it is physical. Put THE TERM ITSELF into the "Reminded" field ("stock-26.09.2026"): we write once per term, and after a new handover the term is different — and calling again is possible.
  9) THE METRIC — MADE IT BEFORE THE STOCK RAN OUT: the share of orders placed before the consumable ran out, next to it — how many were late and how many people have no stock right now. These are exactly the clients the neighbouring salon took.
The ready bot "Contact lenses and optics: a stock for each eye and a repeat prescription" is built this way.

A THING AT THE MASTER'S: A STATUS WITH A DATE, A PHOTO AT INTAKE AND MONEY IN TWO PARTS (a tailoring studio, shoe and appliance repair, furniture restoration — everything where a thing is handed over and waited for).
  1) A STATUS WITHOUT THE DATE OF THE CHANGE IS HALF AN ANSWER: write "in progress since September 23", not "in progress". The client must see whether this status is fresh or forgotten a month ago. Keep in the order THE STATUS DATE and THE HISTORY as a string ("accepted 22.09 · in progress 23.09"): it answers "and when did you do this?" without a call.
  2) PROMISE A TERM ONCE AND COMPARE WITH IT: the order has "ready by". When the thing becomes ready, record "on time" (yes or no) ONCE — after that this mark does not change, even if the order is returned to work. The workshop's main metric rests on it: DONE ON TIME.
  3) THE ORDER TERM IS THE LONGEST WORK, NOT THE SUM OF TERMS: the master does two works of two days in two days, they take the thing once. Adding up the terms means lying to the client upward and losing them.
  4) PHOTOGRAPH THE THING AT INTAKE: the shot goes into the order (a File ID does not expire) and is shown to the client by a button. This removes the dispute "was the stain there?" and protects both sides; in dry cleaning, appliance and shoe repair it is always needed.
  5) MONEY IN TWO PARTS: a prepayment at intake (a percentage from the settings), the rest at handover. Show both parts EVERYWHERE — in the card, in the ready letter, in the reminder — and make the handover button at once with the remaining sum: the master must not calculate in their head.
  6) A FITTING IS A STATUS WITH ITS OWN WINDOW: the day and the window are kept in the order, the client confirms by a button or moves it, taken windows are not offered to others, and before booking the day is re-read. If a repair does not need a fitting, turn it off with a setting, and the status path shortens by itself.
  7) A REDO IS A RETURN TO WORK, NOT A NEW ORDER: the redo counter grows, the term is recalculated by the same works, the client gets a new date. A separate redo order breaks both the history and the statistics.
  8) SHOW THE OVERDUE TO THE MASTER, NOT ONLY TO THE CLIENT: the duty robot collects the orders whose term has passed by a round and sends the list to the employees. Meanwhile the bot honestly writes to the client "the master is delayed" — silence here is worse than bad news.
  9) THE RECEIVER CREATES THE ORDER, AND THE CLIENT CONNECTS BY A NUMBER: the columns "Client ClientID", "Client Messenger", "Client BotID" are empty until the client writes the number from the receipt to the bot (the row's autofields belong to the receiver). A taken order cannot be intercepted — the number may have been overheard; show the status, but do not change the owner.
The ready bot "Tailoring and shoe repair: order status, fitting and a photo of the thing" is built this way.

A DELIVERY WINDOW: CONFIRMATION THE DAY BEFORE AND THE PRICE BEFORE THE TRIP (furniture and appliance delivery, waste removal, installation at an address — everything a vehicle with people goes to).
  1) "WE ARE GOING" AND "WE ARE EXPECTED" ARE DIFFERENT STATUSES: keep "handed over for delivery" and "window confirmed" separately and load the vehicle by the second. One status for both meanings deprives the owner of the answer to the main question of the day — who will not be at home tomorrow.
  2) ASK FOR CONFIRMATION THE DAY BEFORE, NOT IN THE MORNING: in the morning it is too late to move — the route is built, the place in the window is taken in vain. Put the mark "window-dd.mm.yyyy" INTO THE ORDER ITSELF and set it BEFORE the letter: two rounds in a row do not ask twice, and after a move the mark is erased — so it can be asked again.
  3) WHO DID NOT ANSWER — AS A SEPARATE SCREEN FOR AN EMPLOYEE, WITH PHONES: this list is read in the evening to call around, not in the morning to explain. The duty robot sends it itself.
  4) CALCULATE THE TRIP PRICE BEFORE THE TRIP AND SHOW IT AS LINES: delivery, lifting, assembly — each with its own sum. A single number "8 100 ₽" at the door looks like the movers' invention; a breakdown — like an invoice.
  5) LIFTING IS CALCULATED FROM THE FLOOR, THE LIFT AND THE NUMBER OF PIECES: the first floor and a lift to the door — without a surcharge, a lift — a fixed price per order, without a lift — per each floor above the first and per each piece. Ask for the floor with a separate question if it was not in the address: without the floor the sum cannot be calculated.
  6) PUT THE READY ESTIMATE INTO THE ORDER AS A STRING: the settings will change tomorrow, while the sum named to the client must not change. Recalculating the estimate from the settings retroactively means arguing with the client at the door in their own words.
  7) THE CONDITIONS SCREEN IS A COUNTER, NOT A FORM: the lift, assembly and the zone are toggled by buttons, the estimate is rebuilt in THE SAME message (the button setting "Replace the previous message"). An employee will not finish filling in five separate questions in a row.
  8) INTO ONE WINDOW — AS MANY ORDERS AS THE VEHICLE CAN CARRY: the limit is set by a setting, taken windows are not offered, and before booking the day is re-read — while the client was choosing, the window may have been taken. A promise "from 14 to 18" without a limit stops being a promise.
  9) "DID NOT FIND THEM IN" IS A COUNTER AND A RETURN TO CHOOSING A WINDOW, NOT THE END: the order is alive, the client gets a new window button. Do not count a move after "did not find them in" as a second penalty: that trip has already been counted.
 10) THE MAIN NUMBER OF THE SUMMARY — DELIVERED THE FIRST TIME (without moves and without "did not find them in"). A repeated trip costs a day of the vehicle, and it is rarely paid for; next to it show the share of confirmations — it tells whether the node itself works.
 11) WHAT WE CARRY — IN WORDS, THE NUMBER NEXT TO IT IS PIECES: "The Milan sofa and a sliding wardrobe, 4 pieces". Not the price and not the quantity of goods — exactly pieces: lifting and assembly are counted by them.
 12) THE MORNING REMINDER IS TURNED OFF BY A BUTTON, THE CONFIRMATION REQUEST IS NOT: and if there is a "do not remind" button, the round MUST read this list. A button nobody reads is a deception.
The ready bot "Furniture and appliance delivery: a delivery window, lifting and assembly" is built this way.

A LIVE QUEUE AND A SUBSCRIPTION FOR A THING (a car wash, tyre fitting without booking, a barbershop on a first-come basis, a pickup point — everything where people arrive and wait on the spot).
  1) "YOU ARE THIRD" IS NOT AN ANSWER: third behind three five-minute works and third behind three hour-long works are different times. Calculate the queue BY BAYS (masters, boxes): a bay is freed when the work standing on it is finished — and then lay out those waiting over the bays being freed. The length of the list without time is useless.
  2) THE WORK TIME IS A PRICE LIST FIELD, NOT A GUESS: each service has its own "minutes". The whole queue is calculated from them, so a service without a time breaks the calculation for everyone.
  3) THE PLACE IN THE QUEUE IS NOT STORED BUT CALCULATED ANEW on every screen. A number written into a row lies a minute later: someone ahead was finished, someone did not come.
  4) THE PROMISE IS WRITTEN ONCE AND NOT RECALCULATED: put "promised minutes" into the order at the moment of joining, and take the fact from the work start time. The metric PROMISED / CAME OUT rests on this pair — it shows whether the bot can be trusted. If the promise is recalculated retroactively, the metric always shows that you guessed right.
  5) ROUND MINUTES TO FIVE: "in 23 minutes" is a promise that cannot be kept. And name THE ARRIVAL HOUR ("come by 12:25"), not only the duration: the person needs to decide when to leave.
  6) CALL THEM TO COME IN ADVANCE, NOT WHEN THE BAY IS ALREADY EMPTY: so many minutes before as needed to get there (a setting). An empty bay waiting for a client is lost money.
  7) DO NOT GIVE TWO PLACES AT ONCE: a place is taken by a thing (a car), not by a person. And allow CANCELLATION only before the work starts: what is already being done is not removed from the queue.
  8) BIND A SUBSCRIPTION TO A THING, NOT TO A SUBSCRIBER: the key — a plate number, a bicycle number, a serial number. Then the charge works even when someone else brought the thing, and the employee does not have to look for anyone. Charge BY THE PREVIOUS REMAINDER and choose the subscription that EXPIRES EARLIER.
  9) AN EXPIRING SUBSCRIPTION IS THE BEST REASON TO WRITE: the money has already been paid, but the service has not been received. The duty robot warns N days before, the mark "expires-dd.mm.yyyy" lies in the subscription itself. In the summary show how many washes will soon expire: it is both a loss of trust and deferred revenue.
 10) BRING THE THING'S NUMBER TO ONE FORM: plate numbers are typed in Latin and Cyrillic, with spaces and dashes. Without normalisation the subscription "is not found" for its own owner. Also recognise such a number IN ANY message without a marker: it is sent first thing, without waiting for a question.
 11) A WORK BEING DONE TWICE AS LONG AS THE NORM IS A FORGOTTEN STATUS: show such rows to the employee. While the thing is listed at a bay, the queue is calculated wrongly for everyone else.
 12) THE DUTY ROBOT'S ROUND IS FREQUENT HERE (5 minutes, by a setting): the queue lives in minutes, not days. Be sure to move the period into the settings.
The ready bot "Car wash: a queue in minutes, "the car is ready" and a subscription" is built this way.

A LIST OF REMARKS: WHEN THE MAIN ENTITY IS NOT AN ORDER BUT AN ITEM (accepting a flat, a defect list, an object audit, an inspection checklist — everything where one object has DOZENS of items with their own fate).
  1) THE STATUS BELONGS TO THE ITEM, NOT TO THE OBJECT: each remark lives its own path and its own term. One status for the whole flat answers no question: is "under repair" two items or twenty?
  2) DO NOT STORE THE OBJECT'S STATUS — CALCULATE it from the items ("there are live ones" → under repair, "all closed" → ready for inspection, "the act is signed" → accepted). A separate field creates a second truth, and it diverges from the first on the very first day.
  3) THE TERM RUNS FROM THE DAY OF RECORDING AND DOES NOT MOVE ON A RETURN: otherwise the contractor gets a new term for their own unfinished work, and fixing things formally becomes profitable. Say so to both sides.
  4) GROUP THE ITEMS BY PLACE (room, unit, floor) and choose the place BY A BUTTON FROM A DIRECTORY: written in words, "kitchen", "Kitchen" and "in the kitchen" become three different places, and the breakdown falls apart. Make the list of places a separate list, so that the owner adjusts it.
  5) ONLY AN "AFTER" PHOTO CAN CLOSE AN ITEM: do not allow setting "fixed" without a shot, first ask for a photo and only then show the button. The word "done" costs nothing — the customer will come and check anyway.
  6) THE CUSTOMER CHECKS, NOT THE CONTRACTOR: "fixed" goes to them with a shot and two buttons — accept or return. A RETURN IS A PATH BACK ALONG THE SAME ITEM (a return counter), not a new row: otherwise both the history and the statistics fall apart.
  7) AN ITEM HAS TWO SHOTS — "AT INSPECTION" AND "AFTER": show the customer the one that matters now (after fixing — the second). Make ONE shot request screen, and what exactly we ask for is decided by the "waiting" marker: two almost identical screens diverge at the first edit.
  8) UNTIL THE CUSTOMER HAS CHECKED, THE WORK STANDS: the duty robot hurries them after N days, and the letter says so plainly. This is not pushiness, but an explanation of why nothing moves.
  9) SHOW THE OVERDUE TO THE CONTRACTOR, NOT TO THE CUSTOMER: the customer already understands that the term has passed, and can do nothing about it. The list of overdue items is a screen and a letter for the employees.
 10) THE NEXT STEP STARTS WITH CLOSING THE LAST ITEM: when no live ones remain, the bot ITSELF tells the employee "time to call for a repeat inspection" and gives a button. Waiting until someone notices it with their eyes means losing days.
 11) THE CUSTOMER SIGNS THE FINAL AND ONLY WITH AN EMPTY LIST: signing with open items is impossible — the list was created for them. After signing, new items are not added to it: this is already the warranty, and answer accordingly.
 12) THE MAIN NUMBER OF THE SUMMARY — DONE THE FIRST TIME (accepted without a second round). Next to it — RETURNS: they show where things are fixed formally, and "on time" — whether they make it at all.
The ready bot "Flat acceptance: remarks with photos, a fixing term and a repeat inspection" is built this way.

RENTING THINGS: NAMED UNITS, A TIMER AND A DEPOSIT (equipment rental, tool hire, car sharing in the yard, issuing equipment against a deposit).
  1) THE INVENTORY IS UNITS, NOT SERVICES: a list row = ONE thing with its own number, price and deposit. Two identical models — two rows. A reservation takes a SPECIFIC unit, otherwise on the spot it turns out that there are no free ones, although the bot promised.
  2) A RESERVATION HOLDS THE THING TOO: count a unit as taken both on reservation and on handover. If only the handover holds it, two people reserve the same thing.
  3) A RESERVATION MUST EXPIRE: they did not come within N minutes (a setting) — the duty robot removes the reservation ITSELF and frees the thing. The rental must not stand still because of someone who changed their mind and did not say so.
  4) HANDOVER BY A CODE, NOT BY A NAME: the client gets four digits on reservation, the employee enters them and sees what to hand over. There is a queue at the counter, and looking for a surname takes longer.
  5) THE TIMER IS CALCULATED NOW, NOT STORED: "40 minutes left" becomes 39 a minute later. And THE TERM RUNS FROM THE FACT OF HANDOVER, not from the reservation: the person may have come later.
  6) OVERDUE DRIPS IN MONEY AND IS VISIBLE TO BOTH SIDES: to the client in the card and by a letter, to the employee — as a list. Count it from the term, with GRACE MINUTES (getting there and returning also takes time) and as a percentage of the hourly rate for each STARTED hour.
  7) THE DEPOSIT IS REDUCED BY THE SURCHARGE — this is the point of a deposit, otherwise it just lies there. The bot calculates itself: how much to return, and if the surcharge is more than the deposit — how much to pay extra. The employee only hands over the money.
  8) WRITE THE PRICE AND THE DEPOSIT INTO THE RENTAL AT THE MOMENT OF RESERVATION: the price list changes tomorrow, but exactly as much as was taken must be returned.
  9) A DAY IS NOT MORE EXPENSIVE THAN THE SUM OF HOURS: if the hours added up to more than the daily rate, take the daily rate. Otherwise the rental itself punishes a long rental, which is profitable for it.
 10) A RENEWAL INSTEAD OF OVERDUE: if nobody is waiting for the unit, give an "extend" button — it is cheaper for the client and more profitable for the rental than a fine. If the next person has already reserved it, honestly refuse and ask to return it on time.
 11) A THING IS TAKEN OUT OF CIRCULATION: the state "under repair" removes a unit from handover. A thing IN SOMEONE'S HANDS cannot be sent for repair — first accept the return.
 12) THE MAIN NUMBER OF THE SUMMARY — HOURS IN RENTAL: a rental lives by how much time a thing works, not by how much of it was bought. And be sure to show WHAT WAS NEVER TAKEN: this is a direct answer to what is superfluous and what it is time to sell.
The ready bot "Rental: a reservation with a code, a rental timer and a deposit" is built this way.

SIGN-UP FOR A GROUP WITH A PREPAYMENT BY TRANSFER (hikes and tours, on-site master classes, camps, expeditions, any events where the number of places is finite and the money comes by transfer, not by link).
  1) A GROUP IS PLACES, AND A RESERVATION TAKES A PLACE TOO. If only a paid sign-up holds a place, one place is sold twice — and at the meeting point there is one person more than places.
  2) COUNT THE PLACES AT THE MOMENT OF DISPLAY, do not store them as a number: while a person reads the description, they are being taken. And DO NOT OFFER A GROUP WITHOUT PLACES: offering and refusing is worse than not offering.
  3) WRITE THE PRICE AND THE PREPAYMENT INTO THE SIGN-UP at the moment of signing up: the price list will change, but the person signed up at today's price.
  4) A RESERVATION MUST EXPIRE: the prepayment was not made within N hours (a setting) — the duty robot removes the reservation ITSELF and returns the place to the group. Before that REMIND about the payment: the person may simply have forgotten, and the place will go.
  5) A TRANSFER IS NOT A PAYMENT BY LINK. The bot shows the details (a phone number with the bank, a card, the recipient), the person transfers from their bank and NAMES THE SUM, and the organiser checks it against the statement and confirms it. A payment — a separate row with the status "waiting → accepted/rejected".
  6) UNTIL CONFIRMATION IT IS NOT MONEY BUT A PROMISE: move the place to "paid" only when the confirmed sum has covered the prepayment. And WHILE ONE TRANSFER WAITS FOR A CHECK, DO NOT ACCEPT A SECOND — otherwise the payments are doubled.
  7) CALCULATE THE MONEY FROM THE PAYMENTS, do not store it as a number in the sign-up: the number diverges from them at the first rejected transfer. "How much the client has paid in total" is taken from there too — the sum of their accepted payments.
  8) DO NOT CALL TO TRANSFER WITHOUT DETAILS: until the owner has entered them, send the person to the organiser and hold the place. A person who transferred "somewhere" means lost money and a scandal.
  9) DO NOT SILENTLY ACCEPT A SUM GREATER THAN THE REMAINDER: say how much really remains.
 10) RENTAL AT AN EVENT: an item WITH A SIZE — A SEPARATE ROW with its own stock (skis 170 and skis 180 are different rows), while tents and backpacks have no size at all. COUNT THE BUSYNESS FOR A SPECIFIC EVENT: the same thing is free in July and taken in February. Check the item's season against the event's season.
 11) ONE ITEM OF EACH KIND PER PERSON: one person does not need a second set, and another will not get one.
 12) THE OWNER'S MAIN NUMBER — THE FILL RATE OF THE GROUPS. An empty place is not "minus one person" but money that will never come: the event takes place on the same dates and with the same expenses.
 13) THE LIST OF GROUP PARTICIPANTS IS A DEPARTURE LIST: the name, the status, how much was paid of the total and what the person takes from the rental.
 14) THE MINI APP SHOWS THE LIST, THE BOT HOLDS THE PLACE: events are chosen by eye (dates, price, how many places remain), but the sign-up returns to the chat, where the accounting goes. The app gets the places at the moment of the request, it does not store them.
The ready bot "Hikes: sign-up for a group, a prepayment by transfer and equipment" is built this way.

SERVICE UNDER A CONTRACT: A SERIAL NUMBER, A TERM IN WORKING HOURS, A REPORT WITH A PHOTO (servicing coffee machines, cash registers, refrigerators, lifts, air conditioners for businesses; on-site repair under a contract with a reaction time).
  1) A REQUEST LIVES ON THE SERIAL NUMBER: the client writes the number from the nameplate, and the bot itself finds the model, the outlet, the phone and the contract in the equipment list. Do not ask for what is already recorded.
  2) BRING THE NUMBER TO ONE FORM: uppercase, without spaces, dashes and dots, and Cyrillic look-alikes (С, М, А, Е, Н, К, О, Р, Т, Х, В) — into Latin. "CM-24017", "cm 24017" and "СМ24017" in the Russian layout are one machine. The number is written without a button too — recognise it in a message.
  3) ONE LIVE REQUEST PER MACHINE: both shifts write about a broken cash register. Do not create a second request — show the first one with the status, the master and the term and let them add a note to the master. Before writing, re-read the requests once more: while the client was describing the breakdown, the other shift may have created a request.
  4) THE TERM RUNS IN THE CONTRACT'S WORKING HOURS: the mode as a string ("24/7", "mon-fri 9-18"), a request on Friday at 17:00 with a 4-hour reaction — until Monday 12:00. Name the HOUR to the client ("the master will be there no later than 16:00"), not "N working hours left": the hour stays true a minute later too.
  5) WRITE THE CONTRACT TERMS INTO THE REQUEST on creation — the reaction and fixing hours, the mode and the terms themselves. The contract will be re-signed, but the promise to the client has already been given; a repeat visit is counted by the same terms.
  6) NO CONTRACT OR IT HAS EXPIRED — THE VISIT IS PAID, and the client agrees to the price with a button BEFORE the request. The dispatcher sees an expired contract by a note: it is a reason to renew.
  7) THE REACTION IS TIMED BY THE STATUS "ON SITE", the fixing — by "DONE". So "on the way" and "on site" are different statuses, and the time of each mark is written into the request.
  8) "DONE" — ONLY WITH A REPORT AND A PHOTO: what was done and a shot of the result. Without them there is no closing button and the status is not set. The shot goes to the client together with the report by File ID (sendtofileid), and the client's photo of the breakdown — at once to the assigned master.
  9) ONLY THE REQUEST'S MASTER MOVES THE STATUS: the request was reassigned while the previous one was on the way — their "On site" moves nothing. Edit the assignment by the previous status AND the previous master: two dispatchers assign at the same time, and the second must not overwrite the first.
 10) WHETHER IT IS SOLVED IS DECIDED BY THE CLIENT: "everything works" closes the request, "not solved" within the return window returns it TO THE SAME master with a new term from now and a repeat counter, and the previous report and shot go into the history — the second visit is closed with new ones. Later than the window — it is a new breakdown.
 11) LATENESS — HONESTLY: the term has passed, the master is not there — one letter to the client ("the master is late, the dispatcher knows"), not silence. A forgotten "on the way" for longer than N hours is a reason to write to the master: the client sees that the master is still on the way.
 12) ACCUMULATE REMINDER MARKS IN ONE FIELD SEPARATED BY SPACES: "burning", "late" and "stuck" are different reasons, one must not erase another. Look for a mark as a whole, with a word boundary; a status change erases all of them.
 13) THE SERVICE'S MAIN NUMBER — SOLVED ON THE FIRST VISIT: the service pays for the second visit, and the client stands without the machine a second time. Next to it — reaction and fixing on time, and machines that break again (two requests within 30 days): a direct answer that it is time to replace them.
The ready bot "Equipment service: a request by serial number and a term under the contract" is built this way.

AN OUTAGE AT AN ADDRESS: A KNOWN OUTAGE ANSWERS BY ITSELF, A MAILING BY ZONE IN PORTIONS (a management company, an internet provider, power grids, a water utility, a heating network; "I have no water/electricity/internet").
  1) A RESIDENT WRITES THE ADDRESS ONCE, AND IT IS BROUGHT TO ONE FORM: "12 Lenina St., apt. 45", "Lenina 12-45", "Ensk, Lenina 12" — one house. Compare the words of the house's street (all of them must be in the resident's text — the city and extra words do not interfere) and the number with the building; remove the flat, the entrance, the floor and the phone from the house number. The street type (avenue, lane, square) separates namesakes. Two equally matching houses — ask by buttons; no such number — offer the houses of the same street. Write the address for the resident exactly as it is in the houses list.
  2) THE OUTAGE ZONE IS SECTIONS AND ADDRESSES. A house has sections separated by commas — different ones for different services: a transformer substation for electricity, a boiler house for heating and hot water, a communication node for the internet. A house is in the zone if its section, the house itself or its whole street is named in it. Show the resident houses, not "TS-14".
  3) RECOGNISE THE SERVICE BY THE BEGINNINGS OF WORDS from the services list: "no hot water" — hot (matched "горяч" and "вод"), not cold. A root — only from the start of a word: "экран" (screen) is not "кран" (tap). A tie — by buttons from the matched ones. A message without a button "no internet" is the question itself: straight to the check by address.
  4) A KNOWN OUTAGE ANSWERS BY ITSELF: the service matched, the house is in the zone, the outage is ongoing — the resident immediately gets the reason and the term, "no need to call, you are on the list". Write a request row with the status "outage" — this is a call that did not happen; one row per resident per outage. The main number of the summary — the share of such answers.
  5) THE TERM — HONESTLY: named — "we will fix it by 18:00" (an hour, not "N left"); passed — "the term has passed, the crew is still working, we will name a new one"; not named — "we are clarifying the term". Write a postponement with the previous term and a shift counter; the first named term is not a shift. Parse the term from the dispatcher's text ("by 18:00", "18.00", "tomorrow 12:00", "3 hours", "from 01.10 10:00 to 18:00"); something unclear — ask again, do not invent.
  6) A PLANNED SHUTDOWN IS AN OUTAGE WITH A START: it is visible to the house's residents in advance, it is "ongoing" half an hour before the start, before that it does not answer "no water".
  7) THE MAILING TO EVERYONE IN THE ZONE GOES IN PORTIONS THROUGH THE DUTY ROBOT. The engine sends to an array of recipients one by one with a pause, a thousand residents take minutes, so keep in the outage the field "Mailing" = "code~how many sent" ("new~0", "term~150", "fixed~all"). The duty robot takes the next K recipients, FIRST edits the field by the previous value (staked a claim), then sends. While there are still people to write to — the next round in a minute. Take the recipients in the order of the residents list rows — it does not float between portions.
  8) RECIPIENTS: the residents of the zone's houses who have not turned off notifications, and EVERYONE who complained about this outage, even with notifications turned off. A new event replaces the unsent one — so each event's letter is self-sufficient (what, where, the term). To residents — only in the "write from/to" hours, to employees — always.
  9) A MASS MAILING IS IRREVERSIBLE: create an outage with a screen "Check and send" with the number of houses and residents. The mailing is made by the duty robot — if there is no live one, whoever set it becomes it.
 10) THE LETTER ABOUT FIXING — WITH THE BUTTON "I STILL HAVE NONE": it is already one's own breakdown, the request is linked to the outage at once. "Not fixed" at the dispatcher returns the outage to work, and the residents get a letter again.
 11) "LOOKS LIKE AN OUTAGE": the N-th request for one service from one section within M minutes, and there is no outage — a letter to the dispatchers with the button "Create an outage" (the service and the section are already chosen). Exactly at the N-th and once per cluster: a house has several sections, and a neighbouring section that reached the threshold later has the same complaints. Answers by the bot ("outage") do not count.
 12) TEXT ON A SCREEN THAT REDRAWS ITSELF IS ACCEPTED BY A TWIN BLOCK without "replace the previous one" with the same scenario: otherwise the bot's answer lands above the dispatcher's message.
 13) THE ACCOUNT STARTS WITH WHERE THE STATUS LIES: the term has passed, the term is not named, the answer to a request is late, "looks like an outage", ongoing mailings, the duty robot is off. The duty robot tells the employees about this itself, with marks separated by spaces.
The ready bot "Outages at an address: a term without a call and notifying the section" is built this way.

NPS: ONE RATING 0–10, A REASON BY BUTTONS AND CALLING THE DETRACTORS (a satisfaction survey, "would you recommend us", an index by weeks).
  1) ONE QUESTION IN ONE TAP: the 0–10 scale is sent as READY buttons right in the letter, not "press to open the survey". A twenty-question form does not work: three people fill it out of politeness.
  2) THE RATING BUTTON CARRIES THE SURVEY NUMBER ("r~number~rating"). The question is asked by the duty robot, and by that time the subscriber stands on any other screen; from a rating alone it is unclear which question they are answering — especially after a reminder.
  3) THE GROUPS ARE CALCULATED BY SETTINGS ("promoter from", "detractor up to"), not hard-coded: different companies have different thresholds.
  4) THE INDEX IS THE SHARE OF PROMOTERS MINUS THE SHARE OF DETRACTORS AND ONLY OVER THOSE WHO ANSWERED, so ALWAYS show the response rate next to it ("4 of 5 answered — 80%"). Nobody answered — say so, do not show zero: from three answers the index means nothing.
  5) THE REASON — BY BUTTONS OF ITS OWN GROUP: to a detractor "what spoiled it", to a passive "what to add to make it a 10", to a promoter "what you liked". The reasons — in a list with the field "for whom". Allow ticking several (by a setting), skipping and adding words.
  6) A DETRACTOR GOES INTO THE CALL LIST AT ONCE, and the client is told an HONEST TERM ("we will call back by 16:00"), not "we passed it on". The operator takes the call with a button (an edit by the previous operator — the second gets "already taken"), marks the result; "does not pick up" is not a dead end: the bot itself writes to the client and waits for a convenient time.
  7) FIXED — ASK AGAIN after N days with the same question, keeping the previous survey's number in the new row. "Detractors won back" is the main figure of working with the dissatisfied; without asking again there is nowhere to take it from.
  8) A SURVEY GETS TIRESOME FASTER THAN IT SEEMS: ask one person no more often than the wave window (a quarter), remind ONCE, write only in working hours and keep "do not ask" as one button in the menu and under each question. An employee marks a visit — the bot asks after N hours, and if the guest was asked recently, it honestly warns.
  9) A WAVE: clients who have not been asked for a long time are called by the duty robot itself, a few per round — so the survey goes evenly, not over the whole base at once.
 10) A PROMOTER INVITES A FRIEND: a personal link "?start=p-CODE", the code is written into the client ONCE (on the first start), the invitation counter is edited by the previous value. In the summary — how many came and how many of them answered.
 11) IN THE SUMMARY: the week's index and against the previous one, the response rate, the groups as numbers, the top reasons FOR EACH group, detractors without a call as numbers and "detractors won back".
The ready bot "NPS and reasons: an index by weeks and calling the detractors" is built this way.

WIN-BACK "HAVEN'T SEEN YOU IN A WHILE": STEPS BY HOW LONG AGO, ITS OWN REASON AND OFFER, A RETURN BY A VISIT (a cafe, a salon, a barbershop, a car service, a shop — everywhere people come more than once).
  1) A STEP IS DATA, NOT CODE: the name, "days from", "days to", the reason and the offer lie as a list row. 30/60/90 is only an example: for a barbershop 45/90/150, for a car service 180/270/365. An empty "days to" means "and further".
  2) THE LETTER HAS EXACTLY WHAT IS WRITTEN IN THE STEP. Do not invent an offer: an empty field — only the reason goes out. The bot must not promise a discount that does not exist.
  3) ONE STEP — ONCE PER PERSON: accumulate the sent ones in the client separated by spaces and check before sending. Keep a pause between letters ("not more often than, days"), even if a person moved to the next step within a week.
  4) A VISIT RESETS THE STEPS (by a setting): came — the round starts anew. Otherwise a client who returned can never be called a second time.
  5) UNSUBSCRIBING — IN EVERY LETTER and in the menu. A mailing that cannot be left reads as spam already at the second letter. Write nothing to an unsubscribed person except answers to their own messages.
  6) DO NOT WRITE AT NIGHT: a "write from/to" window. A "haven't seen you in a while" letter at one in the morning turns off more than just the bot.
  7) WRITE THE STEP AND THE LETTER DATE BEFORE SENDING and by an edit BY THE PREVIOUS VALUE: otherwise two rounds in a row call a person twice, and someone who unsubscribed a minute ago gets a letter.
  8) CALL A FEW PEOPLE PER ROUND (by a setting), not the whole base at once: the messenger is calmer, and the mailing goes evenly.
  9) A RETURN IS COUNTED BY A VISIT: an employee marks a guest by a code or a phone, and if there was a letter N days before that, it is marked "returned". The duty robot also catches visits that were marked later. This is the only honest metric of such a mailing.
 10) COUNT THE RETURN SHARE FOR EACH STEP SEPARATELY: the owner needs to know not "how many were sent" but which step brings people back and which only irritates. Name the best step only when it has collected at least a few letters.
The ready bot "Win-back "haven't seen you in a while": steps by how long ago and returns" is built this way.

PERSONAL DATES AND GREETINGS: A GIFT WITH A CODE, A TERM AND REDEMPTION AT THE TILL (a coffee shop, a salon, a shop, a service — everywhere a client is remembered by name).
  1) KEEP A BIRTHDAY WITHOUT A YEAR ("dd.mm"). The age is irrelevant, and the year gets in the way of comparing "is it today". Accept "05.10.1990" too — just discard the year, do not argue with the person about the format. AN ANNIVERSARY — the other way round, from the FULL date of the first purchase: the greeting names how many years you have been together.
  2) THE FIRST PURCHASE IS SET BY AN EMPLOYEE and exactly ONCE: the anniversary is counted from it. The client does not edit it — otherwise the anniversary turns into a way to beg for a gift.
  3) AN OCCASION IS DATA: the name, where the date comes from, how many days before to warn, what we give, the code's term. The owner edits them as list rows. The greeting contains exactly what is written in the occasion: there is no gift — do not promise a gift.
  4) AN OCCASION FIRES ONCE A YEAR. Accumulate marks "code-year" in the client and TAKE THE YEAR OF THE UPCOMING DATE, not of today: on December 29 a greeting for January 2 must set the mark of the NEXT year, otherwise in three days the bot greets again.
  5) MARK FEBRUARY 29 ON THE 28TH IN A NON-LEAP YEAR: otherwise the occasion disappears for three years out of four.
  6) A GIFT IS A BEARER CODE WITH A TERM. Six characters of Latin letters and digits (it is dictated by voice), "valid until" in the row itself. Redeem it by an edit BY THE PREVIOUS STATUS: two cashiers with one code do not hand out the gift twice.
  7) CALCULATE THE STATUS BY THE DATE, not only by the field: the term may have expired a minute ago, and the duty robot has not reached this row yet. Show an expired code to the cashier as "expired", not "no such code" — these are different conversations with the guest.
  8) DO NOT ISSUE A SECOND GIFT FOR THE SAME OCCASION while the first is alive: one occasion — one code a year. Set the mark anyway, otherwise the round spins idle.
  9) THE CASHIER MUST SEE WHAT THEY HAND OUT: what we give, to whom and until what date — before pressing "Redeemed", not after.
 10) THE MAIN FIGURE — THE REDEMPTION SHARE, not the number of greetings: issued, redeemed, expired for each occasion. A greeting without a visit is worth nothing.
The ready bot "Personal dates: a greeting with a gift and redemption at the till" is built this way.

PAYMENT REMINDERS: PERIODICITY, SUMS, A PAUSE AND EDITING (a personal assistant: utilities, subscriptions, a loan, insurance, tax, rent).
  1) THE PERIOD IS PAYMENT DATA, not code: one-off, month, quarter, half a year, year, week lie in a list row. COUNT THE SHIFT FROM THE PAYMENT DATE, NOT FROM THE DAY OF THE PRESS: paid on the 3rd for the 1st — the next term is still the 1st, otherwise an annual payment creeps a week forward.
  2) KEEP THE PAYMENT DAY IN A SEPARATE FIELD: a payment on the 31st reminds on the 28th in February (on the 29th in a leap year), and on the 31st again in March. Without a separate field the day slides to the 28th forever.
  3) A PAUSE DOES NOT DELETE THE PAYMENT AND DOES NOT MOVE THE DATE, but when resuming, PULL the date up to the nearest future one: otherwise after a holiday the person gets a pack of overdues for all the missed months at once.
  4) REMIND THREE TIMES AND NO MORE OFTEN: in advance (each payment has its own term), on the payment day and once a day while the overdue is not closed. Write the reminder mark BEFORE the letter and by an edit BY THE PREVIOUS VALUE, the overdue mark — with a date ("late-dd.mm.yyyy"): otherwise two passes of the round in a row remind twice.
  5) KEEP SILENT BEFORE THE REMINDER HOUR, and wake the round EXACTLY BY THIS HOUR: if you sleep "with a larger step", the bot oversleeps the morning and writes towards the evening.
  6) "PAID" DOES TWO THINGS AT ONCE: it writes the payment into the history and moves the date to the next period. The move — by an edit BY THE PREVIOUS DATE: a second press in a row no longer finds the row, and the payment is not doubled. Close a one-off payment after it is paid.
  7) THE PAYMENT HISTORY STAYS AFTER THE PAYMENT IS DELETED: "how much I paid" must not change retroactively. Make deletion two-step — pressing "Delete" by accident is easier than it seems.
  8) EDIT ONE FIELD AT A TIME, not the whole form again: the sum, the date, the period, the reminder term — each with its own question. After an edit REMOVE the reminder marks, otherwise the new term passes silently. Send the fields that did not change with their previous values: the engine skips an empty assignment.
  9) THIS IS A PERSONAL BOT: set the round as a delayed launch FOR THE SUBSCRIBER THEMSELVES, the duty robot is not needed — nobody sees others' payments. The round lives while there are live payments, and goes out by itself when none remain.
 10) THE OWNER'S MAIN FIGURE — THE AVERAGE MONTHLY SPENDING by their own payments, not the number of reminders: people come into such a bot a second time for it.
The ready bot "Payment reminders: periodicity, sums and a pause" is built this way.

A BOT CAN CALL THE API OF ITS OWN CONSTRUCTOR. Besides cancelling a delayed
launch, /api/chain/start, /api/chain/stop, /api/block/start
and the other methods are called the same way: an ordinary "Data exchange" action, POST-json, the key
in the body. Put the account key into the SETTINGS LIST and read it by a variable — writing it in the
action itself is forbidden: the bot export would go out together with the key. Leave the settings
list EMPTY: the client enters their own key after
installation.
For booking reminders in the calendar this mechanism is NOT needed: a booking has
its own three reminders (see cal_record notify).

  addtagcheck: true, addtag: ["paid"]        set tags
  deltagcheck: true, deltag: ["new"]         remove tags
  addtag: ["Этап: #{stage}"]                 a tag from a variable: the engine substitutes it,
                                             a tag empty after substitution is not set
  tagtocheck: true,                          tags — to ANOTHER subscriber (a manager
  tagto: [{ clientid: "#{c_cid}",            moves a lead on the CRM board). Together
    messenger: "#{c_mid}", botid: "#{c_bid}" }]  with addtagcheck / deltagcheck

  sendto: true                               send a message to someone else
  sendtomes: "A new request from [FullName]"
  sendtobtn: [ ... ]
  sendtosubscribers: [ { botid: "#{botid}", messenger: "#{messenger}",
                         clientid: "#{clientid}" } ]

  ONE RECIPIENT RECORD SENDS TO EVERYONE AT ONCE IF IT HOLDS ARRAYS. Reading
  the list in a loop and sending one by one is not needed: the engine itself iterates over the recipients
  when botid, messenger and clientid were substituted AS ARRAYS OF THE SAME LENGTH
  (bcontrolup.js: an Array.isArray check of all three and a loop over them).
  And they become arrays by themselves when a list read found several rows.
  So a mailing to performers is built in two steps:
    1. a list read with a filter (subject, district, "Access until" not passed) —
       the fields ClientID, Messenger, BotID into three variables;
    2. sending to the bot with { clientid: "#{to_cid}", messenger: "#{to_mid}",
       botid: "#{to_bid}" } — it goes to each one found.
  A string with commas is also expanded into an array, so the list of recipients
  can be built with "Build a string" separated by ",".
  CAREFUL WITH ONE ROW: one found — the variables become scalars,
  and that is right, one message goes out. But if the lengths of the three variables
  DIVERGED (for example, someone has an empty BotID), the engine does not make the mailing
  at all — select rows so that all three fields are filled.

  sendtotag: "order"                         remember the numbers of the sent messages
  sendtodelete: true, sendtodeltag: "order"  delete the messages by this marker
  sendtodelexception: "[ClientID]"           for whom not to delete (separated by commas)

  A MAILING TO PERFORMERS WITH DELETING THE TAKEN ONE. An order went to ten drivers,
  one took it — the message with the "Accept" button must not hang for the other nine:
  they press, get "already taken" and stop trusting the bot. The engine
  can delete what was sent, in Telegram and in MAX:
    1. the mailing has sendtotag: "order" — the engine puts the message numbers
       into the variable _sendtomessid (an object: marker → recipients and numbers);
    2. in the NEXT step write them into the order row: listEdit of the field "Mailing"
       with the value "#{_sendtomessid}". ANOTHER subscriber will delete them —
       the performer who took it — they do not have this variable;
    3. in the "take the order" scenario, after a successful write, read "Mailing"
       and return the object with a JS action: varstore['_sendtomessid'] = JSON.parse(...)
       (a list read may also give a ready object — check typeof);
       the same action has done: { sendtodelete: true, sendtodeltag: "order",
       sendtodelexception: "[ClientID]" } — the taker keeps the message;
    4. the same without the exception — when the customer cancels the order.
  Put the "Accept" button right into the mailing: nextMessage — a block with [Actions],
  the order code in data ([Data1] in its scenario). One press instead of "open
  the card → accept". "Taxi Mega" and "Taxi Service AI" are built this way.
  Put done with deletion on a step WITHOUT branching: for a step with branches the main
  done is the "else" branch, it runs only if no branch matched.

  messengercheck: true                       a message to an account employee
  messengerall: true                         to all active employees
  messenger: [ { label: "Peter", value: 123 } ]  or named individually
  messengermes: "A new request from [FullName]"
  messengerbtn: [ ... ]                      buttons of the message to the employee
  messengermedia: [ ... ]                    attachments of the message to the employee

  finishcheck: true                           end the scenario of the chain step
  finishstep: "3"                             which step to launch, from 1; empty — the next one
  finishnow: true                             do not wait for the step interval
  finishtocheck: true                         end it not for oneself but for the named ones
  finishto: [ { botid: "#{botid}", messenger: "#{messenger}",
                clientid: "#{clientid}" } ]

  emailcheck: true, email: "...", emailmes: "..."     an email
  webhookcheck: true, webhook: "https://..."          call a webhook

A TRAINING CHAIN: A LESSON AND HOMEWORK.

A lesson step is THREE blocks in a row, not one:
  1) the lesson block: the lesson text, dontwaitanswer = true (a transition without waiting);
  2) the assignment block: the text [Actions], the "before sending" scenario gives the assignment,
     a 5-second delay — so that the assignment comes as a separate message;
  3) the work intake block: the text [Actions], the "before sending" scenario accepts
     a file or a screenshot and forwards it to the curator.
Make the transition from the second block to the third by a condition on a global variable:
if #{g_dz} = yes. Then homework checking is turned on and off
by one variable value, not by two different builds of the bot.

THE CURATOR IS STORED IN A LIST, NOT IN A BUTTON. Create a "Curator" list with the fields
Name, ClientID, Messenger, BotID. A separate scenario records there whoever
executed the command. Receiving and forwarding media is addressed by these fields. Do not carry
the student's or the curator's data inside button values — the link falls apart
as soon as a student has several works.

THE CURATOR'S VERDICT — a separate chain launched by a tag. Its first block parses
the curator's answer and goes to the needed block through "Exit → go to block"
(exittype startmes), not through buttons.

THE ORDER IN THE "DO" AREA. If one condition has "Exit — return
a message" and tags, the engine first sends the block and only then sets the tags.
Do not try to work around this with delays: a chain launched by a tag still
works after the block's message.

"END THE SCENARIO" EXISTS IN TWO PLACES — as a mark in the "Do" area
(finishcheck) and as an "Exit" option (exittype exitparent). They do one
and the same thing. Use only one: together they close the step twice and jump
over the next one.

ENDING A CHAIN STEP. While a step's scenario runs, the next step
is not launched — it waits. `finishcheck` closes the step, and the chain goes on.
This is not the same as `exit`: `exit` returns from the action scenario
to the dialog, while the chain step stays open.

With `finishto` the step is closed for OTHER subscribers — this is how a curator from their own
scenario sends the student the next lesson, while the student presses nothing.
The recipient format is the same as for `sendtosubscribers`. Without `finishto` the step
is closed for the subscriber who runs the scenario.

`finishstep` accepts a variable: "#{lesson_number}". A number outside the chain
does not break it — the next step in order is launched.

A notification to the request recipient is done through sendto with the variables
#{botid}, #{messenger}, #{clientid} — they are filled by the recipe "Auto-adding
the request recipient at launch".

SENDING TO AN EMPLOYEE.

Employees are the managers bound to the account on the "Employees" page.
The bot writes to them together with the answer to the subscriber: a request, a payment, a complaint,
an appointment. The setting exists in two places and is arranged the same way: at a block
(the fields messengercheck, messengerall, messenger, messengermes, messengerbtn,
messengermedia, messengerfileid) and in done of a scenario's "Do" area.

HOW TO CHOOSE THE RECIPIENTS.

1. The employees list is passed in the request and the bot description says who gets
   what ("requests — to Peter, reviews — to Anna") — set messengerall: false
   and list the needed ones in messenger as objects {label, value} FROM THE PASSED
   LIST, as they are. Inventing a value is forbidden: it is the subscriber identifier
   in the database, an invented one points nowhere.

2. In all other cases — messengerall: true and messenger: null. This
   is the main way: the engine finds the recipients while the bot works, so
   the bot keeps working after being moved to another account. The owner will later
   open the block and choose a specific one if they wish.

3. The employees list is empty — still messengerall: true. Employees
   will be added later, and the setting will start working by itself. Do not add the note
   "choose an employee" to name: there is nothing to choose.

WHAT TO WRITE TO AN EMPLOYEE. A message to an employee is a work card, not a copy
of the answer to the subscriber. Collect in it what the employee does not have at hand: the name
and a link to the subscriber, their answers, the sum, the time. Substitution templates
and variables work, including in buttons.

Useful buttons for an employee: "Take into work" with a tag (action), a link
to the conversation with the subscriber (link). A "Launch block" button (nextmessage)
works in the bot where the employee themselves is — use it only
if the employee is subscribed to the same bot.

AN EXAMPLE:

  done: {
    messengercheck: true,
    messengerall: true,
    messengermes: "A new request\n\nName: [AnswerID(0yj9iLem)]\nPhone: [AnswerID(t3Xdd2d0)]\nSubscriber: [FullName]",
    messengerbtn: [
      { id: 0, name: "Take into work", url: "", action: "action",
        addtag: ["took_request"], deltag: null, nextMessage: "", nextData: "", data: "" }
    ]
  }

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.8. A FULL EXAMPLE
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

await scenario("Request intake", async (qb, ctx) => {
  await qb.step("write", {
    name: "Write the request to the list",
    config: qb.cfg.listAdd(ctx.lists["Requests"], {
      "Name": "#{name}", "Phone": "#{phone}", "Comment": "#{question}"
    }),
    next: "sum_check"
  })

  await qb.step("sum_check", {
    name: "A big order?",
    config: qb.cfg.ops(),
    branches: [
      { cond: '#{sum} > 50000', next: "urgent_to_manager" }
    ],
    next: "ordinary_answer"
  })

  await qb.step("urgent_to_manager", {
    name: "Notify the manager",
    config: qb.cfg.ops(),
    done: {
      exit: true,
      exitmes: "The request is accepted, a personal manager will contact you.",
      sendto: true,
      sendtomes: "A BIG REQUEST from [FullName], sum #{sum}",
      sendtosubscribers: [ { botid: "#{botid}", messenger: "#{messenger}",
                             clientid: "#{clientid}" } ]
    }
  })

  await qb.step("ordinary_answer", {
    name: "Answer to the client",
    config: qb.cfg.ops(),
    done: { exit: true, exitmes: "Thank you! The request is accepted." }
  })
})

A step without work of its own is qb.cfg.ops() without operations: such a step does
nothing and serves only as a fork.

A BACKGROUND SCENARIO — without messages and without transitions, only logic. It is called
by a block and works silently:

await scenario("Visit tracking", async (qb, ctx) => {
  await qb.step("find_client", {
    name: "Does the client exist already?",
    config: qb.cfg.listFind(ctx.lists["Clients"],
      { pole: "ClientID", value: "[ClientID]", filtervar: "hasclient" }),
    branches: [ { cond: '#{hasclient} = 1', next: "count_visit" } ],
    next: "add_client"
  })

  await qb.step("add_client", {
    name: "A new client",
    config: qb.cfg.listAdd(ctx.lists["Clients"], {
      "Name": "[FullName]", "ClientID": "[ClientID]", "Visits": "1"
    })
  })

  await qb.step("count_visit", {
    name: "Plus a visit",
    config: qb.cfg.listEdit(ctx.lists["Clients"],
      { pole: "ClientID", value: "[ClientID]" },
      [ { pole: "Visits", op: "set", value: "[Self] + 1" } ])
  })
})

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.9. CHECK BEFORE OUTPUTTING external
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1. Not a single action identifier in the code. Only labels.
2. Not a single if/else/for/while for branching on subscriber data.
3. await stands before every scenario and every qb.step.
4. Every label in next and branches exists in this same scenario.
5. Labels inside a scenario do not repeat.
6. All ctx.lists / ctx.webapps / ctx.blocks / ctx.globals are named exactly
   as in the previously generated properties.
7. Every config is built by a qb.cfg.* builder — not a single object with action
   settings written out by hand.
8. Operation values (type of webapp, gcalendar, referal and the others) are taken from
   the lists of section 3.6, not invented.
9. Loops (a jump backward) end, there are fewer than 100 steps in a scenario.
10. The output is only code, without markdown and explanations.

==================================================================================
INSTRUCTIONS ON WEBAPP (moved from the constructor interface)
==================================================================================

COMMON TO ALL APP TYPES:
- An app is launched from a button of the "link" type under a message, from a keyboard
  button or from the bot's menu button (@BotFather -> Bot Settings -> Menu Button),
  or as a MiniApp (@BotFather -> Bot Settings -> Configure Mini App).
- The app link = https://api.quescha.com/webapp/[urlid], where urlid is the
  urlid field of the webapp[] object.
- For launching from buttons under a message and from a keyboard button, the bot token
  (settings.token) is OPTIONAL. For the MAX messenger the token is MANDATORY.
- After returning from the app to the bot, the current block's answer becomes a marker
  string. Set up its processing by a condition in the block (especially if the app
  is opened from the menu/keyboard button — then a condition is needed on every block
  where the subscriber may open it).

IN MAX A BOT HAS ONE APP. In Telegram the button goes out with the app address,
and two apps in one bot (for example, a calculator and a calendar) are opened
each by its own button. In MAX the engine makes an "open the app" button (open_app)
from a link button (action: "link") in TWO cases: the url holds the app address
https://api.quescha.com/webapp/… OR the bot's username (send.js). So put
one and the same link button with the app address for both messengers —
there is no need to substitute #{_botuser} into url, and no need to check the messenger for the button.
In MAX the app that the owner specified for the bot
on the MAX platform (business.max.ru) opens. Whatever app button is pressed — that one opens.
So in a bot for both messengers:
  • choose ONE app for MAX — the one that cannot be done without (a calendar
    with busyness and reminders cannot be replaced, while a form can be done with buttons);
  • lead the rest in MAX with buttons: split the menu by [Messenger] (setVar
    into a variable, the branch '#{messenger}' = 'max') and give MAX another path;
  • in the bot description tell the owner which app to specify for the bot in MAX
    and that the bot token is needed there in the app settings.

A PRICE FROM AN APP IS A HINT, NOT A FACT. A calculator that shows the price
on the fly works on the client's device: the number from it can be substituted. The app
sends the bot the INPUT (sizes, options, quantity), and the bot calculates the sum itself
in a scenario — by the price list, WITH THE SAME formula as in the app. Write this sum
to the client, the manager and into the request. The price list is given to the app by a scenario
on a webhook (the marker {{webhook:Name}}, the answer in _response), so the prices live
in one list, and editing the list changes both the calculator and the bot.

TYPE cat (Shop catalog):
- The catalog app lets you sell products in the bot.
- After the order is sent, the bot gets the answer webapp:catalog. Add processing
  with the condition '[Answer]' = 'webapp:catalog' and lead to the order processing block.
- In the processing block set up an action scenario with the WebApp/MiniApp action,
  operation type 'cat_order' ("Каталог: обработка заказа" — catalog: order processing) — it saves the contents
  and the sum of the order into variables. Then, for example, generate a payment link.
- The same action puts the order contents for writing off the stock into the variable ItemsID.
  If a write-off is needed, pass it to the payment link as an element
  payloads { name: "ItemsID", data: "#{ItemsID}" }.
- Products are webappdata[], categories are webapprubric[]. Create test products.

TYPE cal (Booking calendar):
- The calendar app lets you make bookings by a time schedule or
  reserve days/intervals.
- After returning from the app the bot gets the answer webapp:calendar. Process it
  with the condition '[Answer]' = 'webapp:calendar'.
- In the processing block first set up the WebApp/MiniApp action, operation type
  'cal_data' ("Календарь: данные из приложения" — calendar: data from the app) — it receives the booking data into
  variables. Then (in the same or another action scenario, usually after collecting
  all of the client's data) — the operation type 'cal_record' ("Календарь: сделать запись" — calendar: make a booking).
- The booking must get into the app, not only into a list: the app shows the busyness of dates
  and times to other clients ONLY by its own bookings.
  Keeping a list next to it is allowed, but it is an addition, not a replacement.
- Cancelling a booking — only with the 'cal_del_record' operation. It must be told EXPLICITLY
  what to delete, otherwise it silently does nothing and the time stays taken:
    recdelid — the booking id in the database. It was returned by recidvar of 'cal_record' at the moment
               of booking; save it in the list next to the request and substitute it here;
    delnum   — the ordinal number from "Calendar: get all the client's bookings"
               (this operation fills the list of bookings from which the client
               chooses what to cancel).
  A "cancelled" mark in a list is not enough: the list is your accounting, while the busyness
  of the time is calculated by the app only by its own bookings.
- FILL IN THE info FIELD OF 'cal_record'. It is visible in the app's account in the list
  of bookings and goes into the cancellation message. Without it the owner sees only the date
  and time and does not understand who is booked, with whom and for what. Put there the service,
  the performer, the client's name and the request number.

TYPE own (Open a website inside Telegram):
- The app opens any website inside Telegram (settings.url).
- url can hold a variable with a link, for example a generated payment
  link #{paylink}. url must not contain 'quescha.com/webapp'.

  A PAYMENT WINDOW INSIDE TELEGRAM IS BUILT ONLY THIS WAY. A link-type button
  with the payment link itself takes the subscriber to a browser — they leave the bot.
  A link to the app opens the payment AS A WINDOW OVER the chat:
    1) the "Payments" action, operation getlink, vlink: "paylink" — the link lands
       in a conversation variable;
    2) an app webapp[] of type "own", settings.url = "#{paylink}";
    3) in the message a link-type button with the address
       https://api.quescha.com/webapp/[urlid] of THIS app.
  The subscriber presses the button, the app substitutes the variable's value
  and redirects to the payment — all inside the messenger.
  In a saved app the address may look like "http://#{paylink}":
  the constructor adds http:// if the field does not have it. This does not interfere —
  the prefix is discarded together with the rest of the address, both forms are equivalent.

  THREE CONDITIONS WITHOUT WHICH THIS SILENTLY DOES NOT WORK:
  • THE VARIABLE REPLACES THE WHOLE ADDRESS, it is not inserted into it. The app takes
    THE FIRST #{variable} and goes to its value; everything else written
    in url is discarded. So "https://site.ru/pay?id=#{number}" leads
    to a bare order number. If you need an address from pieces, build it whole
    into a variable in advance and put only that variable into url.
  • IT IS A CONVERSATION VARIABLE, not a global variable and not a list field.
    The app reads it from the variables of the chain the subscriber stands in.
